### getBankZone()

```js
function getBankZone(zoneId) {
  return BANK_ZONES.find((z) => z.id === zoneId) || null;
}
```

### getUnlockedBankZones()

```js
function getUnlockedBankZones(business) {
  const staffCount = typeof business.staff === 'number' ? business.staff : 0;
  return BANK_ZONES.filter((z) => staffCount >= (z.staffUnlock || 0));
}
```

### getOwnedBranches()

```js
function getOwnedBranches(business) {
  return business.branches || [];
}
```

### getBranchByZone()

```js
function getBranchByZone(business, zoneId) {
  return (business.branches || []).find((b) => b.zoneId === zoneId) || null;
}
```

- **STAFF_HIRE_COST**: 125000000

- **STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY**: 2500

- **STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS**: 3000

- **STAFF_HIRE_MAX_MS**: 2700000

- **MAX_HIRING_AT_ONCE**: 3

### getStaffSalaryHourly()

```js
function getStaffSalaryHourly(business) {
  const members = Array.isArray(business.staffMembers) ? business.staffMembers : [];
  return members.reduce((total, m) => total + (m.salary || STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY), 0);
}
```

### makeStaffMember()

```js
function makeStaffMember() {
  const person = generatePerson();
  return {
    id: `staff_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    name: person.name,
    gender: person.gender,
    age: person.age,
    skill: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) + 3,
    salary: STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY,
    hiredAt: Date.now(),
  };
}
```

### getStaffMembers()

```js
function getStaffMembers(business) {
  return business.staffMembers || [];
}
```

### fireStaff()

```js
function fireStaff(business, staffId) {
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: Math.max(0, (typeof business.staff === 'number' ? business.staff : 0) - 1),
    staffMembers: (Array.isArray(business.staffMembers) ? business.staffMembers : []).filter((s) => s.id !== staffId),
  };
}
```

### raiseStaff()

```js
function raiseStaff(business, staffId) {
  const members = (Array.isArray(business.staffMembers) ? business.staffMembers : []).map((s) => {
    if (s.id === staffId) return { ...s, salary: Math.round((s.salary || STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY) * 1.15) };
    return s;
  });
  return { ...business, staffMembers: members };
}
```

### trainStaff()

```js
function trainStaff(business, staffId) {
  const members = (Array.isArray(business.staffMembers) ? business.staffMembers : []).map((s) => {
    if (s.id === staffId) return { ...s, skill: Math.min(10, (s.skill || 5) + 1) };
    return s;
  });
  return { ...business, staffMembers: members };
}
```

### hireStaff()

```js
function hireStaff(business) {
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  if (hiring.length >= MAX_HIRING_AT_ONCE) return { error: 'max_hiring' };
  const now = Date.now();
  const duration = STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (STAFF_HIRE_MAX_MS - STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS);
  const member = makeStaffMember();
  return {
    ...business,
    hiringStaff: [...hiring, {
      id: `hire_${now}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
      instanceId: `hire_${now}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
      hiredAt: now,
      completesAt: now + duration,
      staffMember: member,
    }],
  };
}
```

### completeHiredStaff()

```js
function completeHiredStaff(business) {
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const completed = hiring.filter((h) => h.completesAt <= Date.now());
  if (completed.length === 0) return business;
  let staffMembers = Array.isArray(business.staffMembers) ? business.staffMembers : [];
  if (staffMembers.length === 0 && (typeof business.staff === 'number' ? business.staff : 0) > 0) {
    staffMembers = Array.from({ length: business.staff }, () => makeStaffMember());
  }
  const newMembers = completed.map((h) => h.staffMember || makeStaffMember());
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (typeof business.staff === 'number' ? business.staff : 0) + completed.length,
    staffMembers: [...staffMembers, ...newMembers],
    hiringStaff: hiring.filter((h) => h.completesAt > Date.now()),
  };
}
```

### instantHireStaff()

```js
function instantHireStaff(business, hireId) {
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const hire = hiring.find((h) => h.instanceId === hireId || h.id === hireId);
  if (!hire) return business;
  let staffMembers = Array.isArray(business.staffMembers) ? business.staffMembers : [];
  if (staffMembers.length === 0 && (typeof business.staff === 'number' ? business.staff : 0) > 0) {
    staffMembers = Array.from({ length: business.staff }, () => makeStaffMember());
  }
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (typeof business.staff === 'number' ? business.staff : 0) + 1,
    staffMembers: [...staffMembers, hire.staffMember || makeStaffMember()],
    hiringStaff: hiring.filter((h) => h.instanceId !== hireId && h.id !== hireId),
  };
}
```

### VAULT_UPGRADE_NAMES

- Reinforced Steel, Time-Lock System, Biometric Scanner, Alarm Upgrade, Secondary Vault, Gold Reserve Room, Safe Deposit Boxes, Armoured Transport, Underground Bunker, Fort Knox Protocol

### getVaultUpgradeCost()

```js
function getVaultUpgradeCost(branch, vaultLevel) {
  const zone = getBankZone(branch.zoneId);
  const base = 75000000 * (zone?.wealth || 1);
  return Math.floor(base * Math.pow(1.5, vaultLevel));
}
```

### getVaultCapacity()

```js
function getVaultCapacity(branch, business) {
  const zone = getBankZone(branch.zoneId);
  if (!zone) return 0;
  const vaultLevel = branch.vaultLevel || 1;
  const levelBonus = Math.floor((business.level || 1) / 5) * 0.1;
  const staffBonus = Math.floor((typeof business.staff === 'number' ? business.staff : 0) / 5) * 0.05;
  const baseCap = 42000000 * zone.wealth * vaultLevel;
  return Math.floor(baseCap * (1 + levelBonus + staffBonus));
}
```

- **MIN_DEPOSIT_RATE**: 1

- **MAX_DEPOSIT_RATE**: 10

- **MIN_CREDIT_RATE**: 5

- **MAX_CREDIT_RATE**: 25

- **DEFAULT_DEPOSIT_RATE**: 3

- **DEFAULT_CREDIT_RATE**: 12

### setDepositRate()

```js
function setDepositRate(business, zoneId, rate) {
  const clamped = Math.max(MIN_DEPOSIT_RATE, Math.min(MAX_DEPOSIT_RATE, rate));
  const branches = (business.branches || []).map((b) =>
    b.zoneId === zoneId ? { ...b, depositRate: clamped } : b
  );
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### setCreditRate()

```js
function setCreditRate(business, zoneId, rate) {
  const clamped = Math.max(MIN_CREDIT_RATE, Math.min(MAX_CREDIT_RATE, rate));
  const branches = (business.branches || []).map((b) =>
    b.zoneId === zoneId ? { ...b, creditRate: clamped } : b
  );
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### getDepositorAttractiveness()

```js
function getDepositorAttractiveness(depositRate) {
  // 1% = 0.3x, 10% = 1.5x
  return 0.3 + (depositRate - 1) * (1.2 / 9);
}
```

### getBorrowerAppetite()

```js
function getBorrowerAppetite(creditRate) {
  // 5% = 1.5x, 25% = 0.3x
  return 1.5 - (creditRate - 5) * (1.2 / 20);
}
```

### getStaffEfficiency()

```js
function getStaffEfficiency(business) {
  const staff = typeof business.staff === 'number' ? business.staff : 0;
  if (staff === 0) return 0;
  return staff * 0.05;
}
```

### getBranchDepositors()

```js
function getBranchDepositors(branch, business) {
  const zone = getBankZone(branch.zoneId);
  if (!zone) return 0;
  const depositRate = branch.depositRate || DEFAULT_DEPOSIT_RATE;
  const attract = getDepositorAttractiveness(depositRate);
  const staffEff = getStaffEfficiency(business);
  const levelMult = Math.pow(1.12, Math.max(1, business.level || 1) - 1);
  const base = zone.population * 0.15 * attract * staffEff * levelMult;
  return Math.floor(base);
}
```

### getBranchTargetDeposits()

```js
function getBranchTargetDeposits(branch, business) {
  const zone = getBankZone(branch.zoneId);
  if (!zone) return 0;
  const depositors = getBranchDepositors(branch, business);
  const avgDeposit = 920000 * zone.wealth;
  const totalDeposits = depositors * avgDeposit;
  const capacity = getVaultCapacity(branch, business);
  return Math.min(totalDeposits, capacity);
}
```

### getBranchDeposits()

```js
function getBranchDeposits(branch, business) {
  return branch.currentDeposits || 0;
}
```

### getBranchLoanRevenue()

```js
function getBranchLoanRevenue(branch, business) {
  const deposits = getBranchDeposits(branch, business);
  const creditRate = branch.creditRate || DEFAULT_CREDIT_RATE;
  const borrowerAppetite = getBorrowerAppetite(creditRate);
  // Lend out deposits at credit rate, scaled by borrower appetite
  const loanedOut = deposits * borrowerAppetite;
  return Math.floor(loanedOut * creditRate / 100);
}
```

### getBranchDepositCost()

```js
function getBranchDepositCost(branch, business) {
  const deposits = getBranchDeposits(branch, business);
  const depositRate = branch.depositRate || DEFAULT_DEPOSIT_RATE;
  return Math.floor(deposits * depositRate / 100);
}
```

### getBankLevelMultiplier()

```js
function getBankLevelMultiplier(business) {
  return Math.pow(1.12, Math.max(1, business.level || 1) - 1);
}
```

### getBranchNetIncome()

```js
function getBranchNetIncome(branch, business) {
  const revenue = getBranchLoanRevenue(branch, business);
  const cost = getBranchDepositCost(branch, business);
  return (revenue - cost) * getBankLevelMultiplier(business);
}
```

### getBankHourlyIncome()

```js
function getBankHourlyIncome(business) {
  const branches = business.branches || [];
  if (branches.length === 0) {
    return -getStaffSalaryHourly(business);
  }
  let total = 0;
  for (const b of branches) {
    if (!isBranchOpen(b)) continue;
    total += getBranchNetIncome(b, business);
  }
  const salary = getStaffSalaryHourly(business);
  return Math.floor(total - salary);
}
```

- **BRANCH_OPEN_MS_MIN**: 3000

- **BRANCH_OPEN_MS_MAX**: 2700000

### getOpeningTimeRemaining()

```js
function getOpeningTimeRemaining(branch) {
  if (!branch || !branch.openingAt) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, branch.openingAt - Date.now());
}
```

### isBranchOpen()

```js
function isBranchOpen(branch) {
  if (!branch) return false;
  return !branch.openingAt || branch.openingAt <= Date.now();
}
```

### getOpeningBranches()

```js
function getOpeningBranches(business) {
  return (business.branches || []).filter((b) => !isBranchOpen(b));
}
```

### completeOpeningBranches()

```js
function completeOpeningBranches(business) {
  return {
    ...business,
    branches: (business.branches || []).map((b) => ({
      ...b,
      openingAt: b.openingAt && b.openingAt <= Date.now() ? null : b.openingAt,
    })),
  };
}
```

### openBranch()

```js
function openBranch(business, zoneId) {
  const zone = getBankZone(zoneId);
  if (!zone) return { error: 'invalid_zone' };
  const staffCount = typeof business.staff === 'number' ? business.staff : 0;
  if (staffCount < (zone.staffUnlock || 0)) return { error: 'staff_locked' };
  if (getBranchByZone(business, zoneId)) return { error: 'already_exists' };
  const openCost = zone.openCost;
  const branch = {
    zoneId,
    name: `${zone.name} Branch`,
    vaultLevel: 1,
    depositRate: DEFAULT_DEPOSIT_RATE,
    creditRate: DEFAULT_CREDIT_RATE,
    depositors: 0,
    currentDeposits: 0,
    fillMinutes: 360 + Math.random() * 120,
    openingAt: Date.now() + BRANCH_OPEN_MS_MIN + Math.random() * (BRANCH_OPEN_MS_MAX - BRANCH_OPEN_MS_MIN),
    openedAt: null,
    transactionHistory: [],
  };
  return {
    ...business,
    branches: [...(business.branches || []), branch],
    capitalisation: (business.capitalisation || 0) + openCost,
    cost: openCost,
  };
}
```

### instantOpenBranch()

```js
function instantOpenBranch(business, zoneId) {
  const zone = getBankZone(zoneId);
  if (!zone) return { error: 'invalid_zone' };
  const branches = (business.branches || []).map((b) => {
    if (b.zoneId === zoneId && b.openingAt) {
      return { ...b, openingAt: null, openedAt: Date.now() };
    }
    return b;
  });
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### getVaultUpgradeDurationMs()

```js
function getVaultUpgradeDurationMs(vaultLevel) {
  const instantChance = vaultLevel < 10 ? 0.05 : 0.01;
  if (Math.random() < instantChance) return 3000;
  const minMs = 3000;
  const maxMs = 45 * 60 * 1000;
  return minMs + Math.random() * (maxMs - minMs);
}
```

### getVaultUpgradeTimeRemaining()

```js
function getVaultUpgradeTimeRemaining(branch) {
  if (!branch || !branch.vaultUpgradeCompletesAt) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, branch.vaultUpgradeCompletesAt - Date.now());
}
```

### isVaultUpgrading()

```js
function isVaultUpgrading(branch) {
  return branch && branch.vaultUpgradeCompletesAt && branch.vaultUpgradeCompletesAt > Date.now();
}
```

### completeVaultUpgrades()

```js
function completeVaultUpgrades(business) {
  const branches = (business.branches || []).map((b) => {
    if (b.vaultUpgradeCompletesAt && b.vaultUpgradeCompletesAt <= Date.now()) {
      return { ...b, vaultLevel: (b.vaultLevel || 1) + 1, vaultUpgradeCompletesAt: null };
    }
    return b;
  });
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### instantCompleteVaultUpgrade()

```js
function instantCompleteVaultUpgrade(business, zoneId) {
  const branches = (business.branches || []).map((b) => {
    if (b.zoneId === zoneId && b.vaultUpgradeCompletesAt) {
      return { ...b, vaultLevel: (b.vaultLevel || 1) + 1, vaultUpgradeCompletesAt: null };
    }
    return b;
  });
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### upgradeVault()

```js
function upgradeVault(business, zoneId) {
  let cost = 0;
  const branches = (business.branches || []).map((b) => {
    if (b.zoneId !== zoneId) return b;
    if ((b.vaultLevel || 1) >= VAULT_MAX_LEVEL) return b;
    if (b.vaultUpgradeCompletesAt && b.vaultUpgradeCompletesAt > Date.now()) return b;
    cost = getVaultUpgradeCost(b, b.vaultLevel || 1);
    const duration = getVaultUpgradeDurationMs(b.vaultLevel || 1);
    return { ...b, vaultUpgradeCompletesAt: Date.now() + duration };
  });
  return { ...business, branches, cost };
}
```

### processBankBusiness()

```js
function processBankBusiness(business, minutesElapsed) {
  const branches = business.branches || [];
  if (branches.length === 0) {
    const salary = getStaffSalaryHourly(business);
    return { updatedBusiness: business, income: -salary * (minutesElapsed / 60) };
  }

  const now = Date.now();
  const suspended = isSuspended(business);
  let totalRevenue = 0;
  let totalDepositCost = 0;
  let newTransactions = 0;

  const updatedBranches = branches.map((b) => {
    if (!isBranchOpen(b)) return b;

    const depositors = getBranchDepositors(b, business);
    const targetDeposits = getBranchTargetDeposits(b, business);
    const fillMinutes = b.fillMinutes || 420;
    const prevDeposits = b.currentDeposits || 0;
    const fillRate = targetDeposits / fillMinutes;
    let currentDeposits;
    if (targetDeposits >= prevDeposits) {
      currentDeposits = Math.min(targetDeposits, prevDeposits + fillRate * minutesElapsed);
    } else {
      currentDeposits = Math.max(targetDeposits, prevDeposits - fillRate * minutesElapsed);
    }
    const deposits = Math.floor(currentDeposits);

    const branchWithDeposits = { ...b, currentDeposits, depositors };
    const revenue = getBranchLoanRevenue(branchWithDeposits, business);
    const depositCost = getBranchDepositCost(branchWithDeposits, business);

    const hourlyRevenue = suspended ? 0 : revenue * (minutesElapsed / 60);
    const hourlyDepositCost = suspended ? 0 : depositCost * (minutesElapsed / 60);

    totalRevenue += hourlyRevenue;
    totalDepositCost += hourlyDepositCost;
    newTransactions++;

    const txHistory = (b.transactionHistory || []).slice(-20);
    if (txHistory.length < 20 || now - (txHistory[txHistory.length - 1]?.timestamp || 0) > 60000) {
      txHistory.push({
        timestamp: now,
        depositors,
        deposits,
        revenue: Math.floor(hourlyRevenue),
        depositCost: Math.floor(hourlyDepositCost),
        net: Math.floor(hourlyRevenue - hourlyDepositCost),
      });
    }

    return {
      ...b,
      currentDeposits,
      depositors,
      openedAt: b.openedAt || now,
      transactionHistory: txHistory,
    };
  });

  const salary = getStaffSalaryHourly(business);
  const netIncome = totalRevenue - totalDepositCost - salary * (minutesElapsed / 60);
  const updatedBusiness = {
    ...business,
    branches: updatedBranches,
    allTimeEarnings: (business.allTimeEarnings || 0) + netIncome,
    completedTransactions: (business.completedTransactions || 0) + newTransactions,
  };
  return { updatedBusiness, income: netIncome };
}
```

### getAllTransactions()

```js
function getAllTransactions(business) {
  const branches = business.branches || [];
  const all = [];
  for (const b of branches) {
    for (const tx of (b.transactionHistory || [])) {
      all.push({ ...tx, zoneId: b.zoneId, zoneName: getBankZone(b.zoneId)?.name || '' });
    }
  }
  return all.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp).slice(0, 50);
}
```

### getPendingForZone()

```js
function getPendingForZone(business, zoneId) {
  return (business.pendingDeliveries || []).filter((d) => d.zoneId === zoneId);
}
```

### getDistrictCentroid()

```js
function getDistrictCentroid(districtId) {
  const district = CITY_MAP.districts.find((d) => d.id === districtId);
  if (!district || !district.polygon) return { x: 600, y: 450 };
  const points = district.polygon.split(' ').map((p) => {
    const [x, y] = p.split(',').map(Number);
    return { x, y };
  });
  const cx = points.reduce((s, p) => s + p.x, 0) / points.length;
  const cy = points.reduce((s, p) => s + p.y, 0) / points.length;
  return { x: cx, y: cy };
}
```

### migrateBank()

```js
function migrateBank(business) {
  const migrated = { ...business };
  if (typeof migrated.staff !== 'number') {
    migrated.staff = 0;
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(migrated.staffMembers)) {
    migrated.staffMembers = [];
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(migrated.hiringStaff)) {
    migrated.hiringStaff = [];
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(migrated.branches)) {
    migrated.branches = [];
  }
  return migrated;
}
```

### getDistrictById()

### BUS_TYPE_TIER

- **minibus**: 0
- **citybus**: 1
- **doubledecker**: 2
- **coach**: 3
- **electricbus**: 4

### busMeetsRequirement()

```js
function busMeetsRequirement(busTypeId, minBusType) {
  return (BUS_TYPE_TIER[busTypeId] ?? 0) >= (BUS_TYPE_TIER[minBusType] ?? 0);
}
```

### getBusAssignedDistricts()

```js
function getBusAssignedDistricts(bus) {
  return bus.assignedDistricts || [];
}
```

### getBusTierMultiplier()

```js
function getBusTierMultiplier(bus) {
  const busType = getBusType(bus.typeId);
  const baseIncome = busType ? busType.baseHourlyIncome : (bus.baseHourlyIncome || BUS_TYPES[0].baseHourlyIncome);
  const reference = BUS_TYPES[0].baseHourlyIncome;
  return baseIncome / reference;
}
```

### getBusRouteIncome()

```js
function getBusRouteIncome(bus) {
  const districts = getBusAssignedDistricts(bus);
  if (districts.length === 0) return 0;
  const base = districts.reduce((sum, id) => {
    const d = getDistrictById(id);
    return sum + (d ? d.incomePerHr : 0);
  }, 0);
  return base * getBusTierMultiplier(bus) * (1 + bus.passengerHappiness) * (1 + bus.popularity);
}
```

### getRouteSegmentIncome()

```js
function getRouteSegmentIncome(segmentIds) {
  if (!segmentIds || segmentIds.length === 0) return 0;
  return segmentIds.reduce((sum, id) => {
    const d = getDistrictById(id);
    return sum + (d ? d.incomePerHr : 0);
  }, 0);
}
```

### getCustomRouteIncome()

```js
function getCustomRouteIncome(business) {
  if (!business.fleet || business.fleet.length === 0) return 0;
  let total = 0;
  for (const bus of business.fleet) {
    if (bus.milesRemaining <= 0 || bus.needsMaintenance) continue;
    if (!bus.assignedDriverId) continue;
    total += getBusRouteIncome(bus);
  }
  return Math.floor(total);
}
```

### canAddSegment()

```js
function canAddSegment(newSegId, currentSegmentIds) {
  if (currentSegmentIds.length === 0) return true;
  const newSeg = getDistrictById(newSegId);
  if (!newSeg) return false;
  for (const segId of currentSegmentIds) {
    if (newSeg.adjacent.includes(segId)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}
```

### areSegmentsConnected()

```js
function areSegmentsConnected(segmentIds) {
  if (segmentIds.length <= 1) return true;
  const visited = new Set();
  const queue = [segmentIds[0]];
  visited.add(segmentIds[0]);
  while (queue.length > 0) {
    const id = queue.shift();
    const d = getDistrictById(id);
    if (!d) continue;
    for (const adj of d.adjacent) {
      if (segmentIds.includes(adj) && !visited.has(adj)) {
        visited.add(adj);
        queue.push(adj);
      }
    }
  }
  return visited.size === segmentIds.length;
}
```

### canRemoveSegment()

```js
function canRemoveSegment(segId, currentSegmentIds) {
  const remaining = currentSegmentIds.filter((id) => id !== segId);
  return areSegmentsConnected(remaining);
}
```

```js
  return BUS_TYPES.find((b) => b.id === typeId) || null;
}
```

### getRoute()

```js

  return ROUTES.find((r) => r.id === routeId) || null;
}
```

```js
function getStops(routeId) {
  return ROUTE_STOPS[routeId] || [];
}
```

### getBusLevelMultiplier()

```js
function getBusLevelMultiplier(business) {
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level || 1);
  return Math.pow(1.12, level - 1);
}
```

### getBusCurrentIncomeHourly()

```js
function getBusCurrentIncomeHourly(bus, business) {
  if (!isBusDelivered(bus)) return 0;
  if (bus.milesRemaining <= 0 || bus.needsMaintenance) return 0;
  if (!bus.assignedDriverId) return 0;
  const levelMult = getBusLevelMultiplier(business);
  const assigned = getBusAssignedDistricts(bus);
  let raw = 0;
  if (assigned.length > 0) {
    raw = getBusRouteIncome(bus);
  } else {
    const route = getRoute(business.selectedRoute);
    if (route) {
      raw = (getBusType(bus.typeId)?.baseHourlyIncome || bus.baseHourlyIncome) * route.incomeMultiplier * (1 + bus.passengerHappiness) * (1 + bus.popularity);
    }
  }
  return raw * levelMult;
}
```

### getBusRevenueHourly()

```js
function getBusRevenueHourly(business) {
  if (!business.fleet || business.fleet.length === 0) return 0;
  let total = 0;
  for (const bus of business.fleet) {
    total += getBusCurrentIncomeHourly(bus, business);
  }
  return Math.floor(total);
}
```

### getBusPayrollHourly()

```js
function getBusPayrollHourly(business) {
  return (business.staffMembers || []).reduce((total, m) => total + (m.salary || STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY), 0);
}
```

### getBusInsuranceCostHourly()

```js
function getBusInsuranceCostHourly(business) {
  const fleet = business.fleet || [];
  let total = 0;
  for (const bus of fleet) {
    if (bus.insured) total += (bus.cost * INSURANCE_DAILY_RATE) / 24;
  }
  return total;
}
```

### getBusHourlyIncome()

```js
function getBusHourlyIncome(business) {
  const revenue = getBusRevenueHourly(business);
  const payroll = getBusPayrollHourly(business);
  const insurance = getBusInsuranceCostHourly(business);
  return Math.floor(revenue - payroll - insurance);
}
```

### processBusFleet()

```js
function processBusFleet(business, minutesElapsed) {
  business = completeHiredDrivers(business);
  const hoursFraction = minutesElapsed / 60;
  const suspended = isSuspended(business);
  if (!business.fleet || business.fleet.length === 0) {
    const payroll = getBusPayrollHourly(business) * hoursFraction;
    return { updatedBusiness: { ...business, allTimeEarnings: (business.allTimeEarnings || 0) - payroll }, income: -payroll };
  }
  const route = getRoute(business.selectedRoute);
  const levelMult = getBusLevelMultiplier(business);
  let income = 0;
  const updatedFleet = business.fleet.map((bus) => {
    if (!isBusDelivered(bus)) return bus;
    if (bus.milesRemaining <= 0 || bus.needsMaintenance) return bus;
    if (!bus.assignedDriverId) return bus;
    const assigned = getBusAssignedDistricts(bus);
    let busIncome;
    let milesPerDay;
    if (assigned.length > 0) {
      busIncome = getBusRouteIncome(bus);
      const dists = assigned.map((id) => getDistrictById(id)).filter(Boolean);
      milesPerDay = dists.length > 0 ? dists.reduce((s, d) => s + (d.milesPerDay || 0), 0) : 0;
    } else if (route) {
      busIncome = bus.baseHourlyIncome * route.incomeMultiplier * (1 + bus.passengerHappiness) * (1 + bus.popularity);
      milesPerDay = route.speedMph * 6;
    } else {
      return bus;
    }
    const milesPerMinute = milesPerDay / (24 * 60);
    const milesToUse = milesPerMinute * minutesElapsed;
    let earned = 0;
    let miles = bus.milesRemaining;
    if (miles >= milesToUse) {
      earned = suspended ? 0 : busIncome * hoursFraction;
      miles = miles - milesToUse;
    } else {
      const availableMinutes = miles / milesPerMinute;
      earned = suspended ? 0 : busIncome * (availableMinutes / 60);
      miles = 0;
    }
    income += earned * levelMult;
    return { ...bus, milesRemaining: miles, needsMaintenance: miles <= 0 ? true : bus.needsMaintenance };
  });
  const payroll = getBusPayrollHourly(business) * hoursFraction;
  const insurance = getBusInsuranceCostHourly(business) * hoursFraction;
  income -= (payroll + insurance);
  const updatedBusiness = {
    ...business,
    fleet: updatedFleet,
    allTimeEarnings: (business.allTimeEarnings || 0) + income,
  };
  return { updatedBusiness, income };
}
```

- **BUS_DELIVERY_HOURS**: 0.25

### isBusDelivered()

```js
function isBusDelivered(bus) {
  if (!bus.deliveredAt) return true;
  return Date.now() >= bus.deliveredAt;
}
```

### getBusDeliveryTimeRemaining()

```js
function getBusDeliveryTimeRemaining(bus) {
  if (!bus.deliveredAt) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, bus.deliveredAt - Date.now());
}
```

### getMaxBusSlots()

```js
function getMaxBusSlots(business) {
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level || 1);
  return Math.floor(3 + (30 - 3) * (level - 1) / 29);
}
```

### buyBus()

```js
function buyBus(business, typeId) {
  const type = getBusType(typeId);
  if (!type) return null;
  const bus = {
    instanceId: `${typeId}_${Date.now()}`,
    typeId,
    name: type.name,
    cost: type.cost,
    baseHourlyIncome: type.baseHourlyIncome,
    maxMiles: type.maxMiles,
    milesRemaining: type.maxMiles,
    passengerHappiness: type.passengerHappiness,
    popularity: type.popularity,
    emoji: type.emoji,
    color: type.color,
    needsMaintenance: false,
    assignedDriverId: null,
    insured: false,
    deliveredAt: Date.now() + BUS_DELIVERY_HOURS * 60 * 60 * 1000,
  };
  return {
    ...business,
    fleet: [...(business.fleet || []), bus],
    capitalisation: (business.capitalisation || 0) + type.cost,
  };
}
```

### maintainBus()

```js
function maintainBus(business, instanceId) {
  const bus = business.fleet.find((b) => b.instanceId === instanceId);
  if (!bus) return { business, refund: 0, scrapped: false };
  const milesPct = bus.maxMiles > 0 ? bus.milesRemaining / bus.maxMiles : 0;
  const scrapChance = Math.max(0, 0.3 * (1 - milesPct));
  const scrap = Math.random() < scrapChance;
  if (scrap) {
    const refund = Math.floor(bus.cost * (bus.insured ? INSURANCE_SCRAP_REFUND_RATE : 0.2));
    const updatedFleet = business.fleet.filter((b) => b.instanceId !== instanceId);
    return {
      business: {
        ...business,
        fleet: updatedFleet,
        capitalisation: Math.max(0, (business.capitalisation || 0) - bus.cost),
      },
      refund,
      scrapped: true,
    };
  }
  const updatedFleet = business.fleet.map((b) =>
    b.instanceId === instanceId ? { ...b, milesRemaining: b.maxMiles, needsMaintenance: false } : b
  );
  return { business: { ...business, fleet: updatedFleet }, refund: 0, scrapped: false };
}
```

### selectRoute()

```js
function selectRoute(business, routeId) {
  return { ...business, selectedRoute: routeId };
}
```

### getMaintenanceCost()

```js
function getMaintenanceCost(bus) {
  return Math.floor(bus.cost * 0.08);
}
```

```js
(business) => business.staffMembers || []
```

### getHiringCount()

```js
(business) => (business.hiringStaff || []).length
```

### canHireMore()

```js
(business) => getHiringCount(business) < MAX_HIRING_AT_ONCE
```

### hireBusDrivers()

```js
function hireBusDrivers(business, count) {
  const current = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const availableSlots = MAX_HIRING_AT_ONCE - current.length;
  const toHire = Math.min(count, availableSlots);
  if (toHire <= 0) return null;
  const isFirstHireEver = (business.staffMembers || []).length === 0 && current.length === 0;
  const now = Date.now();
  const cost = toHire * STAFF_HIRE_COST;
  const newHires = Array.from({ length: toHire }, (_, i) => {
    const duration = STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (STAFF_HIRE_MAX_MS - STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS);
    const person = generatePerson();
    return {
      id: `bus_driver_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}_${i}`,
      name: person.name,
      gender: person.gender,
      age: person.age,
      completesAt: isFirstHireEver && i === 0 ? now : now + duration,
    };
  });
  return {
    ...business,
    hiringStaff: [...current, ...newHires],
    capitalisation: (business.capitalisation || 0) + cost,
  };
}
```

### completeHiredDrivers()

```js
function completeHiredDrivers(business) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const completed = hiring.filter((h) => h.completesAt <= now);
  if (completed.length === 0) return business;
  const remaining = hiring.filter((h) => h.completesAt > now);
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (business.staff || 0) + completed.length,
    staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), ...completed.map((h) => ({ id: h.id, name: h.name, gender: h.gender, age: h.age, skill: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) + 3, salary: STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY, hiredAt: now }))],
    hiringStaff: remaining,
  };
}
```

### instantHireDriver()

```js
function instantHireDriver(business, hireId) {
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const target = hiring.find((h) => h.id === hireId);
  if (!target) return business;
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (business.staff || 0) + 1,
    staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), { id: target.id, name: target.name, gender: target.gender, age: target.age, skill: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) + 3, salary: STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY, hiredAt: Date.now() }],
    hiringStaff: hiring.filter((h) => h.id !== hireId),
  };
}
```

### getHireTimeRemaining()

```js
function getHireTimeRemaining(hire) {
  return Math.max(0, hire.completesAt - Date.now());
}
```

### assignBusDriver()

```js
function assignBusDriver(business, busInstanceId, driverId) {
  const driverExists = (business.staffMembers || []).some((s) => s.id === driverId);
  if (!driverExists) return business;
  const fleet = (business.fleet || []).map((b) => {
    if (b.instanceId === busInstanceId) return { ...b, assignedDriverId: driverId };
    if (b.assignedDriverId === driverId) return { ...b, assignedDriverId: null };
    return b;
  });
  return { ...business, fleet };
}
```

### unassignBusDriver()

```js
function unassignBusDriver(business, busInstanceId) {
  const fleet = (business.fleet || []).map((b) =>
    b.instanceId === busInstanceId ? { ...b, assignedDriverId: null } : b
  );
  return { ...business, fleet };
}
```

### fireBusDriver()

```js
function fireBusDriver(business, driverId) {
  const fleet = (business.fleet || []).map((b) =>
    b.assignedDriverId === driverId ? { ...b, assignedDriverId: null } : b
  );
  return {
    ...business,
    fleet,
    staffMembers: (business.staffMembers || []).filter((s) => s.id !== driverId),
  };
}
```

### raiseBusDriver()

```js
function raiseBusDriver(business, driverId) {
  const members = (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => {
    if (s.id === driverId) return { ...s, salary: Math.round((s.salary || STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY) * 1.15) };
    return s;
  });
  return { ...business, staffMembers: members };
}
```

### trainBusDriver()

```js
function trainBusDriver(business, driverId) {
  const members = (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => {
    if (s.id === driverId) return { ...s, skill: Math.min(10, (s.skill || 5) + 1) };
    return s;
  });
  return { ...business, staffMembers: members };
}
```

### getBusHealthPct()

```js
function getBusHealthPct(bus) {
  return bus.maxMiles > 0 ? bus.milesRemaining / bus.maxMiles : 0;
}
```

### canInsureBus()

```js
function canInsureBus(bus) {
  return !bus.insured && getBusHealthPct(bus) > INSURANCE_HEALTH_THRESHOLD;
}
```

### getInsuranceDailyCost()

```js
function getInsuranceDailyCost(bus) {
  return Math.floor(bus.cost * INSURANCE_DAILY_RATE);
}
```

### buyBusInsurance()

```js
function buyBusInsurance(business, instanceId) {
  const bus = (business.fleet || []).find((b) => b.instanceId === instanceId);
  if (!bus || !canInsureBus(bus)) return business;
  const fleet = business.fleet.map((b) => (b.instanceId === instanceId ? { ...b, insured: true } : b));
  return { ...business, fleet };
}
```

```js
    case 'bus': return BUS_TAX_RATE;

}
```

### getUpgradeName()

```js
function getUpgradeName(business) {
  const sub = getSubType(business.type, business.subType);
  if (!sub || business.level >= sub.maxLevel) return 'Fully upgraded';
  const titles = business.type === 'bus'
    ? BUS_UPGRADE_TITLES
    : business.type === 'courier'
    ? COURIER_UPGRADE_TITLES
    : business.type === 'gamedev'
    ? GAMEDEV_UPGRADE_TITLES
    : business.type === 'construction'
    ? CONSTRUCTION_UPGRADE_TITLES
    : business.type === 'dealership'
    ? DEALERSHIP_UPGRADE_TITLES
    : business.type === 'clothing'
    ? CLOTHING_UPGRADE_TITLES
    : business.type === 'bank'
    ? BANK_UPGRADE_TITLES
    : business.type === 'moviestudio'
    ? MOVIESTUDIO_UPGRADE_TITLES
    : UPGRADE_TITLES;
  const title = titles[(business.level - 1) % titles.length];
  const phase = Math.floor((business.level - 1) / titles.length) + 1;
  return `${title} · Phase ${phase}`;
}
```

### getSubType()

```js
function getSubType(type, subTypeKey) {
  return BUSINESS_CATEGORIES[type]?.subTypes?.[subTypeKey];
}
```

### getUpgradeCost()

```js
function getUpgradeCost(business) {
  const sub = getSubType(business.type, business.subType);
  if (!sub) return Infinity;
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level);
  const base = sub.cost * 0.1;
  return Math.floor(base * Math.pow(1.15, level));
}
```

### getGlobalBoostMultiplier()

```js
function getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt) {
  if (!boostExpiresAt) return 1;
  return boostExpiresAt > Date.now() ? 10 : 1;
}
```

### getMaxHourlyIncome()

```js
function getMaxHourlyIncome(business, boostExpiresAt) {
  const sub = getSubType(business.type, business.subType);
  if (!sub || business.isSuspended) return 0;
  if (business.type === 'cafe') return getCafeHourlyIncome(business);
  if (business.type === 'bus') return getBusHourlyIncome(business);
  if (business.type === 'courier') return getCarHourlyIncome(business);
  if (business.type === 'gamedev') return getGameDevHourlyIncome(business);
  if (business.type === 'construction') return getConstructionHourlyIncome(business);
  if (business.type === 'moviestudio') return getMovieStudioHourlyIncome(business);
  const base = sub.baseHourlyIncome;
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level);
  const growthRate = sub.growthRate || 1.15;
  const income = Math.floor(base * Math.pow(growthRate, level - 1));
  return income * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
}
```

### getHourlyStockUsage()

```js
function getHourlyStockUsage(business) {
  const sub = getSubType(business.type, business.subType);
  if (!sub) return 0;
  return (sub.stockPerHourPerLevel || 0) * Math.max(1, business.level);
}
```

### getHourlyIncome()

```js
function getHourlyIncome(business, boostExpiresAt) {
  if (business.type === 'cafe') return getCafeHourlyIncome(business) * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
  if (business.type === 'bus') return getBusHourlyIncome(business) * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
  if (business.type === 'courier') return getCarHourlyIncome(business) * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
  if (business.type === 'gamedev') return getGameDevHourlyIncome(business) * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
  if (business.type === 'construction') return getConstructionHourlyIncome(business) * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
  if (business.type === 'dealership') return getDealershipHourlyIncome(business) * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
  if (business.type === 'clothing') return getClothingHourlyIncome(business) * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
  if (business.type === 'bank') return getBankHourlyIncome(business) * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
  if (business.type === 'moviestudio') return getMovieStudioHourlyIncome(business) * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
  const max = getMaxHourlyIncome(business, boostExpiresAt);
  const usage = getHourlyStockUsage(business);
  if (usage <= 0) return max;
  const stock = business.stock || 0;
  if (stock <= 0) return 0;
  const factor = Math.min(1, stock / usage);
  return Math.floor(max * factor);
}
```

### getStockCost()

```js
function getStockCost(business, quantity) {
  const sub = getSubType(business.type, business.subType);
  if (!sub) return Infinity;
  return Math.ceil(quantity * (sub.stockCostPerUnit || 0));
}
```

### processBusinesses()

```js
function processBusinesses(ownedBusinesses, minutesElapsed, boostExpiresAt) {
  const { updatedBusinesses: afterUpgrades } = completePendingUpgrades(ownedBusinesses);
  const businesses = afterUpgrades;
  let totalIncome = 0;
  const updated = businesses.map((business) => {
    if (business.type === 'cafe') {
      const { updatedBusiness, income } = processCafeBusiness(business, minutesElapsed);
      totalIncome += income * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
      return updatedBusiness;
    }
    if (business.type === 'bus') {
      const { updatedBusiness, income } = processBusFleet(business, minutesElapsed);
      totalIncome += income * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
      return updatedBusiness;
    }
    if (business.type === 'courier') {
      const { updatedBusiness, income } = processCourierFleet(business, minutesElapsed);
      totalIncome += income * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
      return updatedBusiness;
    }
    if (business.type === 'gamedev') {
      const { updatedBusiness, income } = processGameDev(business, minutesElapsed);
      totalIncome += income * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
      return updatedBusiness;
    }
    if (business.type === 'construction') {
      const { updatedBusiness, income } = processConstruction(business, minutesElapsed);
      totalIncome += income * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
      return updatedBusiness;
    }
    if (business.type === 'dealership') {
      const { updatedBusiness, income } = processDealership(business, minutesElapsed);
      totalIncome += income * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
      return updatedBusiness;
    }
    if (business.type === 'clothing') {
      const { updatedBusiness, income } = processClothingBusiness(business, minutesElapsed);
      totalIncome += income * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
      return updatedBusiness;
    }
    if (business.type === 'bank') {
      const { updatedBusiness, income } = processBankBusiness(business, minutesElapsed);
      totalIncome += income * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
      return updatedBusiness;
    }
    if (business.type === 'moviestudio') {
      const { updatedBusiness, income } = processMovieStudio(business, minutesElapsed);
      totalIncome += income * getGlobalBoostMultiplier(boostExpiresAt);
      return updatedBusiness;
    }
    const maxIncome = getMaxHourlyIncome(business, boostExpiresAt);
    const usage = getHourlyStockUsage(business);
    const hoursFraction = minutesElapsed / 60;
    const needed = usage * hoursFraction;
    const stock = business.stock || 0;
    const use = Math.min(stock, needed);
    const income = needed > 0 ? maxIncome * hoursFraction * (use / needed) : maxIncome * hoursFraction;
    totalIncome += income;
    return {
      ...business,
      stock: Math.max(0, stock - use),
      allTimeEarnings: (business.allTimeEarnings || 0) + income,
    };
  });
  return { updatedBusinesses: updated, totalIncome };
}
```

### buyStock()

```js
function buyStock(business, quantity) {
  const sub = getSubType(business.type, business.subType);
  if (!sub || quantity <= 0) return null;
  return {
    ...business,
    stock: (business.stock || 0) + quantity,
  };
}
```

### getTaxOwed()

```js
function getTaxOwed(business) {
  const sub = getSubType(business.type, business.subType);
  if (!sub) return 0;
  const hoursSincePaid = (Date.now() - (business.taxLastPaidAt || business.startedAt || Date.now())) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
  const daysSincePaid = hoursSincePaid / 24;
  const net = Math.max(0, getHourlyIncome(business));
  return Math.floor(net * getBusinessTaxRate(business) * 24 * daysSincePaid);
}
```

### isSuspended()

```js
function isSuspended(business) {
  if (getTaxOwed(business) <= 0) return false;
  const hoursSincePaid = (Date.now() - (business.taxLastPaidAt || business.startedAt || Date.now())) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
  return hoursSincePaid >= 24 * 5;
}
```

### getHoursUntilSuspension()

```js
function getHoursUntilSuspension(business) {
  const hoursSincePaid = (Date.now() - (business.taxLastPaidAt || business.startedAt || Date.now())) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
  return Math.max(0, 24 * 5 - hoursSincePaid);
}
```

### getDailyTax()

```js
function getDailyTax(business) {
  const sub = getSubType(business.type, business.subType);
  if (!sub) return 0;
  const net = Math.max(0, getHourlyIncome(business));
  return Math.floor(net * getBusinessTaxRate(business) * 24);
}
```

### getSuspensionTimestamp()

```js
function getSuspensionTimestamp(business) {
  return (business.taxLastPaidAt || business.startedAt || Date.now()) + 5 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
}
```

### getSellPrice()

```js
function getSellPrice(business) {
  const sub = getSubType(business.type, business.subType);
  if (!sub) return 0;
  let upgradeTotal = 0;
  for (let l = 1; l < business.level; l++) {
    const tempBiz = { ...business, level: l };
    upgradeTotal += getUpgradeCost(tempBiz);
  }
  const earningsValue = (business.allTimeEarnings || 0) * 0.5;
  const taxPenalty = getTaxOwed(business);
  const fleetValue = (business.type === 'bus' || business.type === 'courier')
    ? (business.fleet || []).reduce((sum, v) => sum + (v.cost || 0), 0)
    : business.type === 'gamedev'
    ? (business.games || []).reduce((sum, g) => sum + (g.income || 0), 0) * 0.5
    : business.type === 'construction'
    ? (business.jobs || []).reduce((sum, j) => sum + (j.payout || 0), 0) * 0.3
    : business.type === 'dealership'
    ? (business.dealerships || []).reduce((sum, dp) => sum + (dp.inventory || []).reduce((s, v) => s + (v.wholesale || 0), 0), 0)
    : business.type === 'clothing'
    ? (business.branches || []).reduce((sum, b) => sum + (b.assignedStaffIds || []).length * 5000, 0)
    : business.type === 'bank'
    ? (business.branches || []).reduce((sum, b) => sum + (b.currentDeposits || 0) * 0.1, 0)
    : business.type === 'moviestudio'
    ? (business.movies || []).reduce((sum, m) => sum + (m.income || 0), 0) * 0.5
    : 0;
  return Math.max(0, Math.floor(sub.cost + upgradeTotal + earningsValue + fleetValue - taxPenalty));
}
```

### getTotalBusinessIncome()

```js
function getTotalBusinessIncome(ownedBusinesses, boostExpiresAt) {
  return ownedBusinesses.reduce((sum, b) => sum + getHourlyIncome(b, boostExpiresAt), 0);
}
```

### calculateBusinessIncome()

```js
function calculateBusinessIncome(ownedBusinesses, minutesElapsed, boostExpiresAt) {
  const { totalIncome } = processBusinesses(ownedBusinesses, minutesElapsed, boostExpiresAt);
  return totalIncome;
}
```

### startBusiness()

```js
function startBusiness(type, subTypeKey) {
  const sub = getSubType(type, subTypeKey);
  if (!sub) return null;
  const now = Date.now();
  const base = {
    id: `${type}_${subTypeKey}_${now}`,
    type,
    subType: subTypeKey,
    name: sub.name,
    level: 1,
    allTimeEarnings: 0,
    capitalisation: sub.cost,
    stock: sub.startingStock || 0,
    taxLastPaidAt: now,
    startedAt: now,
  };
  if (type === 'cafe') {
    return { ...base, cafeVersion: 2, branches: [{ branchId: 'suburbs', menuIds: ['espresso', 'latte', 'pastry'], upgrades: {}, assignedStaffIds: [] }], staffMembers: [], hiringStaff: [], supplies: { beans: 0, milk: 0, pastry: 0, produce: 0 }, pendingSupplies: [], tutorialStep: 0 };
  }
  if (type === 'bus') {
    return { ...base, fleet: [], selectedRoute: 'towncentre' };
  }
  if (type === 'courier') {
    return { ...base, fleet: [], staff: 3, hiringStaff: [], activeJobType: 'food' };
  }
  if (type === 'gamedev') {
    return { ...base, games: [], contracts: [], availableContracts: [], staff: 2, hiringStaff: [], contractsRefreshedAt: now };
  }
  if (type === 'construction') {
    return { ...base, jobs: [], resources: {}, pendingResources: {}, deliveries: [], availableJobs: [], jobsRefreshedAt: now, selectedZones: [], staff: 2, hiringStaff: [] };
  }
  if (type === 'dealership') {
    return { ...base, dealerships: [], pendingDeliveries: [], staff: 0, hiringStaff: [], busLicense: false, completedSales: 0 };
  }
  if (type === 'clothing') {
    return { ...base, clothingVersion: 2, branches: [{ branchId: 'suburbs', menuIds: ['tshirt', 'jeans', 'sneakers'], upgrades: {}, assignedStaffIds: [] }], staffMembers: [], hiringStaff: [], supplies: { basics: 0, denim: 0, premium: 0, accessories: 0 }, pendingSupplies: [] };
  }
  if (type === 'bank') {
    return { ...base, branches: [], staff: 0, staffMembers: [], hiringStaff: [], completedTransactions: 0 };
  }
  if (type === 'moviestudio') {
    return { ...base, movies: [], staff: 2, staffMembers: [], hiringStaff: [], sets: [] };
  }
  return base;
}
```

### getUpgradeDuration()

```js
function getUpgradeDuration(level) {
  const minMs = 3000;
  const maxMs = 45 * 60 * 1000;
  return Math.floor(minMs + Math.random() * (maxMs - minMs));
}
```

### upgradeBusiness()

```js
function upgradeBusiness(business) {
  const cost = getUpgradeCost(business);
  const sub = getSubType(business.type, business.subType);
  if (!sub || business.level >= sub.maxLevel) return null;
  if (business.upgradeCompletesAt && business.upgradeCompletesAt > Date.now()) return null;
  const duration = getUpgradeDuration(business.level);
  return {
    ...business,
    upgradeCompletesAt: Date.now() + duration,
    upgradeDuration: duration,
  };
}
```

### completePendingUpgrades()

```js
function completePendingUpgrades(businesses) {
  const now = Date.now();
  let anyChanged = false;
  const updated = businesses.map((business) => {
    if (business.upgradeCompletesAt && business.upgradeCompletesAt <= now) {
      const cost = getUpgradeCost(business);
      anyChanged = true;
      const { upgradeCompletesAt, upgradeDuration, ...rest } = business;
      return {
        ...rest,
        level: business.level + 1,
        capitalisation: (business.capitalisation || 0) + cost,
      };
    }
    return business;
  });
  return { updatedBusinesses: updated, anyChanged };
}
```

### completeUpgradeNow()

```js
function completeUpgradeNow(business) {
  if (!business.upgradeCompletesAt) return business;
  const cost = getUpgradeCost(business);
  const { upgradeCompletesAt, upgradeDuration, ...rest } = business;
  return {
    ...rest,
    level: business.level + 1,
    capitalisation: (business.capitalisation || 0) + cost,
  };
}
```

### getUpgradeProgress()

```js
function getUpgradeProgress(business) {
  if (!business.upgradeCompletesAt) return null;
  const now = Date.now();
  const duration = business.upgradeDuration || getUpgradeDuration(business.level);
  const remaining = Math.max(0, business.upgradeCompletesAt - now);
  const elapsed = duration - remaining;
  const pct = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (elapsed / duration) * 100));
  return { remaining, pct, duration };
}
```

### payTax()

```js
function payTax(business) {
  return {
    ...business,
    taxLastPaidAt: Date.now(),
  };
}
```

### activateGlobalBusinessBoost()

```js
function activateGlobalBusinessBoost() {
  return Date.now() + 60 * 60 * 1000;
}
```

### getOptimalStaffForBranch()

```js
function getOptimalStaffForBranch(branchId) {
  const zone = getCafeBranch(branchId);
  if (!zone) return 1;
  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(zone.capacity / 8));
}
```

```js
(id) => CAFE_STAFF_TYPES.find((item) => item.id === id) || null
```

```js
(id) => CAFE_MENU.find((item) => item.id === id) || null
```

```js
(business, typeId) => (business.staffMembers || []).filter((member) => !typeId || member.typeId === typeId)
```

```js
(business) => business.branches || []
```

```js
(hire) => Math.max(0, hire.completesAt - Date.now())
```

### formatCafeTime()

```js
function formatCafeTime(ms) {
  const seconds = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(ms / 1000));
  const hours = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const minutes = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
  return hours ? `${hours}h ${minutes}m` : `${minutes}m ${seconds % 60}s`;
}
```

### makeStaffMember()

```js
(typeId, rating) => {
  const type = getCafeStaffType(typeId);
  const person = generatePerson();
  return {
    id: `cafe_staff_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    typeId,
    name: person.name,
    gender: person.gender,
    age: person.age,
    rating: rating ?? Math.floor(type.ratingMin + Math.random() * (type.ratingMax - type.ratingMin + 1)),
    salary: type?.salaryHourly || 0,
  };
}
```

### hydrateCafe()

```js
function hydrateCafe(business) {
  if (business.cafeVersion === 2) {
    const needsMigration = (business.branches || []).some((b) => !b.assignedStaffIds);
    if (!needsMigration) return business;
    return {
      ...business,
      branches: (business.branches || []).map((b) => ({
        ...b,
        assignedStaffIds: b.assignedStaffIds || (b.openedAt ? (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => s.id) : []),
      })),
    };
  }
  const oldStock = business.stock || 0;
  const existingBranches = business.branches || [];
  return {
    ...business,
    cafeVersion: 2,
    branches: existingBranches.length > 0
      ? existingBranches.map((b) => ({ ...b, assignedStaffIds: b.assignedStaffIds || (b.openedAt ? (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => s.id) : []) }))
      : [{ branchId: 'suburbs', menuIds: ['espresso', 'latte', 'pastry'], upgrades: {}, assignedStaffIds: [] }],
    staffMembers: business.staffMembers || [],
    hiringStaff: business.hiringStaff || [],
    supplies: business.supplies || { beans: Math.max(30, oldStock), milk: Math.max(30, oldStock), pastry: Math.max(20, Math.floor(oldStock / 2)), produce: 15 },
    pendingSupplies: business.pendingSupplies || [],
    tutorialStep: business.tutorialStep || 0,
  };
}
```

### hireCafeStaff()

```js
function hireCafeStaff(business, typeId, count) {
  const type = getCafeStaffType(typeId);
  if (!type || count < 1 || count > MAX_HIRE_BATCH) return { error: 'invalid_hire' };
  if ((business.hiringStaff || []).some((hire) => hire.typeId === typeId)) return { error: 'type_hiring' };
  const isFirstHireEver = (business.staffMembers || []).length === 0 && (business.hiringStaff || []).length === 0;
  const now = Date.now();
  const hires = Array.from({ length: count }, () => {
    const duration = STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (STAFF_HIRE_MAX_MS - STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS);
    return { id: `cafe_hire_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`, typeId, rating: Math.floor(type.ratingMin + Math.random() * (type.ratingMax - type.ratingMin + 1)), completesAt: isFirstHireEver ? now : now + duration };
  });
  return { ...business, hiringStaff: [...(business.hiringStaff || []), ...hires] };
}
```

### completeCafeHires()

```js
function completeCafeHires(business) {
  const ready = (business.hiringStaff || []).filter((hire) => hire.completesAt <= Date.now());
  if (!ready.length) return business;
  return { ...business, staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), ...ready.map((hire) => makeStaffMember(hire.typeId, hire.rating))], hiringStaff: (business.hiringStaff || []).filter((hire) => hire.completesAt > Date.now()) };
}
```

### instantHireCafeStaff()

```js
function instantHireCafeStaff(business, hireId) {
  const hire = (business.hiringStaff || []).find((item) => item.id === hireId);
  if (!hire) return business;
  return { ...business, staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), makeStaffMember(hire.typeId, hire.rating)], hiringStaff: business.hiringStaff.filter((item) => item.id !== hireId) };
}
```

### fireCafeStaff()

```js
function fireCafeStaff(business, staffId) {
  const branches = getCafeOwnedBranches(business).map((b) => ({
    ...b,
    assignedStaffIds: (b.assignedStaffIds || []).filter((id) => id !== staffId),
  }));
  return {
    ...business,
    branches,
    staffMembers: (business.staffMembers || []).filter((s) => s.id !== staffId),
  };
}
```

### raiseCafeStaff()

```js
function raiseCafeStaff(business, staffId) {
  const members = (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => {
    if (s.id === staffId) {
      const type = getCafeStaffType(s.typeId);
      const currentSalary = type?.salaryHourly || 0;
      return { ...s, salary: Math.round((s.salary || currentSalary) * 1.15) };
    }
    return s;
  });
  return { ...business, staffMembers: members };
}
```

### trainCafeStaff()

```js
function trainCafeStaff(business, staffId) {
  const members = (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => {
    if (s.id === staffId) return { ...s, rating: Math.min(10, (s.rating || 5) + 1) };
    return s;
  });
  return { ...business, staffMembers: members };
}
```

### isCafeBranchUnlocked()

```js
function isCafeBranchUnlocked(business, branchId) {
  const branch = getCafeBranch(branchId);
  if (!branch) return false;
  if (!branch.requiresBranch) return true;
  return !!getCafeBranchById(business, branch.requiresBranch);
}
```

### getUnlockedCafeBranches()

```js
function getUnlockedCafeBranches(business) {
  return CAFE_BRANCHES.filter((b) => isCafeBranchUnlocked(business, b.id));
}
```

### openCafeBranch()

```js
function openCafeBranch(business, branchId) {
  const branch = getCafeBranch(branchId);
  if (!branch) return { error: 'invalid_branch' };
  if (!isCafeBranchUnlocked(business, branchId)) return { error: 'locked' };
  if (getCafeBranchById(business, branchId)) return { error: 'owned' };
  return { ...business, branches: [...getCafeOwnedBranches(business), { branchId, menuIds: ['espresso', 'latte', 'pastry'], upgrades: {}, assignedStaffIds: [] }] };
}
```

### getAssignedStaff()

```js
function getAssignedStaff(business, branchId) {
  const branch = getCafeBranchById(business, branchId);
  if (!branch) return [];
  return (branch.assignedStaffIds || []).map((id) => (business.staffMembers || []).find((s) => s.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
}
```

### assignCafeStaff()

```js
function assignCafeStaff(business, branchId, staffId) {
  const branch = getCafeBranchById(business, branchId);
  if (!branch) return business;
  if (!(business.staffMembers || []).some((s) => s.id === staffId)) return business;
  const branches = getCafeOwnedBranches(business).map((b) => {
    if (b.branchId === branchId) {
      return { ...b, assignedStaffIds: [...new Set([...(b.assignedStaffIds || []), staffId])] };
    }
    return { ...b, assignedStaffIds: (b.assignedStaffIds || []).filter((id) => id !== staffId) };
  });
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### unassignCafeStaff()

```js
function unassignCafeStaff(business, branchId, staffId) {
  const branch = getCafeBranchById(business, branchId);
  if (!branch) return business;
  const branches = getCafeOwnedBranches(business).map((b) =>
    b.branchId === branchId
      ? { ...b, assignedStaffIds: (b.assignedStaffIds || []).filter((id) => id !== staffId) }
      : b
  );
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### canOpenCafeBranch()

```js
function canOpenCafeBranch(business, branchId) {
  const branch = getCafeBranchById(business, branchId);
  if (!branch) return { canOpen: false, reason: 'invalid_branch' };
  if (branch.openedAt || branch.openingAt) return { canOpen: false, reason: 'already_open' };
  const assigned = getAssignedStaff(business, branchId);
  if (assigned.length === 0) return { canOpen: false, reason: 'no_staff' };
  const supplies = business.supplies || {};
  const hasSupplies = CAFE_INGREDIENTS.some((ing) => (supplies[ing.id] || 0) > 0);
  if (!hasSupplies) return { canOpen: false, reason: 'no_supplies' };
  return { canOpen: true };
}
```

### startCafeBranchOpening()

```js
function startCafeBranchOpening(business, branchId) {
  const check = canOpenCafeBranch(business, branchId);
  if (!check.canOpen) return { error: check.reason };
  const isFirstBranchEver = getCafeOwnedBranches(business).every((b) => !b.openedAt);
  const openMs = isFirstBranchEver ? 5000 : BRANCH_OPEN_MS_MIN + Math.random() * (BRANCH_OPEN_MS_MAX - BRANCH_OPEN_MS_MIN);
  const branches = getCafeOwnedBranches(business).map((b) =>
    b.branchId === branchId ? { ...b, openingAt: Date.now() + openMs } : b
  );
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### completeCafeOpenings()

```js
(business) => ({ ...business, branches: getCafeOwnedBranches(business).map((branch) => branch.openingAt && branch.openingAt <= Date.now() ? { ...branch, openedAt: Date.now(), openingAt: null } : branch) })
```

### instantOpenCafeBranch()

```js
function instantOpenCafeBranch(business, branchId) {
  const branches = getCafeOwnedBranches(business).map((b) =>
    b.branchId === branchId && b.openingAt ? { ...b, openedAt: Date.now(), openingAt: null } : b
  );
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### buyCafeSupplies()

```js
function buyCafeSupplies(business, ingredientId, quantity) {
  const ingredient = getCafeIngredient(ingredientId);
  if (!ingredient || quantity <= 0) return null;
  const isFirstSupplyEver = (business.pendingSupplies || []).length === 0
    && Object.values(business.supplies || {}).every((v) => !v);
  const deliveryMs = isFirstSupplyEver ? 0 : SUPPLY_DELIVERY_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (SUPPLY_DELIVERY_MAX_MS - SUPPLY_DELIVERY_MIN_MS);
  const delivery = {
    id: `cafe_supply_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    ingredientId,
    quantity,
    deliveredAt: Date.now() + deliveryMs,
  };
  return { business: { ...business, pendingSupplies: [...(business.pendingSupplies || []), delivery] }, cost: ingredient.unitCost * quantity };
}
```

### getCafeSupplyDeliveryTimeRemaining()

```js
function getCafeSupplyDeliveryTimeRemaining(delivery) {
  return Math.max(0, delivery.deliveredAt - Date.now());
}
```

### getArrivedCafeSupplies()

```js
function getArrivedCafeSupplies(business) {
  const now = Date.now();
  return (business.pendingSupplies || []).filter((d) => d.deliveredAt <= now);
}
```

### completeCafeSupplies()

```js
function completeCafeSupplies(business) {
  const arrived = getArrivedCafeSupplies(business);
  if (!arrived.length) return business;
  const pending = (business.pendingSupplies || []).filter((d) => d.deliveredAt > Date.now());
  const supplies = { ...(business.supplies || {}) };
  for (const d of arrived) supplies[d.ingredientId] = (supplies[d.ingredientId] || 0) + d.quantity;
  return { ...business, pendingSupplies: pending, supplies };
}
```

### instantCafeSupply()

```js
function instantCafeSupply(business, deliveryId) {
  const delivery = (business.pendingSupplies || []).find((d) => d.id === deliveryId);
  if (!delivery) return business;
  const updated = { ...business, pendingSupplies: (business.pendingSupplies || []).map((d) => d.id === deliveryId ? { ...d, deliveredAt: Date.now() } : d) };
  return completeCafeSupplies(updated);
}
```

### setCafeMenu()

```js
(business, branchId, menuIds) => ({ ...business, branches: getCafeOwnedBranches(business).map((branch) => branch.branchId === branchId && !branch.openingAt ? { ...branch, menuIds } : branch) })
```

### getCafeSalaryHourly()

```js
function getCafeSalaryHourly(business) {
  return getCafeStaffMembers(business).reduce((total, member) => total + (member.salary || getCafeStaffType(member.typeId)?.salaryHourly || 0), 0);
}
```

### getCafeLevelMultiplier()

```js
function getCafeLevelMultiplier(business) {
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level || 1);
  return Math.pow(1.12, level - 1);
}
```

### getCafeSalesHourly()

```js
function getCafeSalesHourly(business) {
  const levelMult = getCafeLevelMultiplier(business);
  let total = 0;
  for (const branch of getCafeOwnedBranches(business)) {
    if (branch.openingAt || !branch.openedAt) continue;
    const zone = getCafeBranch(branch.branchId);
    const assignedStaff = (branch.assignedStaffIds || []).map((id) => (business.staffMembers || []).find((s) => s.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
    const assignedCount = assignedStaff.length;
    const optimalStaff = getOptimalStaffForBranch(branch.branchId);
    const capacityFactor = Math.min(1, assignedCount / optimalStaff);
    const managerCount = assignedStaff.filter((s) => s.typeId === 'manager').length;
    const footfallVolumeMult = 1 + zone.footfall * 0.15;
    total += (branch.menuIds || []).reduce((sum, id) => {
      const item = getCafeMenuItem(id);
      return sum + (item ? item.price * item.demand * zone.footfall * capacityFactor * (1 + managerCount * 0.03) : 0);
    }, 0) * footfallVolumeMult;
  }
  return total * levelMult;
}
```

### getCafeIngredientCostHourly()

```js
function getCafeIngredientCostHourly(business) {
  const levelMult = getCafeLevelMultiplier(business);
  let total = 0;
  for (const branch of getCafeOwnedBranches(business)) {
    if (branch.openingAt || !branch.openedAt) continue;
    const zone = getCafeBranch(branch.branchId);
    const assignedStaff = (branch.assignedStaffIds || []).map((id) => (business.staffMembers || []).find((s) => s.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
    const assignedCount = assignedStaff.length;
    const optimalStaff = getOptimalStaffForBranch(branch.branchId);
    const capacityFactor = Math.min(1, assignedCount / optimalStaff);
    for (const id of branch.menuIds || []) {
      const item = getCafeMenuItem(id);
      if (!item) continue;
      for (const [ingredientId, units] of Object.entries(item.ingredients)) total += item.demand * zone.footfall * capacityFactor * units * (getCafeIngredient(ingredientId)?.unitCost || 0);
    }
  }
  return total * levelMult;
}
```

### getCafeBranchNetIncomeHourly()

```js
function getCafeBranchNetIncomeHourly(business, branchId) {
  const branch = getCafeBranchById(business, branchId);
  if (!branch || branch.openingAt || !branch.openedAt) return 0;
  const zone = getCafeBranch(branchId);
  if (!zone) return 0;
  const assignedStaff = (branch.assignedStaffIds || []).map((id) => (business.staffMembers || []).find((s) => s.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
  const assignedCount = assignedStaff.length;
  const optimalStaff = getOptimalStaffForBranch(branchId);
  const capacityFactor = Math.min(1, assignedCount / optimalStaff);
  const managerCount = assignedStaff.filter((s) => s.typeId === 'manager').length;
  const footfallVolumeMult = 1 + zone.footfall * 0.15;
  let revenue = 0;
  let ingredientCost = 0;
  for (const id of branch.menuIds || []) {
    const item = getCafeMenuItem(id);
    if (!item) continue;
    revenue += item.price * item.demand * zone.footfall * capacityFactor * (1 + managerCount * 0.03) * footfallVolumeMult;
    for (const [ingredientId, units] of Object.entries(item.ingredients)) {
      ingredientCost += item.demand * zone.footfall * capacityFactor * units * (getCafeIngredient(ingredientId)?.unitCost || 0);
    }
  }
  const payroll = assignedStaff.reduce((sum, s) => sum + (getCafeStaffType(s.typeId)?.salaryHourly || 0), 0);
  return revenue - ingredientCost - payroll - zone.rentHourly;
}
```

### getCafeRentHourly()

```js
function getCafeRentHourly(business) {
  return getCafeOwnedBranches(business).filter((branch) => branch.openedAt && !branch.openingAt).reduce((total, branch) => total + (getCafeBranch(branch.branchId)?.rentHourly || 0), 0);
}
```

### getCafeHourlyIncome()

```js
function getCafeHourlyIncome(business) {
  const hydrated = hydrateCafe(business);
  return Math.floor(getCafeSalesHourly(hydrated) - getCafeIngredientCostHourly(hydrated) - getCafeSalaryHourly(hydrated) - getCafeRentHourly(hydrated));
}
```

### processCafeBusiness()

```js
function processCafeBusiness(business, minutesElapsed) {
  let updated = hydrateCafe(business);
  updated = completeCafeHires(updated);
  updated = completeCafeOpenings(updated);
  updated = completeCafeSupplies(updated);
  const hours = minutesElapsed / 60;
  const ingredientNeeds = {};
  for (const branch of getCafeOwnedBranches(updated)) {
    if (branch.openingAt || !branch.openedAt) continue;
    const zone = getCafeBranch(branch.branchId);
    const assignedCount = (branch.assignedStaffIds || []).length;
    const optimalStaff = getOptimalStaffForBranch(branch.branchId);
    const capacityFactor = Math.min(1, assignedCount / optimalStaff);
    for (const id of branch.menuIds || []) {
      const item = getCafeMenuItem(id);
      if (!item) continue;
      for (const [ingredientId, units] of Object.entries(item.ingredients)) ingredientNeeds[ingredientId] = (ingredientNeeds[ingredientId] || 0) + item.demand * zone.footfall * capacityFactor * units * hours;
    }
  }
  const supplyFactor = Object.entries(ingredientNeeds).reduce((factor, [id, needed]) => needed > 0 ? Math.min(factor, (updated.supplies?.[id] || 0) / needed) : factor, 1);
  const grossSales = getCafeSalesHourly(updated) * hours * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, supplyFactor));
  const ingredientCost = getCafeIngredientCostHourly(updated) * hours * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, supplyFactor));
  const payroll = getCafeSalaryHourly(updated) * hours;
  const rent = getCafeRentHourly(updated) * hours;
  const supplies = { ...(updated.supplies || {}) };
  Object.entries(ingredientNeeds).forEach(([id, needed]) => { supplies[id] = Math.max(0, (supplies[id] || 0) - needed * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, supplyFactor))); });
  const suspended = isSuspended(updated);
  const income = suspended ? -(payroll + rent) : grossSales - ingredientCost - payroll - rent;
  return { updatedBusiness: { ...updated, supplies, allTimeEarnings: (updated.allTimeEarnings || 0) + income }, income };
}
```

### getOptimalStaffForStore()

```js
function getOptimalStaffForStore(storeId) {
  const zone = getClothingStore(storeId);
  if (!zone) return 1;
  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(zone.capacity / 8));
}
```
