### formatClothingTime()

```js
function formatClothingTime(ms) {
  const seconds = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(ms / 1000));
  const hours = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const minutes = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
  return hours ? `${hours}h ${minutes}m` : `${minutes}m ${seconds % 60}s`;
}
```

### makeStaffMember()

```js
(typeId, rating) => {
  const type = getClothingStaffType(typeId);
  const person = generatePerson();
  return {
    id: `clothing_staff_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    typeId,
    name: person.name,
    gender: person.gender,
    age: person.age,
    rating: rating ?? Math.floor(type.ratingMin + Math.random() * (type.ratingMax - type.ratingMin + 1)),
    salary: type?.salaryHourly || 0,
  };
}
```

### hydrateClothing()

```js
function hydrateClothing(business) {
  if (business.clothingVersion === 2) {
    const needsMigration = (business.branches || []).some((b) => !b.assignedStaffIds);
    if (!needsMigration) return business;
    return {
      ...business,
      branches: (business.branches || []).map((b) => ({
        ...b,
        assignedStaffIds: b.assignedStaffIds || (b.openedAt ? (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => s.id) : []),
      })),
    };
  }
  const oldStock = business.stock || 0;
  const existingBranches = business.branches || [];
  return {
    ...business,
    clothingVersion: 2,
    branches: existingBranches.length > 0
      ? existingBranches.map((b) => ({ ...b, assignedStaffIds: b.assignedStaffIds || (b.openedAt ? (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => s.id) : []) }))
      : [{ branchId: 'suburbs', menuIds: ['tshirt', 'jeans', 'sneakers'], upgrades: {}, assignedStaffIds: [] }],
    staffMembers: business.staffMembers || [],
    hiringStaff: business.hiringStaff || [],
    supplies: business.supplies || { basics: Math.max(30, oldStock), denim: Math.max(30, oldStock), premium: Math.max(20, Math.floor(oldStock / 2)), accessories: 15 },
    pendingSupplies: business.pendingSupplies || [],
  };
}
```

### hireClothingStaff()

```js
function hireClothingStaff(business, typeId, count) {
  const type = getClothingStaffType(typeId);
  if (!type || count < 1 || count > MAX_HIRE_BATCH) return { error: 'invalid_hire' };
  if ((business.hiringStaff || []).some((hire) => hire.typeId === typeId)) return { error: 'type_hiring' };
  const isFirstHireEver = (business.staffMembers || []).length === 0 && (business.hiringStaff || []).length === 0;
  const now = Date.now();
  const hires = Array.from({ length: count }, () => {
    const duration = STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (STAFF_HIRE_MAX_MS - STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS);
    return { id: `clothing_hire_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`, typeId, rating: Math.floor(type.ratingMin + Math.random() * (type.ratingMax - type.ratingMin + 1)), completesAt: isFirstHireEver ? now : now + duration };
  });
  return { ...business, hiringStaff: [...(business.hiringStaff || []), ...hires] };
}
```

### completeClothingHires()

```js
function completeClothingHires(business) {
  const ready = (business.hiringStaff || []).filter((hire) => hire.completesAt <= Date.now());
  if (!ready.length) return business;
  return { ...business, staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), ...ready.map((hire) => makeStaffMember(hire.typeId, hire.rating))], hiringStaff: (business.hiringStaff || []).filter((hire) => hire.completesAt > Date.now()) };
}
```

### instantHireClothingStaff()

```js
function instantHireClothingStaff(business, hireId) {
  const hire = (business.hiringStaff || []).find((item) => item.id === hireId);
  if (!hire) return business;
  return { ...business, staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), makeStaffMember(hire.typeId, hire.rating)], hiringStaff: business.hiringStaff.filter((item) => item.id !== hireId) };
}
```

### fireClothingStaff()

```js
function fireClothingStaff(business, staffId) {
  const branches = getClothingOwnedStores(business).map((b) => ({
    ...b,
    assignedStaffIds: (b.assignedStaffIds || []).filter((id) => id !== staffId),
  }));
  return {
    ...business,
    branches,
    staffMembers: (business.staffMembers || []).filter((s) => s.id !== staffId),
  };
}
```

### raiseClothingStaff()

```js
function raiseClothingStaff(business, staffId) {
  const members = (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => {
    if (s.id === staffId) {
      const type = getClothingStaffType(s.typeId);
      const currentSalary = type?.salaryHourly || 0;
      return { ...s, salary: Math.round((s.salary || currentSalary) * 1.15) };
    }
    return s;
  });
  return { ...business, staffMembers: members };
}
```

### trainClothingStaff()

```js
function trainClothingStaff(business, staffId) {
  const members = (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => {
    if (s.id === staffId) return { ...s, rating: Math.min(10, (s.rating || 5) + 1) };
    return s;
  });
  return { ...business, staffMembers: members };
}
```

### isClothingStoreUnlocked()

```js
function isClothingStoreUnlocked(business, storeId) {
  const store = getClothingStore(storeId);
  if (!store) return false;
  if (!store.requiresStore) return true;
  return !!getClothingStoreById(business, store.requiresStore);
}
```

### getUnlockedClothingStores()

```js
function getUnlockedClothingStores(business) {
  return CLOTHING_STORES.filter((b) => isClothingStoreUnlocked(business, b.id));
}
```

### openClothingStore()

```js
function openClothingStore(business, storeId) {
  const store = getClothingStore(storeId);
  if (!store) return { error: 'invalid_store' };
  if (!isClothingStoreUnlocked(business, storeId)) return { error: 'locked' };
  if (getClothingStoreById(business, storeId)) return { error: 'owned' };
  return { ...business, branches: [...getClothingOwnedStores(business), { branchId: storeId, menuIds: ['tshirt', 'jeans', 'sneakers'], upgrades: {}, assignedStaffIds: [] }] };
}
```

### getAssignedStaff()

```js
function getAssignedStaff(business, storeId) {
  const branch = getClothingStoreById(business, storeId);
  if (!branch) return [];
  return (branch.assignedStaffIds || []).map((id) => (business.staffMembers || []).find((s) => s.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
}
```

### assignClothingStaff()

```js
function assignClothingStaff(business, storeId, staffId) {
  const branch = getClothingStoreById(business, storeId);
  if (!branch) return business;
  if (!(business.staffMembers || []).some((s) => s.id === staffId)) return business;
  const branches = getClothingOwnedStores(business).map((b) => {
    if (b.branchId === storeId) {
      return { ...b, assignedStaffIds: [...new Set([...(b.assignedStaffIds || []), staffId])] };
    }
    return { ...b, assignedStaffIds: (b.assignedStaffIds || []).filter((id) => id !== staffId) };
  });
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### unassignClothingStaff()

```js
function unassignClothingStaff(business, storeId, staffId) {
  const branch = getClothingStoreById(business, storeId);
  if (!branch) return business;
  const branches = getClothingOwnedStores(business).map((b) =>
    b.branchId === storeId
      ? { ...b, assignedStaffIds: (b.assignedStaffIds || []).filter((id) => id !== staffId) }
      : b
  );
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### canOpenClothingStore()

```js
function canOpenClothingStore(business, storeId) {
  const branch = getClothingStoreById(business, storeId);
  if (!branch) return { canOpen: false, reason: 'invalid_store' };
  if (branch.openedAt || branch.openingAt) return { canOpen: false, reason: 'already_open' };
  const assigned = getAssignedStaff(business, storeId);
  if (assigned.length === 0) return { canOpen: false, reason: 'no_staff' };
  const supplies = business.supplies || {};
  const hasSupplies = CLOTHING_STOCK_TYPES.some((ing) => (supplies[ing.id] || 0) > 0);
  if (!hasSupplies) return { canOpen: false, reason: 'no_supplies' };
  return { canOpen: true };
}
```

### startClothingStoreOpening()

```js
function startClothingStoreOpening(business, storeId) {
  const check = canOpenClothingStore(business, storeId);
  if (!check.canOpen) return { error: check.reason };
  const isFirstStoreEver = getClothingOwnedStores(business).every((b) => !b.openedAt);
  const openMs = isFirstStoreEver ? 5000 : STORE_OPEN_MS_MIN + Math.random() * (STORE_OPEN_MS_MAX - STORE_OPEN_MS_MIN);
  const branches = getClothingOwnedStores(business).map((b) =>
    b.branchId === storeId ? { ...b, openingAt: Date.now() + openMs } : b
  );
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### completeClothingOpenings()

```js
(business) => ({ ...business, branches: getClothingOwnedStores(business).map((branch) => branch.openingAt && branch.openingAt <= Date.now() ? { ...branch, openedAt: Date.now(), openingAt: null } : branch) })
```

### instantOpenClothingStore()

```js
function instantOpenClothingStore(business, storeId) {
  const branches = getClothingOwnedStores(business).map((b) =>
    b.branchId === storeId && b.openingAt ? { ...b, openedAt: Date.now(), openingAt: null } : b
  );
  return { ...business, branches };
}
```

### buyClothingSupplies()

```js
function buyClothingSupplies(business, stockTypeId, quantity) {
  const stockType = getClothingStockType(stockTypeId);
  if (!stockType || quantity <= 0) return null;
  const isFirstSupplyEver = (business.pendingSupplies || []).length === 0
    && Object.values(business.supplies || {}).every((v) => !v);
  const deliveryMs = isFirstSupplyEver ? 0 : SUPPLY_DELIVERY_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (SUPPLY_DELIVERY_MAX_MS - SUPPLY_DELIVERY_MIN_MS);
  const delivery = {
    id: `clothing_supply_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    stockTypeId,
    quantity,
    deliveredAt: Date.now() + deliveryMs,
  };
  return { business: { ...business, pendingSupplies: [...(business.pendingSupplies || []), delivery] }, cost: stockType.unitCost * quantity };
}
```

### getClothingSupplyDeliveryTimeRemaining()

```js
function getClothingSupplyDeliveryTimeRemaining(delivery) {
  return Math.max(0, delivery.deliveredAt - Date.now());
}
```

### getArrivedClothingSupplies()

```js
function getArrivedClothingSupplies(business) {
  const now = Date.now();
  return (business.pendingSupplies || []).filter((d) => d.deliveredAt <= now);
}
```

### completeClothingSupplies()

```js
function completeClothingSupplies(business) {
  const arrived = getArrivedClothingSupplies(business);
  if (!arrived.length) return business;
  const pending = (business.pendingSupplies || []).filter((d) => d.deliveredAt > Date.now());
  const supplies = { ...(business.supplies || {}) };
  for (const d of arrived) supplies[d.stockTypeId] = (supplies[d.stockTypeId] || 0) + d.quantity;
  return { ...business, pendingSupplies: pending, supplies };
}
```

### instantClothingSupply()

```js
function instantClothingSupply(business, deliveryId) {
  const delivery = (business.pendingSupplies || []).find((d) => d.id === deliveryId);
  if (!delivery) return business;
  const updated = { ...business, pendingSupplies: (business.pendingSupplies || []).map((d) => d.id === deliveryId ? { ...d, deliveredAt: Date.now() } : d) };
  return completeClothingSupplies(updated);
}
```

### setClothingMenu()

```js
(business, storeId, menuIds) => ({ ...business, branches: getClothingOwnedStores(business).map((branch) => branch.branchId === storeId && !branch.openingAt ? { ...branch, menuIds } : branch) })
```

### getClothingSalaryHourly()

```js
function getClothingSalaryHourly(business) {
  return getClothingStaffMembers(business).reduce((total, member) => total + (member.salary || getClothingStaffType(member.typeId)?.salaryHourly || 0), 0);
}
```

### getClothingLevelMultiplier()

```js
function getClothingLevelMultiplier(business) {
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level || 1);
  return Math.pow(1.12, level - 1);
}
```

### getClothingSalesHourly()

```js
function getClothingSalesHourly(business) {
  const levelMult = getClothingLevelMultiplier(business);
  let total = 0;
  for (const branch of getClothingOwnedStores(business)) {
    if (branch.openingAt || !branch.openedAt) continue;
    const zone = getClothingStore(branch.branchId);
    const assignedStaff = (branch.assignedStaffIds || []).map((id) => (business.staffMembers || []).find((s) => s.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
    const assignedCount = assignedStaff.length;
    const optimalStaff = getOptimalStaffForStore(branch.branchId);
    const capacityFactor = Math.min(1, assignedCount / optimalStaff);
    const managerCount = assignedStaff.filter((s) => s.typeId === 'manager').length;
    total += (branch.menuIds || []).reduce((sum, id) => {
      const item = getClothingProduct(id);
      return sum + (item ? item.price * item.demand * zone.footfall * capacityFactor * (1 + managerCount * 0.03) : 0);
    }, 0);
  }
  return total * levelMult;
}
```

### getClothingStockCostHourly()

```js
function getClothingStockCostHourly(business) {
  const levelMult = getClothingLevelMultiplier(business);
  let total = 0;
  for (const branch of getClothingOwnedStores(business)) {
    if (branch.openingAt || !branch.openedAt) continue;
    const zone = getClothingStore(branch.branchId);
    const assignedStaff = (branch.assignedStaffIds || []).map((id) => (business.staffMembers || []).find((s) => s.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
    const assignedCount = assignedStaff.length;
    const optimalStaff = getOptimalStaffForStore(branch.branchId);
    const capacityFactor = Math.min(1, assignedCount / optimalStaff);
    for (const id of branch.menuIds || []) {
      const item = getClothingProduct(id);
      if (!item) continue;
      for (const [stockTypeId, units] of Object.entries(item.stock)) total += item.demand * zone.footfall * capacityFactor * units * (getClothingStockType(stockTypeId)?.unitCost || 0);
    }
  }
  return total * levelMult;
}
```

### getClothingStoreNetIncomeHourly()

```js
function getClothingStoreNetIncomeHourly(business, storeId) {
  const branch = getClothingStoreById(business, storeId);
  if (!branch || branch.openingAt || !branch.openedAt) return 0;
  const zone = getClothingStore(storeId);
  if (!zone) return 0;
  const assignedStaff = (branch.assignedStaffIds || []).map((id) => (business.staffMembers || []).find((s) => s.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
  const assignedCount = assignedStaff.length;
  const optimalStaff = getOptimalStaffForStore(storeId);
  const capacityFactor = Math.min(1, assignedCount / optimalStaff);
  const managerCount = assignedStaff.filter((s) => s.typeId === 'manager').length;
  let revenue = 0;
  let stockCost = 0;
  for (const id of branch.menuIds || []) {
    const item = getClothingProduct(id);
    if (!item) continue;
    revenue += item.price * item.demand * zone.footfall * capacityFactor * (1 + managerCount * 0.03);
    for (const [stockTypeId, units] of Object.entries(item.stock)) {
      stockCost += item.demand * zone.footfall * capacityFactor * units * (getClothingStockType(stockTypeId)?.unitCost || 0);
    }
  }
  const payroll = assignedStaff.reduce((sum, s) => sum + (getClothingStaffType(s.typeId)?.salaryHourly || 0), 0);
  return revenue - stockCost - payroll - zone.rentHourly;
}
```

### getClothingRentHourly()

```js
function getClothingRentHourly(business) {
  return getClothingOwnedStores(business).filter((branch) => branch.openedAt && !branch.openingAt).reduce((total, branch) => total + (getClothingStore(branch.branchId)?.rentHourly || 0), 0);
}
```

### getClothingHourlyIncome()

```js
function getClothingHourlyIncome(business) {
  const hydrated = hydrateClothing(business);
  return Math.floor(getClothingSalesHourly(hydrated) - getClothingStockCostHourly(hydrated) - getClothingSalaryHourly(hydrated) - getClothingRentHourly(hydrated));
}
```

### processClothingBusiness()

```js
function processClothingBusiness(business, minutesElapsed) {
  let updated = hydrateClothing(business);
  updated = completeClothingHires(updated);
  updated = completeClothingOpenings(updated);
  updated = completeClothingSupplies(updated);
  const hours = minutesElapsed / 60;
  const stockNeeds = {};
  for (const branch of getClothingOwnedStores(updated)) {
    if (branch.openingAt || !branch.openedAt) continue;
    const zone = getClothingStore(branch.branchId);
    const assignedCount = (branch.assignedStaffIds || []).length;
    const optimalStaff = getOptimalStaffForStore(branch.branchId);
    const capacityFactor = Math.min(1, assignedCount / optimalStaff);
    for (const id of branch.menuIds || []) {
      const item = getClothingProduct(id);
      if (!item) continue;
      for (const [stockTypeId, units] of Object.entries(item.stock)) stockNeeds[stockTypeId] = (stockNeeds[stockTypeId] || 0) + item.demand * zone.footfall * capacityFactor * units * hours;
    }
  }
  const supplyFactor = Object.entries(stockNeeds).reduce((factor, [id, needed]) => needed > 0 ? Math.min(factor, (updated.supplies?.[id] || 0) / needed) : factor, 1);
  const grossSales = getClothingSalesHourly(updated) * hours * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, supplyFactor));
  const stockCost = getClothingStockCostHourly(updated) * hours * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, supplyFactor));
  const payroll = getClothingSalaryHourly(updated) * hours;
  const rent = getClothingRentHourly(updated) * hours;
  const supplies = { ...(updated.supplies || {}) };
  Object.entries(stockNeeds).forEach(([id, needed]) => { supplies[id] = Math.max(0, (supplies[id] || 0) - needed * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, supplyFactor))); });
  const suspended = isSuspended(updated);
  const income = suspended ? -(payroll + rent) : grossSales - stockCost - payroll - rent;
  return { updatedBusiness: { ...updated, supplies, allTimeEarnings: (updated.allTimeEarnings || 0) + income }, income };
}
```

### getDistrictCentroid()

```js
function getDistrictCentroid(districtId) {
  const d = CITY_MAP.districts.find((d) => d.id === districtId);
  if (!d) return null;
  const pts = d.polygon.split(' ').map((pt) => pt.split(',').map(Number));
  let cx = 0, cy = 0;
  for (const [x, y] of pts) { cx += x; cy += y; }
  return { x: cx / pts.length, y: cy / pts.length };
}
```

### getStaffMembers()

```js
function getStaffMembers(business) {
  return business.staffMembers || [];
}
```

### getStaffSalaryHourly()

```js
function getStaffSalaryHourly(business) {
  return (business.staffMembers || []).reduce((total, m) => total + (m.salary || STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY), 0);
}
```

### getAssignedStaffIds()

```js
function getAssignedStaffIds(business) {
  const activeJobs = (business.jobs || []).filter((j) => !j.completedAt);
  const assigned = new Set();
  for (const job of activeJobs) {
    for (const id of (job.assignedStaffIds || [])) assigned.add(id);
  }
  return assigned;
}
```

### getAvailableStaff()

```js
function getAvailableStaff(business) {
  const assignedIds = getAssignedStaffIds(business);
  return (business.staffMembers || []).filter((s) => !assignedIds.has(s.id));
}
```

### getJobAssignedStaff()

```js
function getJobAssignedStaff(business, jobInstanceId) {
  const job = (business.jobs || []).find((j) => j.instanceId === jobInstanceId && !j.completedAt);
  if (!job) return [];
  const ids = job.assignedStaffIds || [];
  return (business.staffMembers || []).filter((s) => ids.includes(s.id));
}
```

### getAvailableStaffCount()

```js
function getAvailableStaffCount(business) {
  return getAvailableStaff(business).length;

}
```

### getMaxZones()

```js
function getMaxZones(business) {
  const staff = (business.staffMembers || []).length;
  if (staff >= 40) return 6;
  if (staff >= 25) return 5;
  if (staff >= 15) return 4;
  if (staff >= 8) return 3;
  return 2;
}
```

### toggleZone()

```js
function toggleZone(business, zoneId) {
  const current = getSelectedZones(business);
  const max = getMaxZones(business);
  if (current.includes(zoneId)) {
    return { ...business, selectedZones: current.filter((z) => z !== zoneId) };
  }
  if (current.length >= max) return { error: 'max_zones', max };
  return { ...business, selectedZones: [...current, zoneId] };
}
```

### getDistrictCentroid()

```js
function getDistrictCentroid(districtId) {
  const d = CITY_MAP.districts.find((d) => d.id === districtId);
  if (!d) return null;
  const pts = d.polygon.split(' ').map((pt) => pt.split(',').map(Number));
  let cx = 0, cy = 0;
  for (const [x, y] of pts) { cx += x; cy += y; }
  return { x: cx / pts.length, y: cy / pts.length };
}
```

### getRandomPointInDistrict()

```js
function getRandomPointInDistrict(districtId) {
  const d = CITY_MAP.districts.find((d) => d.id === districtId);
  if (!d) return null;
  const pts = d.polygon.split(' ').map((pt) => pt.split(',').map(Number));
  // Bounding box
  let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  for (const [x, y] of pts) {
    if (x < minX) minX = x; if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
    if (y < minY) minY = y; if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
  }
  // Ray casting point-in-polygon test
  const inside = (px, py) => {
    let odd = false;
    for (let i = 0, j = pts.length - 1; i < pts.length; j = i++) {
      const [xi, yi] = pts[i];
      const [xj, yj] = pts[j];
      if (((yi > py) !== (yj > py)) && (px < (xj - xi) * (py - yi) / (yj - yi) + xi)) {
        odd = !odd;
      }
    }
    return odd;
  };
  // Try up to 50 times to find a point inside
  for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
    const px = minX + Math.random() * (maxX - minX);
    const py = minY + Math.random() * (maxY - minY);
    if (inside(px, py)) return { x: px, y: py };
  }
  // Fallback to centroid
  let cx = 0, cy = 0;
  for (const [x, y] of pts) { cx += x; cy += y; }
  return { x: cx / pts.length, y: cy / pts.length };
}
```

### generateMaterialRequirements()

```js
function generateMaterialRequirements(template) {
  const numMaterials = template.minMaterials + Math.floor(Math.random() * (template.maxMaterials - template.minMaterials + 1));
  const shuffled = [...RESOURCES].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
  const selected = shuffled.slice(0, numMaterials);

  const materialPct = 0.20 + Math.random() * 0.24; // 20%-44% of payout
  const totalBudget = template.payout * materialPct;

  const weights = selected.map(() => 0.5 + Math.random());
  const totalWeight = weights.reduce((s, w) => s + w, 0);

  const requirements = {};
  for (let i = 0; i < selected.length; i++) {
    const allocation = (totalBudget * weights[i]) / totalWeight;
    const quantity = Math.max(1, Math.round(allocation / selected[i].cost));
    requirements[selected[i].id] = quantity;
  }

  return requirements;
}
```

### generateAvailableJobs()

```js
function generateAvailableJobs(business) {
  const staff = (business.staffMembers || []).length;
  const level = business.level || 1;
  const unlockedZones = getUnlockedZones(business);
  const unlockedJobIds = new Set(JOB_TEMPLATES.filter((t) => staff >= t.staffUnlock).map((t) => t.id));

  // For each zone, find which unlocked job types can go there
  const zoneJobPool = unlockedZones.map((zone) => {
    const validJobs = zone.jobTypes.filter((jt) => unlockedJobIds.has(jt));
    return { zoneId: zone.id, jobs: validJobs };
  }).filter((z) => z.jobs.length > 0);

  if (zoneJobPool.length === 0) {
    // Fallback: just use unlocked jobs in any zone
    const fallbackJobs = JOB_TEMPLATES.filter((t) => staff >= t.staffUnlock);
    const fallbackZones = unlockedZones.length > 0 ? unlockedZones : CONSTRUCTION_ZONES.slice(0, 3);
    const picked = [...fallbackJobs].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5).slice(0, Math.min(AVAILABLE_JOBS_COUNT, fallbackJobs.length));
    return picked.map((template) => {
      const zone = fallbackZones[Math.floor(Math.random() * fallbackZones.length)];
      const pinPos = getRandomPointInDistrict(zone.id) || getDistrictCentroid(zone.id);
      return makeJob(template, zone.id, zone.payoutMult, zone.name, pinPos);
    });
  }

  // Pick up to AVAILABLE_JOBS_COUNT jobs, distributing across zones
  const result = [];
  const usedZones = [...zoneJobPool];
  while (result.length < AVAILABLE_JOBS_COUNT && usedZones.length > 0) {
    // Pick a random zone from the pool
    const zoneIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * usedZones.length);
    const zp = usedZones[zoneIdx];
    // Pick a random job type from that zone
    const typeId = zp.jobs[Math.floor(Math.random() * zp.jobs.length)];
    const template = JOB_TEMPLATES.find((t) => t.id === typeId);
    if (template) {
      const zone = getZoneById(zp.zoneId);
      const pinPos = getRandomPointInDistrict(zp.zoneId) || getDistrictCentroid(zp.zoneId);
      result.push(makeJob(template, zp.zoneId, zone.payoutMult, zone.name, pinPos));
    }
    // Remove this zone from the pool if we've used all its job types or to ensure variety
    usedZones.splice(zoneIdx, 1);
  }

  // If we still need more jobs, cycle through all zone-job combos
  if (result.length < AVAILABLE_JOBS_COUNT && zoneJobPool.length > 0) {
    const allCombos = [];
    for (const zp of zoneJobPool) {
      for (const typeId of zp.jobs) {
        allCombos.push({ zoneId: zp.zoneId, typeId });
      }
    }
    const shuffled = allCombos.sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    for (const combo of shuffled) {
      if (result.length >= AVAILABLE_JOBS_COUNT) break;
      const template = JOB_TEMPLATES.find((t) => t.id === combo.typeId);
      if (template) {
        const zone = getZoneById(combo.zoneId);
        const pinPos = getRandomPointInDistrict(combo.zoneId) || getDistrictCentroid(combo.zoneId);
        result.push(makeJob(template, combo.zoneId, zone.payoutMult, zone.name, pinPos));
      }
    }
  }

  return result;
}
```

### makeJob()

```js
function makeJob(template, zoneId, zoneMult, zoneName, pinPos) {
  return {
    instanceId: `jobopt_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    typeId: template.id,
    name: template.name,
    emoji: template.emoji,
    desc: template.desc,
    gradient: template.gradient,
    durationHours: template.durationHours,
    payout: Math.round(template.payout * zoneMult),
    staffReq: template.staffReq,
    resources: generateMaterialRequirements(template),
    zoneId,
    zoneName,
    zoneMult,
    pinX: pinPos.x,
    pinY: pinPos.y,
  };
}
```

### getConstructionLevelMultiplier()

```js
function getConstructionLevelMultiplier(business) {
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level || 1);
  return Math.pow(1.12, level - 1);
}
```

### getConstructionPendingPayout()

```js
function getConstructionPendingPayout(business) {
  const levelMult = getConstructionLevelMultiplier(business);
  const activeJobs = (business.jobs || []).filter((j) => !j.completedAt);
  return Math.floor(activeJobs.reduce((sum, j) => sum + j.payout, 0) * levelMult);
}
```

### getConstructionHourlyIncome()

```js
function getConstructionHourlyIncome(business) {
  const levelMult = getConstructionLevelMultiplier(business);
  const activeJobs = (business.jobs || []).filter((j) => !j.completedAt);
  if (activeJobs.length === 0) return 0;
  const hourlyRate = activeJobs.reduce((sum, j) => {
    const duration = j.durationHours || 1;
    return sum + (j.payout * levelMult) / duration;
  }, 0);
  const salary = getStaffSalaryHourly(business);
  return Math.floor(hourlyRate - salary);
}
```

### buyResources()

```js
function buyResources(business, resourceId, quantity) {
  const resource = getResource(resourceId);
  if (!resource || quantity <= 0) return null;
  const capacity = getStorageCapacity(business, resourceId);
  const current = (business.resources || {})[resourceId] || 0;
  const pending = (business.pendingResources || {})[resourceId] || 0;
  if (current + pending + quantity > capacity) return { error: 'storage_full', capacity, current: current + pending };
  const cost = resource.cost * quantity;
  const now = Date.now();
  const deliveryMs = MATERIAL_DELIVERY_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (MATERIAL_DELIVERY_MAX_MS - MATERIAL_DELIVERY_MIN_MS);
  const delivery = {
    instanceId: `delivery_${now}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
    resourceId,
    quantity,
    orderedAt: now,
    arrivesAt: now + deliveryMs,
  };
  const pendingResources = { ...(business.pendingResources || {}), [resourceId]: pending + quantity };
  return {
    business: { ...business, pendingResources, deliveries: [...(business.deliveries || []), delivery] },
    cost,
  };
}
```

### getArrivedDeliveries()

```js
function getArrivedDeliveries(business) {
  const now = Date.now();
  return (business.deliveries || []).filter((d) => d.arrivesAt <= now);
}
```

### processDeliveries()

```js
function processDeliveries(business) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const deliveries = business.deliveries || [];
  const arrived = deliveries.filter((d) => d.arrivesAt <= now);
  if (arrived.length === 0) return business;
  const remaining = deliveries.filter((d) => d.arrivesAt > now);

  const resources = { ...(business.resources || {}) };
  const pendingResources = { ...(business.pendingResources || {}) };

  for (const d of arrived) {
    resources[d.resourceId] = (resources[d.resourceId] || 0) + d.quantity;
    pendingResources[d.resourceId] = Math.max(0, (pendingResources[d.resourceId] || 0) - d.quantity);
  }

  return { ...business, resources, pendingResources, deliveries: remaining };
}
```

### getDeliveryTimeRemaining()

```js
function getDeliveryTimeRemaining(delivery) {
  return Math.max(0, delivery.arrivesAt - Date.now());
}
```

### startJob()

```js
function startJob(business, jobOption) {
  if (!jobOption) return null;
  const availableStaff = getAvailableStaff(business);
  if (availableStaff.length < jobOption.staffReq) return { error: 'staff', available: availableStaff.length, required: jobOption.staffReq };
  const activeJobs = (business.jobs || []).filter((j) => !j.completedAt);
  if (activeJobs.length >= MAX_ACTIVE_JOBS) return { error: 'slots' };

  const resources = { ...(business.resources || {}) };
  for (const [resId, amount] of Object.entries(jobOption.resources)) {
    if ((resources[resId] || 0) < amount) return { error: 'resources', missing: resId };
  }
  for (const [resId, amount] of Object.entries(jobOption.resources)) {
    resources[resId] = (resources[resId] || 0) - amount;
  }

  const assignedStaffIds = availableStaff.slice(0, jobOption.staffReq).map((s) => s.id);

  const now = Date.now();
  const completesAt = now + jobOption.durationHours * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  const job = {
    instanceId: `job_${now}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    typeId: jobOption.typeId,
    name: jobOption.name,
    emoji: jobOption.emoji,
    desc: jobOption.desc,
    gradient: jobOption.gradient,
    startedAt: now,
    completesAt,
    completedAt: null,
    payout: jobOption.payout,
    resources: jobOption.resources,
    durationHours: jobOption.durationHours,
    staffReq: jobOption.staffReq,
    assignedStaffIds,
  };

  const availableJobs = (business.availableJobs || []).filter((j) => j.instanceId !== jobOption.instanceId);

  return {
    business: {
      ...business,
      resources,
      jobs: [...(business.jobs || []), job],
      availableJobs,
    },
  };
}
```

### getCompletedJobs()

```js
function getCompletedJobs(business) {
  const now = Date.now();
  return (business.jobs || []).filter((j) => !j.completedAt && j.completesAt <= now);
}
```

### completeJob()

```js
function completeJob(business, jobInstanceId) {
  const jobs = business.jobs || [];
  const job = jobs.find((j) => j.instanceId === jobInstanceId && !j.completedAt);
  if (!job) return null;
  const now = Date.now();
  if (job.completesAt > now) return null;

  const updatedJobs = jobs.map((j) =>
    j.instanceId === jobInstanceId ? { ...j, completedAt: now } : j
  );

  const levelMult = getConstructionLevelMultiplier(business);
  const payout = Math.floor(job.payout * levelMult);

  return {
    business: { ...business, jobs: updatedJobs },
    payout,
    tax: Math.round(payout * 0.15),
  };
}
```

### getJobTimeRemaining()

```js
function getJobTimeRemaining(job) {
  if (!job || job.completedAt) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, job.completesAt - Date.now());
}
```

### refreshJobs()

```js
function refreshJobs(business) {
  const availableJobs = generateAvailableJobs(business);
  return { ...business, availableJobs, jobsRefreshedAt: Date.now() };
}
```

### getJobsRefreshTimeRemaining()

```js
function getJobsRefreshTimeRemaining(business) {
  const refreshedAt = business.jobsRefreshedAt || 0;
  const nextRefresh = refreshedAt + JOB_REFRESH_HOURS * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  return Math.max(0, nextRefresh - Date.now());
}
```

### hireStaff()

```js
function hireStaff(business, count) {
  if (count <= 0) return null;
  const now = Date.now();
  const hiringStaff = [...(business.hiringStaff || [])];
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const duration = STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (STAFF_HIRE_MAX_MS - STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS);
    const member = makeStaffMember();
    hiringStaff.push({
      instanceId: `hire_${now}_${i}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
      completesAt: now + duration,
      staffMember: member,
    });
  }
  return { ...business, hiringStaff };
}
```

### instantHireStaff()

```js
function instantHireStaff(business, hireId) {
  const hiringStaff = (business.hiringStaff || []).filter((h) => h.instanceId !== hireId);
  let staffMembers = business.staffMembers || [];
  if (staffMembers.length === 0 && (business.staff || 0) > 0) {
    staffMembers = Array.from({ length: business.staff }, () => makeStaffMember());
  }
  return { ...business, staff: (business.staff || 0) + 1, staffMembers: [...staffMembers, makeStaffMember()], hiringStaff };
}
```

### completeHiredStaff()

```js
function completeHiredStaff(business) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const completed = (business.hiringStaff || []).filter((h) => h.completesAt <= now);
  if (completed.length === 0) return business;
  const remaining = (business.hiringStaff || []).filter((h) => h.completesAt > now);
  const newMembers = completed.map((h) => h.staffMember || makeStaffMember());
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (business.staff || 0) + completed.length,
    staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), ...newMembers],
    hiringStaff: remaining,
  };
}
```

### processConstruction()

```js
function processConstruction(business, minutesElapsed) {
  const hoursFraction = minutesElapsed / 60;
  const levelMult = getConstructionLevelMultiplier(business);
  let income = 0;

  let updatedBusiness = processDeliveries(business);
  updatedBusiness = completeHiredStaff(updatedBusiness);

  const suspended = isSuspended(updatedBusiness);
  const now = Date.now();
  const jobs = (updatedBusiness.jobs || []).map((j) => {
    if (!j.completedAt && j.completesAt <= now) {
      if (!suspended) income += Math.floor(j.payout * levelMult);
      return { ...j, completedAt: now };
    }
    return j;
  });
  updatedBusiness = { ...updatedBusiness, jobs };

  const salary = getStaffSalaryHourly(updatedBusiness) * hoursFraction;
  income -= salary;

  return { updatedBusiness, income };
}
```

### generateDriver()

```js
function generateDriver() {
  const person = generatePerson();
  return { name: person.name, gender: person.gender, age: person.age };
}
```

### migrateCourierBusiness()

```js
function migrateCourierBusiness(business) {
  if (business.courierMigrated) return business;

  const migrated = { ...business };

  if (typeof business.staff === 'number' && !business.staffMembers) {
    migrated.staffMembers = Array.from({ length: business.staff }, (_, i) => ({
      id: `courier_driver_migrated_${i}_${Date.now()}`,
      ...generateDriver(),
      hiredAt: Date.now(),
    }));
    delete migrated.staff;
  }

  if (business.hiringStaff) {
    migrated.hiringStaff = business.hiringStaff.map((h) => ({
      ...h,
      ...(h.name ? { name: h.name } : generateDriver()),
    }));
  }

  if (business.fleet) {
    migrated.fleet = business.fleet.map((car) => {
      const carType = getCarType(car.typeId);
      const { assignedZone, ...rest } = car;
      return {
        ...rest,
        assignedZones: car.assignedZones || (assignedZone ? [assignedZone] : []),
        activeJobType: car.activeJobType || (carType ? carType.jobTypes[0] : 'food'),
        assignedDriverId: car.assignedDriverId || null,
        insured: car.insured || false,
      };
    });
  }

  if (!migrated.ownedDistricts) {
    migrated.ownedDistricts = [];
  }

  migrated.courierMigrated = true;
  return migrated;
}
```

### getCarLevelMultiplier()

```js
function getCarLevelMultiplier(business) {
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level || 1);
  return Math.pow(1.12, level - 1);
}
```

```js
}
```

### isDistrictOwned()

```js
function isDistrictOwned(business, districtId) {
```

### isDistrictUnlocked()

```js
function isDistrictUnlocked(business, districtId) {
  const plot = DISTRICT_PLOTS[districtId];
  if (!plot) return false;
  const fleetSize = (business.fleet || []).length;
  const driverCount = (business.staffMembers || []).length;
  const earnings = business.allTimeEarnings || 0;
  return fleetSize >= plot.unlockFleet && driverCount >= plot.unlockDrivers && earnings >= plot.unlockEarnings;
}
```

### getDistrictUnlockStatus()

```js
function getDistrictUnlockStatus(business, districtId) {
  const plot = DISTRICT_PLOTS[districtId];
  if (!plot) return { unlocked: false, owned: false, reasons: [] };
  const owned = isDistrictOwned(business, districtId);
  if (owned) return { unlocked: true, owned: true, reasons: [] };
  const fleetSize = (business.fleet || []).length;
  const driverCount = (business.staffMembers || []).length;
  const earnings = business.allTimeEarnings || 0;
  const reasons = [];
  if (fleetSize < plot.unlockFleet) reasons.push(`${fleetSize}/${plot.unlockFleet} vehicles`);
  if (driverCount < plot.unlockDrivers) reasons.push(`${driverCount}/${plot.unlockDrivers} drivers`);
  if (earnings < plot.unlockEarnings) reasons.push(`$${earnings.toLocaleString()}/$${plot.unlockEarnings.toLocaleString()} earned`);
  return { unlocked: reasons.length === 0, owned: false, reasons };
}
```

### buyDistrict()

```js
function buyDistrict(business, districtId) {
  if (isDistrictOwned(business, districtId)) return null;
  const plot = DISTRICT_PLOTS[districtId];
  if (!plot) return null;
  if (!isDistrictUnlocked(business, districtId)) return null;
  return {
    ...business,
    ownedDistricts: [...getOwnedDistricts(business), districtId],
    capitalisation: (business.capitalisation || 0) + plot.cost,
  };
}
```

```js

  if (!type.requiresDistrict) return true;

}
```

### buyCar()

```js
function buyCar(business, typeId) {
  const type = getCarType(typeId);
  if (!type) return null;
  const car = {
    instanceId: `${typeId}_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    typeId,
    name: type.name,
    cost: type.cost,
    baseHourlyIncome: type.baseHourlyIncome,
    maxMiles: type.maxMiles,
    milesRemaining: type.maxMiles,
    color: type.color,
    emoji: type.emoji,
    needsMaintenance: false,
    assignedDriverId: null,
    assignedZones: [],
    activeJobType: type.jobTypes[0],
    insured: false,
    deliveredAt: Date.now() + CAR_DELIVERY_HOURS * 60 * 60 * 1000,
  };
  return {
    ...business,
    fleet: [...(business.fleet || []), car],
    capitalisation: (business.capitalisation || 0) + type.cost,
  };
}
```

### getMaintenanceCost()

```js
function getMaintenanceCost(car) {
  const milesPct = car.maxMiles > 0 ? car.milesRemaining / car.maxMiles : 0;
  const scrapRisk = Math.max(0, 0.3 * (1 - milesPct));
  return Math.floor((car.cost || 0) * (0.08 + scrapRisk * 0.12));
}
```

### maintainCar()

```js
function maintainCar(business, instanceId) {
  const car = (business.fleet || []).find((c) => c.instanceId === instanceId);
  if (!car) return { business, refund: 0, scrapped: false };
  const milesPct = car.maxMiles > 0 ? car.milesRemaining / car.maxMiles : 0;
  const scrapChance = Math.max(0, 0.3 * (1 - milesPct));

  if (Math.random() < scrapChance) {
    const refund = Math.floor((car.cost || 0) * (car.insured ? INSURANCE_SCRAP_REFUND_RATE : 0.1));
    return {
      business: {
        ...business,
        fleet: business.fleet.filter((c) => c.instanceId !== instanceId),
        capitalisation: Math.max(0, (business.capitalisation || 0) - (car.cost || 0)),
      },
      refund,
      scrapped: true,
    };
  }

  return {
    business: {
      ...business,
      fleet: business.fleet.map((c) =>
        c.instanceId === instanceId
          ? { ...c, milesRemaining: c.maxMiles, needsMaintenance: false }
          : c
      ),
    },
    refund: 0,
    scrapped: false,
  };
}
```

### hireDrivers()

```js
function hireDrivers(business, count) {
  const current = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const availableSlots = MAX_HIRING_AT_ONCE - current.length;
  const toHire = Math.min(count, availableSlots);
  if (toHire <= 0) return null;
  const isFirstHireEver = (business.staffMembers || []).length === 0 && current.length === 0;
  const now = Date.now();
  const cost = toHire * STAFF_HIRE_COST;
  const newHires = Array.from({ length: toHire }, (_, i) => {
    const duration = STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (STAFF_HIRE_MAX_MS - STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS);
    return {
      id: `courier_driver_${now}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}_${i}`,
      ...generateDriver(),
      completesAt: isFirstHireEver && i === 0 ? now : now + duration,
    };
  });
  return {
    ...business,
    hiringStaff: [...current, ...newHires],
    capitalisation: (business.capitalisation || 0) + cost,
  };
}
```

### completeHiredDrivers()

```js
function completeHiredDrivers(business) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const completed = hiring.filter((h) => h.completesAt <= now);
  if (completed.length === 0) return business;
  const remaining = hiring.filter((h) => h.completesAt > now);
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (business.staff || 0) + completed.length,
    staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), ...completed.map((h) => ({ id: h.id, name: h.name, gender: h.gender, age: h.age, skill: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) + 3, salary: STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY, hiredAt: now }))],
    hiringStaff: remaining,
  };
}
```

```js
function instantHireDriver(business, hireId) {
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const target = hiring.find((h) => h.id === hireId);
  if (!target) return business;
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (business.staff || 0) + 1,
    staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), { id: target.id, name: target.name, gender: target.gender, age: target.age, skill: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) + 3, salary: STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY, hiredAt: Date.now() }],
    hiringStaff: hiring.filter((h) => h.id !== hireId),
  };
}
```

```js
}
```

### assignCarDriver()

```js
function assignCarDriver(business, carInstanceId, driverId) {
  const driverExists = (business.staffMembers || []).some((s) => s.id === driverId);
  if (!driverExists) return business;
  const fleet = (business.fleet || []).map((c) => {
    if (c.instanceId === carInstanceId) return { ...c, assignedDriverId: driverId };
    if (c.assignedDriverId === driverId) return { ...c, assignedDriverId: null };
    return c;
  });
  return { ...business, fleet };
}
```

### unassignCarDriver()

```js
function unassignCarDriver(business, carInstanceId) {

  );
  return { ...business, fleet };
}
```

### fireDriver()

```js
    c.assignedDriverId === driverId ? { ...c, assignedDriverId: null } : c
  );

}
```

```js
}
```

### buyCarInsurance()

```js
function buyCarInsurance(business, instanceId) {

  if (!car || !canInsureCar(car)) return business;
  const fleet = business.fleet.map((c) => (c.instanceId === instanceId ? { ...c, insured: true } : c));

}
```

### getCarAssignedZones()

```js
function getCarAssignedZones(car) {
  return car.assignedZones || [];
}
```

### canAddZone()

```js
function canAddZone(business, newZoneId, currentZoneIds) {
  if (!isDistrictOwned(business, newZoneId)) return false;
  if (currentZoneIds.includes(newZoneId)) return false;
  if (currentZoneIds.length === 0) return true;
  const newSeg = getDistrictById(newZoneId);
  if (!newSeg) return false;
  for (const segId of currentZoneIds) {
    if (newSeg.adjacent.includes(segId)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}
```

### areZonesConnected()

```js
function areZonesConnected(zoneIds) {
  if (zoneIds.length <= 1) return true;
  const visited = new Set();
  const queue = [zoneIds[0]];
  visited.add(zoneIds[0]);
  while (queue.length > 0) {
    const id = queue.shift();
    const d = getDistrictById(id);
    if (!d) continue;
    for (const adj of d.adjacent) {
      if (zoneIds.includes(adj) && !visited.has(adj)) {
        visited.add(adj);
        queue.push(adj);
      }
    }
  }
  return visited.size === zoneIds.length;
}
```

### canRemoveZone()

```js
function canRemoveZone(zoneId, currentZoneIds) {
  const remaining = currentZoneIds.filter((id) => id !== zoneId);
  return areZonesConnected(remaining);
}
```

### toggleCarZone()

```js
function toggleCarZone(business, instanceId, zoneId) {
  const car = (business.fleet || []).find((c) => c.instanceId === instanceId);
  if (!car) return business;
  const carType = getCarType(car.typeId);
  if (!carType) return business;
  const current = getCarAssignedZones(car);
  if (current.includes(zoneId)) {
    if (!canRemoveZone(zoneId, current)) return business;
    return {
      ...business,
      fleet: business.fleet.map((c) =>
        c.instanceId === instanceId ? { ...c, assignedZones: current.filter((z) => z !== zoneId) } : c
      ),
    };
  }
  if (current.length >= carType.maxZones) return business;
  if (!canAddZone(business, zoneId, current)) return business;
  return {
    ...business,
    fleet: business.fleet.map((c) =>
      c.instanceId === instanceId ? { ...c, assignedZones: [...current, zoneId] } : c
    ),
  };
}
```

### setCarJobType()

```js
function setCarJobType(business, instanceId, jobType) {
  const car = (business.fleet || []).find((c) => c.instanceId === instanceId);
  if (!car) return business;
  const carType = getCarType(car.typeId);
  if (!carType || !carType.jobTypes.includes(jobType)) return business;
  return {
    ...business,
    fleet: business.fleet.map((c) =>
      c.instanceId === instanceId ? { ...c, activeJobType: jobType } : c
    ),
  };
}
```

### getCarActualIncomeHourly()

```js
function getCarActualIncomeHourly(car, business) {
  if (!isCarDelivered(car)) return 0;
  if (car.milesRemaining <= 0 || car.needsMaintenance) return 0;
  if (!car.assignedDriverId) return 0;
  const carType = getCarType(car.typeId);
  if (!carType) return 0;
  const jobType = car.activeJobType || carType.jobTypes[0];
  const job = getJobType(jobType);
  if (!job) return 0;
  const zones = getCarAssignedZones(car);
  if (zones.length === 0) return 0;
  const levelMult = getCarLevelMultiplier(business);
  let carIncome = 0;
  for (const zoneId of zones) {
    const zoneMult = ZONE_JOB_MULTIPLIERS[zoneId]?.[jobType] || 1.0;
    carIncome += carType.baseHourlyIncome * job.incomeMultiplier * zoneMult;
  }
  return Math.floor(carIncome * levelMult);
}
```

### getCarRevenueHourly()

```js
function getCarRevenueHourly(business) {
  const fleet = business.fleet || [];
  if (fleet.length === 0) return 0;
  const levelMult = getCarLevelMultiplier(business);
  let total = 0;

  for (const car of fleet) {
    if (!isCarDelivered(car)) continue;
    if (car.milesRemaining <= 0 || car.needsMaintenance) continue;
    if (!car.assignedDriverId) continue;
    const carType = getCarType(car.typeId);
    if (!carType) continue;
    const jobType = car.activeJobType || carType.jobTypes[0];
    const job = getJobType(jobType);
    if (!job) continue;
    const zones = getCarAssignedZones(car);
    if (zones.length === 0) continue;

    let carIncome = 0;
    for (const zoneId of zones) {
      const zoneMult = ZONE_JOB_MULTIPLIERS[zoneId]?.[jobType] || 1.0;
      carIncome += carType.baseHourlyIncome * job.incomeMultiplier * zoneMult;
    }
    total += carIncome;
  }

  return Math.floor(total * levelMult);
}
```

### getCarPayrollHourly()

```js
function getCarPayrollHourly(business) {
  return (business.staffMembers || []).reduce((total, m) => total + (m.salary || STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY), 0);
}
```

### getCarInsuranceCostHourly()

```js
function getCarInsuranceCostHourly(business) {
  const fleet = business.fleet || [];
  let total = 0;
  for (const car of fleet) {
    if (car.insured) total += ((car.cost || 0) * INSURANCE_DAILY_RATE) / 24;
  }
  return total;
}
```

### getCarHourlyIncome()

```js
function getCarHourlyIncome(business) {
  const revenue = getCarRevenueHourly(business);
  const payroll = getCarPayrollHourly(business);
  const insurance = getCarInsuranceCostHourly(business);
  return Math.floor(revenue - payroll - insurance);
}
```

### processCourierFleet()

```js
function processCourierFleet(business, minutesElapsed) {
  business = migrateCourierBusiness(business);
  business = completeHiredDrivers(business);

  const fleet = business.fleet || [];
  const hoursFraction = minutesElapsed / 60;
  const levelMult = getCarLevelMultiplier(business);

  const suspended = isSuspended(business);

  if (fleet.length === 0) {
    const payroll = getCarPayrollHourly(business) * hoursFraction;
    return {
      updatedBusiness: { ...business, allTimeEarnings: (business.allTimeEarnings || 0) - payroll },
      income: -payroll,
    };
  }

  let income = 0;
  const updatedFleet = fleet.map((car) => {
    if (!isCarDelivered(car)) return car;
    if (car.milesRemaining <= 0 || car.needsMaintenance) return car;
    if (!car.assignedDriverId) return car;

    const carType = getCarType(car.typeId);
    if (!carType) return car;

    const jobType = car.activeJobType || carType.jobTypes[0];
    const job = getJobType(jobType);
    if (!job) return car;

    const zones = getCarAssignedZones(car);
    if (zones.length === 0) return car;

    let carIncomePerHour = 0;
    for (const zoneId of zones) {
      const zoneMult = ZONE_JOB_MULTIPLIERS[zoneId]?.[jobType] || 1.0;
      carIncomePerHour += carType.baseHourlyIncome * job.incomeMultiplier * zoneMult;
    }

    const milesPerHour = job.milesPerJob * job.jobsPerHour;
    const milesPerMinute = milesPerHour / 60;
    const milesToUse = milesPerMinute * minutesElapsed;

    let earned = 0;
    let miles = car.milesRemaining;

    if (miles >= milesToUse) {
      earned = suspended ? 0 : carIncomePerHour * hoursFraction;
      miles = miles - milesToUse;
    } else {
      const availableMinutes = miles / milesPerMinute;
      earned = suspended ? 0 : carIncomePerHour * (availableMinutes / 60);
      miles = 0;
    }

    income += earned * levelMult;
    return {
      ...car,
      milesRemaining: miles,
      needsMaintenance: miles <= 0 ? true : car.needsMaintenance,
    };
  });

  const payroll = getCarPayrollHourly(business) * hoursFraction;
  const insurance = getCarInsuranceCostHourly(business) * hoursFraction;
  income -= (payroll + insurance);

  const updatedBusiness = {
    ...business,
    fleet: updatedFleet,
    allTimeEarnings: (business.allTimeEarnings || 0) + income,
  };

  return { updatedBusiness, income };
}
```

### formatDeliveryTime()

```js
function formatDeliveryTime(ms) {
  if (ms <= 0) return 'Delivered';
  const totalMins = Math.ceil(ms / 60000);
  const h = Math.floor(totalMins / 60);
  const m = totalMins % 60;
  if (h > 0) return `${h}h ${m}m`;
  return `${m}m`;
}
```

### makeStaffMember()

```js
function makeStaffMember() {
  const person = generatePerson();
  return {
    id: `staff_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    name: person.name,
    gender: person.gender,
    age: person.age,
    skill: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) + 3,
    salary: STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY,
    hiredAt: Date.now(),
  };
}
```

### fireStaff()

```js
function fireStaff(business, staffId) {
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: Math.max(0, (business.staff || 0) - 1),
    staffMembers: (business.staffMembers || []).filter((s) => s.id !== staffId),
  };
}
```

### raiseStaff()

```js
  });

}
```

### getMaxLotCapacity()


### hireStaff()

```js
function hireStaff(business) {
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  if (hiring.length >= MAX_HIRING_AT_ONCE) return { error: 'max_hiring' };
  const duration = STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (STAFF_HIRE_MAX_MS - STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS);
  const completesAt = Date.now() + duration;
  return {
    ...business,
    hiringStaff: [...hiring, { instanceId: `hire_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`, completesAt }],
  };
}
```

### completeHiredStaff()

```js
function completeHiredStaff(business) {
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const completed = hiring.filter((h) => h.completesAt <= Date.now());
  if (completed.length === 0) return business;
  let staffMembers = business.staffMembers || [];
  if (staffMembers.length === 0 && (business.staff || 0) > 0) {
    staffMembers = Array.from({ length: business.staff }, () => makeStaffMember());
  }
  const newMembers = completed.map(() => makeStaffMember());
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (business.staff || 0) + completed.length,
    staffMembers: [...staffMembers, ...newMembers],
    hiringStaff: hiring.filter((h) => h.completesAt > Date.now()),
  };
}
```

### instantHireStaff()

```js
function instantHireStaff(business, hireId) {
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const hire = hiring.find((h) => h.instanceId === hireId);
  if (!hire) return business;
  let staffMembers = business.staffMembers || [];
  if (staffMembers.length === 0 && (business.staff || 0) > 0) {
    staffMembers = Array.from({ length: business.staff }, () => makeStaffMember());
  }
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (business.staff || 0) + 1,
    staffMembers: [...staffMembers, makeStaffMember()],
    hiringStaff: hiring.filter((h) => h.instanceId !== hireId),
  };
}
```

### processDeliveries()

```js
function processDeliveries(business) {
  const arrived = getArrivedDeliveries(business);
  if (arrived.length === 0) return business;
  const pending = (business.pendingDeliveries || []).filter((d) => d.deliveredAt > Date.now());
  let dealerships = [...(business.dealerships || [])];
  for (const d of arrived) {
    const idx = dealerships.findIndex((dp) => dp.zoneId === d.zoneId);
    if (idx >= 0) {
      const zone = getDealershipZone(d.zoneId);
      const type = getVehicleType(d.typeId);
      const isPopular = zone && type && zone.popularVehicles.includes(type.id);
      const popularityMult = isPopular ? 2.0 : 1.0;
      const sellSpeedMult = 1 + (Math.max(1, business.level || 1) - 1) * 0.015;
      const footfall = zone ? zone.footfall : 1.0;
      const sellHours = type ? type.sellHours : 4;
      const sellMs = sellHours * 60 * 60 * 1000 / (footfall * popularityMult * sellSpeedMult);
      dealerships[idx] = {
        ...dealerships[idx],
        inventory: [...(dealerships[idx].inventory || []), {
          instanceId: d.instanceId,
          typeId: d.typeId,
          name: d.name,
          emoji: d.emoji,
          color: d.color,
          wholesale: d.wholesale,
          baseRetail: d.retail,
          retail: d.retail,
          upgrades: 0,
          listedAt: null,
          sellsAt: null,
        }],
      };
    }
  }
  return { ...business, pendingDeliveries: pending, dealerships };
}
```

### instantDelivery()

```js
function instantDelivery(business, deliveryId) {
  const delivery = (business.pendingDeliveries || []).find((d) => d.instanceId === deliveryId);
  if (!delivery) return business;
  const updated = {
    ...business,
    pendingDeliveries: (business.pendingDeliveries || []).map((d) =>
      d.instanceId === deliveryId ? { ...d, deliveredAt: Date.now() } : d
    ),
  };
  return processDeliveries(updated);
}
```

### buyVehicle()

```js
function buyVehicle(business, typeId, zoneId) {
  const type = getVehicleType(typeId);
  if (!type) return { error: 'invalid_type' };
  const level = business.level || 1;
  if (level < type.levelUnlock) return { error: 'level_locked' };
  if (type.needsBusLicense && !hasBusLicense(business)) return { error: 'no_license' };
  const dealership = getDealershipByZone(business, zoneId);
  if (!dealership) return { error: 'no_dealership' };
  const cap = getMaxLotCapacity(zoneId, business);
  const currentInv = (dealership.inventory || []).length;
  const pending = (business.pendingDeliveries || []).filter((d) => d.zoneId === zoneId).length;
  if (currentInv + pending >= cap) return { error: 'lot_full', capacity: cap };
  const deliveryMs = VEHICLE_DELIVERY_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (VEHICLE_DELIVERY_MAX_MS - VEHICLE_DELIVERY_MIN_MS);
  const delivery = {
    instanceId: `delivery_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
    typeId,
    name: type.name,
    emoji: type.emoji,
    color: type.color,
    wholesale: type.wholesale,
    retail: type.retail,
    zoneId,
    deliveredAt: Date.now() + deliveryMs,
  };
  return {
    ...business,
    pendingDeliveries: [...(business.pendingDeliveries || []), delivery],
    capitalisation: (business.capitalisation || 0) + type.wholesale,
    cost: type.wholesale,
  };
}
```

### openDealership()

```js
function openDealership(business, zoneId) {
  const zone = getDealershipZone(zoneId);
  if (!zone) return { error: 'invalid_zone' };
  const level = business.level || 1;
  if (level < zone.levelUnlock) return { error: 'level_locked' };
  const existing = getDealershipByZone(business, zoneId);
  if (existing) return { error: 'already_owned' };
  const dealership = {
    zoneId,
    name: `${zone.name} Dealership`,
    inventory: [],
    openedAt: Date.now(),
    openingAt: Date.now() + DEALERSHIP_OPEN_MS_MIN + Math.random() * (DEALERSHIP_OPEN_MS_MAX - DEALERSHIP_OPEN_MS_MIN),
  };
  return {
    ...business,
    dealerships: [...(business.dealerships || []), dealership],
    capitalisation: (business.capitalisation || 0) + zone.openCost,
    cost: zone.openCost,
  };
}
```

```js
function instantOpenDealership(business, zoneId) {
  const dealerships = (business.dealerships || []).map((dp) => {
    if (dp.zoneId === zoneId && dp.openingAt) {
      const { openingAt, ...rest } = dp;
      return rest;
    }
    return dp;
  });
  return { ...business, dealerships };
}
```

### listVehicleForSale()

```js
function listVehicleForSale(dealership, vehicle, business) {
  const zone = getDealershipZone(dealership.zoneId);
  if (!zone) return vehicle;
  const sellSpeedMult = 1 + (Math.max(1, business.level || 1) - 1) * 0.015;
  const sellMs = (vehicle.sellHours || 4) * 60 * 60 * 1000 / (zone.footfall * sellSpeedMult);
  return { ...vehicle, listedAt: Date.now(), sellsAt: Date.now() + sellMs };
}
```

### upgradeVehicle()

```js
function upgradeVehicle(business, zoneId, vehicleId) {
  let cost = 0;
  const dealerships = (business.dealerships || []).map((dp) => {
    if (dp.zoneId !== zoneId) return dp;
    const vehicle = (dp.inventory || []).find((v) => v.instanceId === vehicleId);
    if (!vehicle) return dp;
    if ((vehicle.upgrades || 0) >= VEHICLE_MAX_UPGRADES) return dp;
    if (vehicle.sellsAt) return dp;
    cost = getVehicleUpgradeCost(vehicle, vehicle.upgrades || 0);
    const newUpgrades = (vehicle.upgrades || 0) + 1;
    const base = vehicle.baseRetail || vehicle.retail;
    const newRetail = Math.round(base * (1 + newUpgrades * 0.30));
    return {
      ...dp,
      inventory: (dp.inventory || []).map((v) =>
        v.instanceId === vehicleId
          ? { ...v, baseRetail: base, upgrades: newUpgrades, retail: newRetail }
          : v
      ),
    };
  });
  return { ...business, dealerships, cost };
}
```

### sellVehicle()

```js
function sellVehicle(business, zoneId, vehicleId) {
  const dealerships = (business.dealerships || []).map((dp) => {
    if (dp.zoneId !== zoneId) return dp;
    const vehicle = (dp.inventory || []).find((v) => v.instanceId === vehicleId);
    if (!vehicle) return dp;
    if (vehicle.sellsAt) return dp;
    const updated = listVehicleForSale(dp, vehicle, business);
    return {
      ...dp,
      inventory: (dp.inventory || []).map((v) =>
        v.instanceId === vehicleId ? updated : v
      ),
    };
  });
  return { ...business, dealerships };
}
```

### instantSellVehicle()

```js
function instantSellVehicle(business, zoneId, vehicleId) {
  let soldVehicle = null;
  const dealerships = (business.dealerships || []).map((dp) => {
    if (dp.zoneId !== zoneId) return dp;
    const vehicle = (dp.inventory || []).find((v) => v.instanceId === vehicleId);
    if (!vehicle) return dp;
    soldVehicle = vehicle;
    const profit = vehicle.retail - vehicle.wholesale;
    const tax = Math.round(profit * DEALERSHIP_TAX_RATE);
    return {
      ...dp,
      inventory: (dp.inventory || []).filter((v) => v.instanceId !== vehicleId),
      soldHistory: [...(dp.soldHistory || []), { ...vehicle, soldAt: Date.now(), tax }].slice(-50),
    };
  });
  if (!soldVehicle) return business;
  const profit = soldVehicle.retail - soldVehicle.wholesale;
  const tax = Math.round(profit * DEALERSHIP_TAX_RATE);
  const netRevenue = soldVehicle.retail - tax;
  return {
    ...business,
    dealerships,
    allTimeEarnings: (business.allTimeEarnings || 0) + netRevenue,
    completedSales: (business.completedSales || 0) + 1,
  };
}
```

### processDealership()

```js
function processDealership(business, minutesElapsed) {
  const dealerships = business.dealerships || [];
  if (dealerships.length === 0) {
    const salary = getStaffSalaryHourly(business);
    return { updatedBusiness: business, income: -salary * (minutesElapsed / 60) };
  }
  const now = Date.now();
  let totalRevenue = 0;
  let totalTax = 0;
  const level = business.level || 1;
  let newSalesCount = 0;
  const suspended = isSuspended(business);
  const updatedDealerships = dealerships.map((dp) => {
    if (!isDealershipOpen(dp)) return dp;
    const zone = getDealershipZone(dp.zoneId);
    if (!zone) return dp;
    const remainingInv = [];
    const soldLog = [];
    for (const v of (dp.inventory || [])) {
      if (v.sellsAt && v.sellsAt <= now) {
        const profit = v.retail - v.wholesale;
        const tax = Math.round(profit * DEALERSHIP_TAX_RATE);
        if (!suspended) {
          totalRevenue += v.retail - tax;
        }
        totalTax += tax;
        soldLog.push({ ...v, soldAt: now, tax });
        newSalesCount++;
      } else {
        remainingInv.push(v);
      }
    }
    return {
      ...dp,
      inventory: remainingInv,
      soldHistory: [...(dp.soldHistory || []), ...soldLog].slice(-50),
    };
  });
  const salary = getStaffSalaryHourly(business);
  const netIncome = totalRevenue - salary * (minutesElapsed / 60);
  const updatedBusiness = {
    ...business,
    dealerships: updatedDealerships,
    allTimeEarnings: (business.allTimeEarnings || 0) + netIncome,
    completedSales: (business.completedSales || 0) + newSalesCount,
  };
  return { updatedBusiness, income: netIncome, tax: totalTax };
}
```

### getDealershipLevelMultiplier()

```js
function getDealershipLevelMultiplier(business) {
  return Math.pow(1.12, Math.max(1, business.level || 1) - 1);
}
```

### getDealershipBranchProfitHourly()

```js
function getDealershipBranchProfitHourly(dp, business) {
  if (!isDealershipOpen(dp)) return 0;
  const zone = getDealershipZone(dp.zoneId);
  if (!zone) return 0;
  const level = business.level || 1;
  const levelMult = getDealershipLevelMultiplier(business);
  let total = 0;
  for (const v of (dp.inventory || [])) {
    if (!v.sellsAt) continue;
    const type = getVehicleType(v.typeId);
    if (!type) continue;
    const sellSpeedMult = 1 + (Math.max(1, level) - 1) * 0.015;
    const effectiveSellHours = type.sellHours / (zone.footfall * sellSpeedMult);
    const profitPerHour = (v.retail - v.wholesale) / effectiveSellHours;
    total += profitPerHour * levelMult;
  }
  return total;
}
```

### getDealershipProfitHourly()

```js
function getDealershipProfitHourly(business) {
  const dealerships = business.dealerships || [];
  if (dealerships.length === 0) return 0;
  let total = 0;
  for (const dp of dealerships) {
    total += getDealershipBranchProfitHourly(dp, business);
  }
  return total;
}
```

### getDealershipHourlyIncome()

```js
function getDealershipHourlyIncome(business) {
  const total = getDealershipProfitHourly(business);
  const salary = getStaffSalaryHourly(business);
  return Math.floor(total - salary);
}
```

### getDealershipPendingSales()

```js
function getDealershipPendingSales(business) {
  const dealerships = business.dealerships || [];
  let total = 0;
  for (const dp of dealerships) {
    for (const v of (dp.inventory || [])) {
      if (v.sellsAt) {
        const profit = v.retail - v.wholesale;
        const tax = Math.round(profit * DEALERSHIP_TAX_RATE);
        total += v.retail - tax;
      }
    }
  }
  return total;
}
```

### getAllCompletedSales()

```js
function getAllCompletedSales(business) {
  const dealerships = business.dealerships || [];
  const all = [];
  for (const dp of dealerships) {
    for (const sale of (dp.soldHistory || [])) {
      all.push({ ...sale, zoneId: dp.zoneId, zoneName: getDealershipZone(dp.zoneId)?.name || '' });
    }
  }
  return all.sort((a, b) => (b.soldAt || 0) - (a.soldAt || 0));
}
```

### getRandomPointInDistrict()

```js
function getRandomPointInDistrict(districtId) {
  const d = CITY_MAP.districts.find((d) => d.id === districtId);
  if (!d) return null;
  const pts = d.polygon.split(' ').map((pt) => pt.split(',').map(Number));
  let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  for (const [x, y] of pts) {
    if (x < minX) minX = x; if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
    if (y < minY) minY = y; if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
  }
  const inside = (px, py) => {
    let odd = false;
    for (let i = 0, j = pts.length - 1; i < pts.length; j = i++) {
      const [xi, yi] = pts[i];
      const [xj, yj] = pts[j];
      if (((yi > py) !== (yj > py)) && (px < (xj - xi) * (py - yi) / (yj - yi) + xi)) {
        odd = !odd;
      }
    }
    return odd;
  };
  for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
    const px = minX + Math.random() * (maxX - minX);
    const py = minY + Math.random() * (maxY - minY);
    if (inside(px, py)) return { x: px, y: py };
  }
  let cx = 0, cy = 0;
  for (const [x, y] of pts) { cx += x; cy += y; }
  return { x: cx / pts.length, y: cy / pts.length };
}
```

### getDistrictCentroid()

```js
function getDistrictCentroid(districtId) {
  const d = CITY_MAP.districts.find((d) => d.id === districtId);
  if (!d) return null;
  const pts = d.polygon.split(' ').map((pt) => pt.split(',').map(Number));
  let cx = 0, cy = 0;
  for (const [x, y] of pts) { cx += x; cy += y; }
  return { x: cx / pts.length, y: cy / pts.length };
}
```
