### calculateDevTimeMs()

```js
function calculateDevTimeMs(sizeId, genreIds, platformIds) {
  const size = getGameSize(sizeId);
  if (!size) return 0;
  let devHours = size.devHours;
  const extraGenres = Math.max(0, (genreIds || []).length - 1);
  const extraPlatforms = Math.max(0, (platformIds || []).length - 1);
  devHours *= (1 + extraGenres * MULTI_GENRE_TIME_PENALTY + extraPlatforms * MULTI_PLATFORM_TIME_PENALTY);
  return devHours * 60 * 60 * 1000;
}
```

### getLevelMultiplier()

```js
function getLevelMultiplier(business) {
  return Math.pow(1.12, Math.max(1, business.level || 1) - 1);
}
```

### getMaxGameSlots()

```js
function getMaxGameSlots(business) {
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level || 1);
  return Math.min(MAX_ACTIVE_GAMES, 1 + Math.floor((level - 1) / 6));
}
```

### getMaxContractSlots()

```js
function getMaxContractSlots(business) {
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level || 1);
  return Math.min(MAX_ACTIVE_CONTRACTS, 1 + Math.floor((level - 1) / 10));
}
```

### calculateFatigue()

```js
function calculateFatigue(business, genreIds, topicId, platformIds, sizeId, audienceId) {
  const releasedGames = (business.games || []).filter((g) => g.releasedAt);
  if (releasedGames.length === 0) return { multiplier: 1.0, breakdown: null };

  const now = Date.now();
  const gIds = Array.isArray(genreIds) ? genreIds : [genreIds];

  let topicFatigue = 0, genreFatigue = 0;

  for (const game of releasedGames) {
    const hoursSince = (now - game.releasedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    if (hoursSince >= FATIGUE_WINDOW_HOURS) continue;
    const recencyWeight = 1 - (hoursSince / FATIGUE_WINDOW_HOURS);

    const gameGIds = Array.isArray(game.genreIds) ? game.genreIds : (game.genreId ? [game.genreId] : []);

    if (game.topicId === topicId) topicFatigue += recencyWeight * FATIGUE_PER_REPEAT;

    const genreOverlap = gIds.filter((id) => gameGIds.includes(id)).length;
    if (genreOverlap > 0) genreFatigue += recencyWeight * FATIGUE_PER_REPEAT * genreOverlap;
  }

  const totalFatigue = Math.min(FATIGUE_MAX, topicFatigue + genreFatigue);
  const multiplier = 1 - totalFatigue;

  return {
    multiplier,
    breakdown: {
      topic: Math.round(topicFatigue * 100) / 100,
      genre: Math.round(genreFatigue * 100) / 100,
      total: Math.round(totalFatigue * 100) / 100,
    },
  };
}
```

### calculateGameRating()

```js
function calculateGameRating(business, genreIds, topicId, platformIds, sizeId, audienceId, fatigueMultiplier = 1.0) {
  const size = getGameSize(sizeId);
  const topic = getTopic(topicId);
  if (!size || !topic) return { rating: 1, breakdown: null };

  const gIds = Array.isArray(genreIds) ? genreIds : [genreIds];
  const pIds = Array.isArray(platformIds) ? platformIds : [platformIds];
  const genres = gIds.map(getGenre).filter(Boolean);
  const platforms = pIds.map(getPlatform).filter(Boolean);
  if (genres.length === 0 || platforms.length === 0) return { rating: 1, breakdown: null };

  const staff = business.staff || 0;
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level || 1);
  const audience = getAudience(audienceId);

  // 1. Staff score (0-1): combines staff count vs size requirement AND average skill
  const staffRatio = Math.min(1.5, staff / Math.max(1, size.staffReq));
  const avgSkill = getStaffAvgSkill(business); // 1-10
  const skillFactor = avgSkill / 10; // 0.1-1.0
  const staffScore = Math.min(1.0, staffRatio * 0.5 + skillFactor * 0.5);

  // 2. Topic × Genre fit (average across selected genres)
  let tgSum = 0;
  for (const g of genres) {
    tgSum += (TOPIC_GENRE_FIT[topicId]?.[g.id] ?? 0.5);
  }
  const topicGenreFit = tgSum / genres.length;

  // 3. Platform × Genre fit (average across all genre×platform combos)
  let pgSum = 0, pgCount = 0;
  for (const g of genres) {
    for (const p of platforms) {
      pgSum += (p.genreFit[g.id] ?? 0.8);
      pgCount++;
    }
  }
  const platformFit = pgCount > 0 ? pgSum / pgCount : 0.8;

  // 4. Topic × Audience fit
  const topicAudienceFit = TOPIC_AUDIENCE_FIT[topicId]?.[audienceId] ?? 0.5;

  // 5. Genre × Audience fit (average across selected genres)
  let gaSum = 0;
  for (const g of genres) {
    gaSum += (GENRE_AUDIENCE_FIT[g.id]?.[audienceId] ?? 0.5);
  }
  const genreAudienceFit = gaSum / genres.length;

  // 6. Audience × Size fit
  const audienceSizeFit = AUDIENCE_SIZE_FIT[audienceId]?.[sizeId] ?? 0.5;

  // 7. Genre × Size fit (average across selected genres)
  let gsSum = 0;
  for (const g of genres) {
    gsSum += (GENRE_SIZE_FIT[g.id]?.[sizeId] ?? 0.5);
  }
  const genreSizeFit = gsSum / genres.length;

  // 8. Topic × Size fit
  const topicSizeFit = TOPIC_SIZE_FIT[topicId]?.[sizeId] ?? 0.5;

  // Level bonus (higher level = better studio quality)
  const levelBonus = Math.min(0.3, (level - 1) * 0.02);

  // Multi-genre/platform penalty (harder to balance)
  const multiPenalty = Math.max(0, (genres.length - 1) * 0.05 + (platforms.length - 1) * 0.03);

  // Random factor (±8%)
  const randomFactor = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;

  // Combine factors with weights
  const comboFit = (topicGenreFit * 0.20 + platformFit * 0.10 + topicAudienceFit * 0.10 + genreAudienceFit * 0.10 + audienceSizeFit * 0.10 + genreSizeFit * 0.10 + topicSizeFit * 0.10);

  // Bad fit penalty: each --- (≤0.3) compatibility drags the rating down hard
  const allFits = [topicGenreFit, platformFit, topicAudienceFit, genreAudienceFit, audienceSizeFit, genreSizeFit, topicSizeFit];
  const badFitPenalty = allFits.filter((f) => f <= 0.3).length * 0.15;

  const quality = Math.max(0, (staffScore * 0.40 + comboFit * 0.60 + levelBonus - multiPenalty - badFitPenalty) * randomFactor * fatigueMultiplier);

  const rating = Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Math.round(quality * 10)));

  const breakdown = {
    staffScore: Math.round(staffScore * 100) / 100,
    topicGenreFit: Math.round(topicGenreFit * 100) / 100,
    platformFit: Math.round(platformFit * 100) / 100,
    topicAudienceFit: Math.round(topicAudienceFit * 100) / 100,
    genreAudienceFit: Math.round(genreAudienceFit * 100) / 100,
    audienceSizeFit: Math.round(audienceSizeFit * 100) / 100,
    genreSizeFit: Math.round(genreSizeFit * 100) / 100,
    topicSizeFit: Math.round(topicSizeFit * 100) / 100,
    comboFit: Math.round(comboFit * 100) / 100,
    levelBonus: Math.round(levelBonus * 100) / 100,
    multiPenalty: Math.round(multiPenalty * 100) / 100,
    badFitPenalty: Math.round(badFitPenalty * 100) / 100,
    randomFactor: Math.round(randomFactor * 100) / 100,
    fatigueMultiplier: Math.round(fatigueMultiplier * 100) / 100,
    quality: Math.round(quality * 100) / 100,
  };

  return { rating, breakdown };
}
```

### getGameHourlyIncome()

```js
function getGameHourlyIncome(game) {
  if (!game || !game.releasedAt) return 0;
  const now = Date.now();
  const elapsedHr = (now - game.releasedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
  const bonusHr = game.bonusHours || 0;
  const fatigueMult = game.fatigueMultiplier || 1.0;
  const totalLifespan = (GAME_LIFESPAN_HOURS + bonusHr) * fatigueMult;
  if (elapsedHr >= totalLifespan) return 0;

  const size = getGameSize(game.sizeId);
  if (!size) return 0;

  // Support both old single-id and new array formats
  const gIds = Array.isArray(game.genreIds) ? game.genreIds : (game.genreId ? [game.genreId] : []);
  const pIds = Array.isArray(game.platformIds) ? game.platformIds : (game.platformId ? [game.platformId] : []);
  const genres = gIds.map(getGenre).filter(Boolean);
  const platforms = pIds.map(getPlatform).filter(Boolean);
  if (genres.length === 0 || platforms.length === 0) return 0;

  // Average genre income base
  const avgGenreIncome = genres.reduce((s, g) => s + g.incomeBase, 0) / genres.length;
  // Average platform market share
  const avgMarketShare = platforms.reduce((s, p) => s + p.marketShare, 0) / platforms.length;
  // Multi-platform bonus: releasing on more platforms = more total sales
  const platformBonus = 1 + (platforms.length - 1) * 0.4;
  // Multi-genre bonus: broader appeal
  const genreBonus = 1 + (genres.length - 1) * 0.2;

  // Audience multiplier
  const audience = getAudience(game.audienceId);
  const audienceMult = audience ? audience.incomeMult : 1.0;

  // Base hourly income
  const baseIncome = avgGenreIncome * size.incomeMult * avgMarketShare * platformBonus * genreBonus * audienceMult;

  // Rating multiplier (rating/10)^2 - high ratings earn disproportionately more
  const ratingMult = Math.pow(game.rating / 10, 2);

  // Decay: linear decay over lifespan
  const decayFactor = 1 - (elapsedHr / totalLifespan);

  return Math.floor(baseIncome * ratingMult * decayFactor * fatigueMult);
}
```

### getContractHourlyIncome()

```js
function getContractHourlyIncome(contract) {
  if (!contract || !contract.startedAt) return 0;
  const now = Date.now();
  const elapsedHr = (now - contract.startedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
  if (elapsedHr >= CONTRACT_LIFESPAN_HOURS) return 0;

  const type = getContractType(contract.typeId);
  if (!type) return 0;

  // Decay: linear decay over lifespan
  const decayFactor = 1 - (elapsedHr / CONTRACT_LIFESPAN_HOURS);
  return Math.floor(type.royaltyPerHr * decayFactor);
}
```

### getGameDevHourlyIncome()

```js
function getGameDevHourlyIncome(business) {
  const games = business.games || [];
  const contracts = business.contracts || [];
  const staff = business.staff || 0;
  const levelMult = getLevelMultiplier(business);

  let income = 0;
  for (const game of games) {
    income += getGameHourlyIncome(game) * levelMult;
  }
  for (const contract of contracts) {
    income += getContractHourlyIncome(contract) * levelMult;
  }

  // Subtract staff salaries
  income -= getStaffSalaryHourly(business);

  return income;
}
```

### getReservedStaff()

```js
function getReservedStaff(business) {
  const activeGames = (business.games || []).filter((g) => !g.releasedAt);
  const activeContracts = (business.contracts || []).filter((c) => {
    const elapsed = (Date.now() - c.startedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    return elapsed < CONTRACT_LIFESPAN_HOURS;
  });
  let reserved = 0;
  for (const g of activeGames) {
    const size = getGameSize(g.sizeId);
    if (size) reserved += size.staffReq;
  }
  for (const c of activeContracts) {
    const type = getContractType(c.typeId);
    if (type) reserved += type.staffReq;
  }
  return reserved;
}
```

### getAvailableStaff()

```js
function getAvailableStaff(business) {
  return Math.max(0, (business.staff || 0) - getReservedStaff(business));
}
```

### startGameDev()

```js
function startGameDev(business, genreIds, topicId, platformIds, sizeId, audienceId, title) {
  const size = getGameSize(sizeId);
  if (!size) return null;

  const activeGames = (business.games || []).filter((g) => !g.releasedAt);
  if (activeGames.length >= getMaxGameSlots(business)) return null;

  // Check staff availability
  const availableStaff = getAvailableStaff(business);
  if (availableStaff < size.staffReq) return null;

  const now = Date.now();
  const devTimeMs = calculateDevTimeMs(sizeId, genreIds, platformIds);

  const game = {
    instanceId: `game_${now}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    title: title || 'Untitled',
    genreIds: Array.isArray(genreIds) ? genreIds : [genreIds],
    topicId,
    platformIds: Array.isArray(platformIds) ? platformIds : [platformIds],
    sizeId,
    audienceId: audienceId || 'everyone',
    startedAt: now,
    completesAt: now + devTimeMs,
    releasedAt: null,
    rating: 0,
    income: 0,
  };

  return {
    ...business,
    games: [...(business.games || []), game],
  };
}
```

### releaseGame()

```js
function releaseGame(business, instanceId) {
  const games = (business.games || []).map((g) => {
    if (g.instanceId === instanceId && !g.releasedAt) {
      const genreIds = Array.isArray(g.genreIds) ? g.genreIds : (g.genreId ? [g.genreId] : []);
      const platformIds = Array.isArray(g.platformIds) ? g.platformIds : (g.platformId ? [g.platformId] : []);
      const fatigue = calculateFatigue(business, genreIds, g.topicId, platformIds, g.sizeId, g.audienceId);
      const result = calculateGameRating(business, genreIds, g.topicId, platformIds, g.sizeId, g.audienceId, fatigue.multiplier);
      const rating = typeof result === 'object' ? result.rating : result;
      const breakdown = typeof result === 'object' ? result.breakdown : null;
      return {
        ...g,
        releasedAt: Date.now(),
        rating,
        ratingBreakdown: breakdown,
        fatigueMultiplier: fatigue.multiplier,
        fatigueBreakdown: fatigue.breakdown,
      };
    }
    return g;
  });

  return { ...business, games };
}
```

### getCompletedGames()

```js
function getCompletedGames(business) {
  const now = Date.now();
  return (business.games || []).filter((g) => !g.releasedAt && g.completesAt <= now);
}
```

### getGameDevTimeRemaining()

```js
function getGameDevTimeRemaining(game) {
  if (!game || game.releasedAt) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, game.completesAt - Date.now());
}
```

### marketGame()

```js
function marketGame(business, gameInstanceId) {
  const games = business.games || [];
  const game = games.find((g) => g.instanceId === gameInstanceId && g.releasedAt);
  if (!game) return null;

  const elapsedHr = (Date.now() - game.releasedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
  const bonusHr = game.bonusHours || 0;
  const fatigueMult = game.fatigueMultiplier || 1.0;
  const totalLifespan = (GAME_LIFESPAN_HOURS + bonusHr) * fatigueMult;
  if (elapsedHr >= totalLifespan) return null; // game already expired

  const marketingCount = game.marketingCount || 0;

  // Diminishing returns: each marketing adds fewer hours
  // 1st: 6h, 2nd: 5h, 3rd: 4h, 4th: 3h, 5th: 2h, 6th+: 1h
  const bonusHours = Math.max(1, MARKETING_BASE_HOURS - marketingCount);

  // Cost scales with marketing count and game size
  const size = getGameSize(game.sizeId);
  const sizeMult = size ? size.incomeMult : 1;
  const cost = Math.floor(MARKETING_BASE_COST * sizeMult * Math.pow(1.5, marketingCount));

  const updatedGames = games.map((g) => {
    if (g.instanceId === gameInstanceId) {
      return {
        ...g,
        bonusHours: (g.bonusHours || 0) + bonusHours,
        marketingCount: marketingCount + 1,
      };
    }
    return g;
  });

  return { business: { ...business, games: updatedGames }, bonusHours, cost };
}
```

### generateContracts()

```js
function generateContracts(business) {
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level || 1);
  const staff = business.staff || 0;
  const now = Date.now();

  // Available contracts based on level and staff
  const available = CONTRACT_TYPES.filter((c) => {
    return c.staffReq <= Math.max(2, staff + 2) || level >= 3;
  });

  // Pick 3 random contracts
  const shuffled = [...available].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5).slice(0, 3);

  return shuffled.map((type) => ({
    instanceId: `contract_${now}_${type.id}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5)}`,
    typeId: type.id,
    offeredAt: now,
  }));
}
```

### acceptContract()

```js
function acceptContract(business, contractInstanceId) {
  const available = business.availableContracts || [];
  const contract = available.find((c) => c.instanceId === contractInstanceId);
  if (!contract) return null;

  const type = getContractType(contract.typeId);
  if (!type) return null;

  const activeContracts = (business.contracts || []).filter((c) => {
    const elapsed = (Date.now() - c.startedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    return elapsed < CONTRACT_LIFESPAN_HOURS;
  });

  if (activeContracts.length >= getMaxContractSlots(business)) return null;

  // Check staff availability
  const availableStaff = getAvailableStaff(business);
  if (availableStaff < type.staffReq) return null;

  const now = Date.now();
  const newContract = {
    ...contract,
    startedAt: now,
    completesAt: now + type.duration * 60 * 60 * 1000,
  };

  return {
    ...business,
    contracts: [...(business.contracts || []), newContract],
    availableContracts: available.filter((c) => c.instanceId !== contractInstanceId),
  };
}
```

### generateStaffMember()

```js
function generateStaffMember(business) {
  const person = generatePerson();
  const level = business && business.level ? business.level : 1;
  const games = business && business.games ? business.games : [];
  const releasedGames = games.filter((g) => g.releasedAt);
  const avgQuality = releasedGames.length > 0
    ? releasedGames.reduce((s, g) => s + (g.rating || 5), 0) / releasedGames.length
    : 5;
  const qualityFactor = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, avgQuality / 10));
  const levelFactor = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, level / 30));
  const scale = (levelFactor + qualityFactor) / 2;

  const amazingPct = 0.02 + 0.08 * scale;
  const greatPct   = 0.08 + 0.12 * scale;
  const goodPct    = 0.15 + 0.15 * scale;
  const badPct     = 0.50 - 0.20 * scale;
  const vbadPct    = 0.25 - 0.15 * scale;

  const roll = Math.random();
  let skill;
  if (roll < vbadPct) {
    skill = Math.floor(Math.random() * 2) + 1;
  } else if (roll < vbadPct + badPct) {
    skill = Math.floor(Math.random() * 2) + 3;
  } else if (roll < vbadPct + badPct + goodPct) {
    skill = Math.floor(Math.random() * 2) + 5;
  } else if (roll < vbadPct + badPct + goodPct + greatPct) {
    skill = Math.floor(Math.random() * 2) + 7;
  } else {
    skill = Math.random() < 0.05 ? 10 : 9;
  }
  return {
    id: `staff_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    name: person.name,
    age: person.age,
    gender: person.gender,
    skill,
    salary: STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY,
    hiredAt: Date.now(),
  };
}
```

### getStaffAvgSkill()

```js
function getStaffAvgSkill(business) {
  const members = business.staffMembers || [];
  if (members.length === 0) return 5;
  return members.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.skill, 0) / members.length;
}
```

### getStaffSalaryHourly()

```js
function getStaffSalaryHourly(business) {
  return (business.staffMembers || []).reduce((total, m) => total + (m.salary || STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY), 0);
}
```

### hireStaff()

```js
function hireStaff(business, count) {
  const currentHiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  if (currentHiring.length + count > MAX_HIRING_AT_ONCE) return null;

  const now = Date.now();
  const newHires = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const duration = STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (STAFF_HIRE_MAX_MS - STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS);
    const member = generateStaffMember(business);
    newHires.push({
      id: `hire_${now}_${i}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5)}`,
      hiredAt: now,
      completesAt: now + duration,
      staffMember: member,
    });
  }

  return {
    ...business,
    hiringStaff: [...currentHiring, ...newHires],
  };
}
```

### completeHiredStaff()

```js
function completeHiredStaff(business) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const completed = hiring.filter((h) => h.completesAt <= now);
  const stillHiring = hiring.filter((h) => h.completesAt > now);

  if (completed.length === 0) return business;

  const newMembers = completed.map((h) => h.staffMember).filter(Boolean);
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (business.staff || 0) + completed.length,
    staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), ...newMembers],
    hiringStaff: stillHiring,
  };
}
```

### instantHireStaff()

```js
function instantHireStaff(business, hireId) {
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const hire = hiring.find((h) => h.id === hireId);
  if (!hire) return business;

  const newMember = hire.staffMember || generateStaffMember(business);
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (business.staff || 0) + 1,
    staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), newMember],
    hiringStaff: hiring.filter((h) => h.id !== hireId),
  };
}
```

### fireStaff()

```js
function fireStaff(business, staffId) {
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: Math.max(0, (business.staff || 0) - 1),
    staffMembers: (business.staffMembers || []).filter((s) => s.id !== staffId),
  };
}
```

### raiseStaff()

```js
function raiseStaff(business, staffId) {
  const members = (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => {
    if (s.id === staffId) return { ...s, salary: Math.round((s.salary || STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY) * 1.15) };
    return s;
  });
  return { ...business, staffMembers: members };
}
```

### trainStaff()

```js
function trainStaff(business, staffId) {
  const members = (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => {
    if (s.id === staffId) return { ...s, skill: Math.min(10, (s.skill || 5) + 1) };
    return s;
  });
  return { ...business, staffMembers: members };
}
```

### getHireTimeRemaining()

```js
function getHireTimeRemaining(hire) {
  return Math.max(0, hire.completesAt - Date.now());
}
```

### processGameDev()

```js
function processGameDev(business, minutesElapsed) {
  const hoursFraction = minutesElapsed / 60;
  const levelMult = getLevelMultiplier(business);
  const suspended = isSuspended(business);
  let income = 0;

  // Backwards compat: generate staffMembers for existing saves
  let workingBusiness = business;
  if ((business.staff || 0) > 0 && (!business.staffMembers || business.staffMembers.length === 0)) {
    const members = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < business.staff; i++) {
      members.push(generateStaffMember(business));
    }
    workingBusiness = { ...business, staffMembers: members };
  }

  // Income from released games
  const games = (workingBusiness.games || []).map((g) => {
    if (g.releasedAt) {
      const gameIncome = suspended ? 0 : getGameHourlyIncome(g) * levelMult * hoursFraction;
      income += gameIncome;
      return { ...g, income: (g.income || 0) + gameIncome };
    }
    return g;
  });

  // Income from active contracts
  const contracts = (workingBusiness.contracts || []).map((c) => {
    const contractIncome = suspended ? 0 : getContractHourlyIncome(c) * levelMult * hoursFraction;
    income += contractIncome;
    return { ...c, income: (c.income || 0) + contractIncome };
  });

  // Staff salary
  const salary = getStaffSalaryHourly(workingBusiness) * hoursFraction;
  income -= salary;

  // Remove expired games and contracts
  const now = Date.now();
  const activeGames = games.filter((g) => {
    if (!g.releasedAt) return true; // still in development
    const elapsedHr = (now - g.releasedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    const fatigueMult = g.fatigueMultiplier || 1.0;
    const totalLifespan = (GAME_LIFESPAN_HOURS + (g.bonusHours || 0)) * fatigueMult;
    return elapsedHr < totalLifespan;
  });
  const activeContracts = contracts.filter((c) => {
    const elapsedHr = (now - c.startedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    return elapsedHr < CONTRACT_LIFESPAN_HOURS;
  });

  // Refresh available contracts if needed
  let availableContracts = workingBusiness.availableContracts || [];
  const lastRefresh = workingBusiness.contractsRefreshedAt || 0;
  if (now - lastRefresh > CONTRACT_REFRESH_HOURS * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
    availableContracts = generateContracts(workingBusiness);
  }

  return {
    updatedBusiness: {
      ...workingBusiness,
      games: activeGames,
      contracts: activeContracts,
      availableContracts,
      contractsRefreshedAt: now,
      allTimeEarnings: (workingBusiness.allTimeEarnings || 0) + income,
    },
    income,
  };
}
```

### formatTimeRemaining()

```js
function formatTimeRemaining(ms) {
  const totalSec = Math.ceil(ms / 1000);
  const hours = Math.floor(totalSec / 3600);
  const mins = Math.floor((totalSec % 3600) / 60);
  const secs = totalSec % 60;
  if (hours > 0) return `${hours}h ${mins}m`;
  if (mins > 0) return `${mins}m ${secs}s`;
  return `${secs}s`;
}
```

### applyForJob()

```js
function applyForJob(jobId) {
  const job = getJobById(jobId);
  if (!job) return { success: false, job: null };
  const roll = Math.random();
  const success = roll <= job.successRate;
  return { success, job };
}
```

### createInitialMarketState()

```js
function createInitialMarketState() {
  const state = {};
  for (const asset of ALL_ASSETS) {
    const h = [asset.basePrice];
    // Seed with slight variations so graphs have data immediately
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      const variance = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * asset.volatility * asset.basePrice;
      h.push(Math.max(0.01, asset.basePrice + variance));
    }
    state[asset.id] = {
      id: asset.id,
      price: h[h.length - 1],
      prevPrice: h[0],
      history: h,
    };
  }
  return state;
}
```

### getAssetDrift()

```js
function getAssetDrift(asset) {
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < asset.id.length; i++) {
    hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + asset.id.charCodeAt(i);
    hash |= 0;
  }
  const normalized = (Math.abs(hash) % 1000) / 1000;
  // Drift between -0.8% and +0.8% per hour
  return -0.008 + normalized * 0.016;
}
```

### normalRandom()

```js
function normalRandom() {
  let u = 0;
  let v = 0;
  while (u === 0) u = Math.random();
  while (v === 0) v = Math.random();
  return Math.sqrt(-2.0 * Math.log(u)) * Math.cos(2.0 * Math.PI * v);
}
```

### simulatePriceChange()

```js
function simulatePriceChange(currentPrice, volatility, drift, minutesElapsed) {
  const dtHours = minutesElapsed / 60;
  const sigma = volatility * Math.sqrt(dtHours);
  const mu = drift * dtHours;
  const Z = normalRandom();
  const change = Math.exp((mu - 0.5 * sigma * sigma) + sigma * Z);
  return Math.max(currentPrice * change, 0.01);
}
```

### simulateMarket()

```js
function simulateMarket(marketState, lastUpdate, now = Date.now()) {
  const minutesElapsed = Math.max(1, Math.floor((now - lastUpdate) / (1000 * 60)));
  if (minutesElapsed <= 0) return { marketState, minutesElapsed, income: 0 };

  const newState = {};

  for (const asset of ALL_ASSETS) {
    const current = marketState[asset.id];
    if (!current) {
      const drift = getAssetDrift(asset);
      const h = [asset.basePrice];
      let price = asset.basePrice;
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        price = simulatePriceChange(price, asset.volatility, drift, 60);
        h.push(price);
      }
      newState[asset.id] = {
        id: asset.id,
        price: h[h.length - 1],
        prevPrice: h[0],
        history: h,
      };
      continue;
    }

    const drift = getAssetDrift(asset);
    const newPrice = simulatePriceChange(current.price, asset.volatility, drift, minutesElapsed);

    // Build history (keep last 30 data points)
    const newHistory = [...current.history, newPrice].slice(-30);

    newState[asset.id] = {
      id: asset.id,
      price: newPrice,
      prevPrice: current.price,
      history: newHistory,
    };
  }

  return { marketState: newState, minutesElapsed, income: 0 };
}
```

### calculatePassiveIncome()

```js
function calculatePassiveIncome(holdings, marketState, minutesElapsed) {
  let income = 0;
  const hoursFraction = minutesElapsed / 60;
  for (const holding of holdings) {
    // Real estate income
    const reAsset = REAL_ESTATE.find((r) => r.id === holding.assetId);
    if (reAsset) {
      income += reAsset.hourlyIncome * holding.quantity * hoursFraction;
    }
    // Stock dividends - direct hourly % of price
    const stock = STOCKS.find((s) => s.id === holding.assetId);
    if (stock) {
      const state = marketState[stock.id];
      const price = state ? state.price : stock.basePrice;
      const yieldPct = SECTOR_HOURLY_YIELDS[stock.sector] || 0;
      income += yieldPct * price * holding.quantity * hoursFraction;
    }
    // Crypto staking rewards - direct hourly % of price
    const coin = CRYPTO.find((c) => c.id === holding.assetId);
    if (coin) {
      const state = marketState[coin.id];
      const price = state ? state.price : coin.basePrice;
      income += CRYPTO_HOURLY_YIELD * price * holding.quantity * hoursFraction;
    }
  }
  return income * (1 - INVESTMENT_TAX_RATE);
}
```

### getHourlyIncome()

```js
function getHourlyIncome(asset, state) {
  if (asset.id.startsWith('re_')) {
    return (asset.hourlyIncome || 0) * (1 - INVESTMENT_TAX_RATE);
  }
  const price = state ? state.price : asset.basePrice;
  if (asset.id.startsWith('cr_')) {
    return CRYPTO_HOURLY_YIELD * price * (1 - INVESTMENT_TAX_RATE);
  }
  // Stock dividend: direct hourly % of price
  const yieldPct = SECTOR_HOURLY_YIELDS[asset.sector] || 0;
  return yieldPct * price * (1 - INVESTMENT_TAX_RATE);
}
```

### formatMoney()

```js
function formatMoney(n) {
  if (n == null || isNaN(n)) return '$0';
  if (n >= 1e9) return '$' + (n / 1e9).toFixed(2) + 'B';
  if (n >= 1e6) return '$' + (n / 1e6).toFixed(2) + 'M';
  if (n >= 1e3) return '$' + (n / 1e3).toFixed(2) + 'K';
  return '$' + n.toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
}
```

### formatPrice()

```js
function formatPrice(n) {
  if (n == null || isNaN(n)) return '$0.00';
  if (n >= 1e6) return '$' + (n / 1e6).toFixed(2) + 'M';
  if (n >= 1e3) return '$' + (n / 1e3).toFixed(2) + 'K';
  if (n >= 1) return '$' + n.toFixed(2);
  return '$' + n.toFixed(4);
}
```

### formatPercent()

```js
function formatPercent(n) {
  return (n >= 0 ? '+' : '') + n.toFixed(2) + '%';
}
```

### getChangePercent()

```js
function getChangePercent(current, previous) {
  if (previous === 0) return 0;
  return ((current - previous) / previous) * 100;
}
```

### calculateDevTimeMs()

```js
function calculateDevTimeMs(sizeId, genreIds) {
  const size = getMovieSize(sizeId);
  if (!size) return 0;
  let devHours = size.devHours;
  const extraGenres = Math.max(0, (genreIds || []).length - 1);
  devHours *= (1 + extraGenres * MULTI_GENRE_TIME_PENALTY);
  return devHours * 60 * 60 * 1000;
}
```

### getLevelMultiplier()

```js
function getLevelMultiplier(business) {
  return Math.pow(1.12, Math.max(1, business.level || 1) - 1);
}
```

### getMaxMovieSlots()

```js
function getMaxMovieSlots(business) {
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level || 1);
  return Math.min(MAX_ACTIVE_MOVIES, 1 + Math.floor((level - 1) / 6));
}
```

### calculateFatigue()

```js
function calculateFatigue(business, genreIds, topicId, sizeId, audienceId) {
  const releasedMovies = (business.movies || []).filter((m) => m.releasedAt);
  if (releasedMovies.length === 0) return { multiplier: 1.0, breakdown: null };

  const now = Date.now();
  const gIds = Array.isArray(genreIds) ? genreIds : [genreIds];

  let topicFatigue = 0, genreFatigue = 0;

  for (const movie of releasedMovies) {
    const hoursSince = (now - movie.releasedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    if (hoursSince >= FATIGUE_WINDOW_HOURS) continue;
    const recencyWeight = 1 - (hoursSince / FATIGUE_WINDOW_HOURS);

    const movieGIds = Array.isArray(movie.genreIds) ? movie.genreIds : (movie.genreId ? [movie.genreId] : []);

    if (movie.topicId === topicId) topicFatigue += recencyWeight * FATIGUE_PER_REPEAT;

    const genreOverlap = gIds.filter((id) => movieGIds.includes(id)).length;
    if (genreOverlap > 0) genreFatigue += recencyWeight * FATIGUE_PER_REPEAT * genreOverlap;
  }

  const totalFatigue = Math.min(FATIGUE_MAX, topicFatigue + genreFatigue);
  const multiplier = 1 - totalFatigue;

  return {
    multiplier,
    breakdown: {
      topic: Math.round(topicFatigue * 100) / 100,
      genre: Math.round(genreFatigue * 100) / 100,
      total: Math.round(totalFatigue * 100) / 100,
    },
  };
}
```

### calculateMovieRating()

```js
function calculateMovieRating(business, genreIds, topicId, sizeId, audienceId, setId, fatigueMultiplier = 1.0) {
  const size = getMovieSize(sizeId);
  const topic = getTopic(topicId);
  if (!size || !topic) return { rating: 1, breakdown: null };

  const gIds = Array.isArray(genreIds) ? genreIds : [genreIds];
  const genres = gIds.map(getGenre).filter(Boolean);
  if (genres.length === 0) return { rating: 1, breakdown: null };

  const staff = business.staff || 0;
  const level = Math.max(1, business.level || 1);
  const audience = getAudience(audienceId);

  // 1. Staff score (0-1): combines staff count vs size requirement AND average skill
  const staffRatio = Math.min(1.5, staff / Math.max(1, size.staffReq));
  const avgSkill = getStaffAvgSkill(business);
  const skillFactor = avgSkill / 10;
  const staffScore = Math.min(1.0, staffRatio * 0.5 + skillFactor * 0.5);

  // 2. Topic × Genre fit (average across selected genres)
  let tgSum = 0;
  for (const g of genres) {
    tgSum += (TOPIC_GENRE_FIT[topicId]?.[g.id] ?? 0.5);
  }
  const topicGenreFit = tgSum / genres.length;

  // 3. Topic × Audience fit
  const topicAudienceFit = TOPIC_AUDIENCE_FIT[topicId]?.[audienceId] ?? 0.5;

  // 4. Genre × Audience fit (average across selected genres)
  let gaSum = 0;
  for (const g of genres) {
    gaSum += (GENRE_AUDIENCE_FIT[g.id]?.[audienceId] ?? 0.5);
  }
  const genreAudienceFit = gaSum / genres.length;

  // 5. Audience × Size fit
  const audienceSizeFit = AUDIENCE_SIZE_FIT[audienceId]?.[sizeId] ?? 0.5;

  // 6. Genre × Size fit (average across selected genres)
  let gsSum = 0;
  for (const g of genres) {
    gsSum += (GENRE_SIZE_FIT[g.id]?.[sizeId] ?? 0.5);
  }
  const genreSizeFit = gsSum / genres.length;

  // 7. Topic × Size fit
  const topicSizeFit = TOPIC_SIZE_FIT[topicId]?.[sizeId] ?? 0.5;

  // 8. Set quality bonus
  const setBonus = getMovieSet(setId)?.qualityBonus || 0;

  // Level bonus
  const levelBonus = Math.min(0.3, (level - 1) * 0.02);

  // Multi-genre penalty
  const multiPenalty = Math.max(0, (genres.length - 1) * 0.05);

  // Random factor (±8%)
  const randomFactor = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;

  // Combine factors with weights
  const comboFit = (topicGenreFit * 0.20 + topicAudienceFit * 0.12 + genreAudienceFit * 0.12 + audienceSizeFit * 0.12 + genreSizeFit * 0.12 + topicSizeFit * 0.12);

  // Bad fit penalty
  const allFits = [topicGenreFit, topicAudienceFit, genreAudienceFit, audienceSizeFit, genreSizeFit, topicSizeFit];
  const badFitPenalty = allFits.filter((f) => f <= 0.3).length * 0.15;

  const quality = Math.max(0, (staffScore * 0.35 + comboFit * 0.55 + setBonus + levelBonus - multiPenalty - badFitPenalty) * randomFactor * fatigueMultiplier);

  const rating = Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Math.round(quality * 10)));

  const breakdown = {
    staffScore: Math.round(staffScore * 100) / 100,
    topicGenreFit: Math.round(topicGenreFit * 100) / 100,
    topicAudienceFit: Math.round(topicAudienceFit * 100) / 100,
    genreAudienceFit: Math.round(genreAudienceFit * 100) / 100,
    audienceSizeFit: Math.round(audienceSizeFit * 100) / 100,
    genreSizeFit: Math.round(genreSizeFit * 100) / 100,
    topicSizeFit: Math.round(topicSizeFit * 100) / 100,
    setBonus: Math.round(setBonus * 100) / 100,
    comboFit: Math.round(comboFit * 100) / 100,
    levelBonus: Math.round(levelBonus * 100) / 100,
    multiPenalty: Math.round(multiPenalty * 100) / 100,
    badFitPenalty: Math.round(badFitPenalty * 100) / 100,
    randomFactor: Math.round(randomFactor * 100) / 100,
    fatigueMultiplier: Math.round(fatigueMultiplier * 100) / 100,
    quality: Math.round(quality * 100) / 100,
  };

  return { rating, breakdown };
}
```

### getMovieHourlyIncome()

```js
function getMovieHourlyIncome(movie) {
  if (!movie || !movie.releasedAt) return 0;
  const now = Date.now();
  const elapsedHr = (now - movie.releasedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
  const bonusHr = movie.bonusHours || 0;
  const fatigueMult = movie.fatigueMultiplier || 1.0;
  const totalLifespan = (MOVIE_LIFESPAN_HOURS + bonusHr) * fatigueMult;
  if (elapsedHr >= totalLifespan) return 0;

  const size = getMovieSize(movie.sizeId);
  if (!size) return 0;

  const gIds = Array.isArray(movie.genreIds) ? movie.genreIds : (movie.genreId ? [movie.genreId] : []);
  const genres = gIds.map(getGenre).filter(Boolean);
  if (genres.length === 0) return 0;

  const avgGenreIncome = genres.reduce((s, g) => s + g.incomeBase, 0) / genres.length;
  const genreBonus = 1 + (genres.length - 1) * 0.2;

  const audience = getAudience(movie.audienceId);
  const audienceMult = audience ? audience.incomeMult : 1.0;

  const baseIncome = avgGenreIncome * size.incomeMult * genreBonus * audienceMult;
  const ratingMult = Math.pow(movie.rating / 10, 2);
  const decayFactor = 1 - (elapsedHr / totalLifespan);

  return Math.floor(baseIncome * ratingMult * decayFactor * fatigueMult);
}
```

### getMovieStudioHourlyIncome()

```js
function getMovieStudioHourlyIncome(business) {
  const movies = business.movies || [];
  const levelMult = getLevelMultiplier(business);

  let income = 0;
  for (const movie of movies) {
    income += getMovieHourlyIncome(movie) * levelMult;
  }

  income -= getStaffSalaryHourly(business);

  return income;
}
```

### getMovieStudioPendingIncomeHourly()

```js
function getMovieStudioPendingIncomeHourly(business) {
  const movies = business.movies || [];
  const levelMult = getLevelMultiplier(business);
  let income = 0;
  for (const movie of movies) {
    if (movie.releasedAt) {
      income += getMovieHourlyIncome(movie) * levelMult;
    }
  }
  return Math.floor(income);
}
```

### getStaffSalaryHourly()

```js
function getStaffSalaryHourly(business) {
  const members = business.staffMembers || [];
  if (members.length === 0) {
    const staffCount = typeof business.staff === 'number' ? business.staff : 0;
    return staffCount * STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY;
  }
  return members.reduce((total, m) => total + (m.salary || STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY), 0);
}
```

### getMovieStudioPendingSales()

```js
function getMovieStudioPendingSales(business) {
  const movies = business.movies || [];
  let pending = 0;
  for (const movie of movies) {
    if (!movie.releasedAt) continue;
    const elapsedHr = (Date.now() - movie.releasedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    const bonusHr = movie.bonusHours || 0;
    const fatigueMult = movie.fatigueMultiplier || 1.0;
    const totalLifespan = (MOVIE_LIFESPAN_HOURS + bonusHr) * fatigueMult;
    if (elapsedHr >= totalLifespan) continue;
    const remainingHr = totalLifespan - elapsedHr;
    pending += getMovieHourlyIncome(movie) * remainingHr;
  }
  return Math.floor(pending);
}
```

### getReservedStaff()

```js
function getReservedStaff(business) {
  const activeMovies = (business.movies || []).filter((m) => !m.releasedAt);
  let reserved = 0;
  for (const m of activeMovies) {
    const size = getMovieSize(m.sizeId);
    if (size) reserved += size.staffReq;
  }
  return reserved;
}
```

### getAvailableStaff()

```js
function getAvailableStaff(business) {
  const staffCount = typeof business.staff === 'number' ? business.staff : 0;
  return Math.max(0, staffCount - getReservedStaff(business));
}
```

### startMovieDev()

```js
function startMovieDev(business, genreIds, topicId, sizeId, audienceId, title, setId) {
  const size = getMovieSize(sizeId);
  if (!size) return null;

  const activeMovies = (business.movies || []).filter((m) => !m.releasedAt);
  if (activeMovies.length >= getMaxMovieSlots(business)) return null;

  const availableStaff = getAvailableStaff(business);
  if (availableStaff < size.staffReq) return null;

  const now = Date.now();
  const devTimeMs = calculateDevTimeMs(sizeId, genreIds);

  const movie = {
    instanceId: `movie_${now}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    title: title || 'Untitled',
    genreIds: Array.isArray(genreIds) ? genreIds : [genreIds],
    topicId,
    sizeId,
    audienceId: audienceId || 'everyone',
    setId: setId || null,
    startedAt: now,
    completesAt: now + devTimeMs,
    releasedAt: null,
    rating: 0,
    income: 0,
  };

  return {
    ...business,
    movies: [...(business.movies || []), movie],
  };
}
```

### releaseMovie()

```js
function releaseMovie(business, instanceId) {
  const movies = (business.movies || []).map((m) => {
    if (m.instanceId === instanceId && !m.releasedAt) {
      const genreIds = Array.isArray(m.genreIds) ? m.genreIds : (m.genreId ? [m.genreId] : []);
      const fatigue = calculateFatigue(business, genreIds, m.topicId, m.sizeId, m.audienceId);
      const result = calculateMovieRating(business, genreIds, m.topicId, m.sizeId, m.audienceId, m.setId, fatigue.multiplier);
      const rating = typeof result === 'object' ? result.rating : result;
      const breakdown = typeof result === 'object' ? result.breakdown : null;
      return {
        ...m,
        releasedAt: Date.now(),
        rating,
        ratingBreakdown: breakdown,
        fatigueMultiplier: fatigue.multiplier,
        fatigueBreakdown: fatigue.breakdown,
      };
    }
    return m;
  });

  return { ...business, movies };
}
```

```js
}
```

```js
  return Math.max(0, movie.completesAt - Date.now());
}
```

### marketMovie()

```js
function marketMovie(business, movieInstanceId) {
  const movies = business.movies || [];
  const movie = movies.find((m) => m.instanceId === movieInstanceId && m.releasedAt);
  if (!movie) return null;

  const elapsedHr = (Date.now() - movie.releasedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
  const bonusHr = movie.bonusHours || 0;
  const fatigueMult = movie.fatigueMultiplier || 1.0;
  const totalLifespan = (MOVIE_LIFESPAN_HOURS + bonusHr) * fatigueMult;
  if (elapsedHr >= totalLifespan) return null;

  const marketingCount = movie.marketingCount || 0;
  const bonusHours = Math.max(1, MARKETING_BASE_HOURS - marketingCount);

  const size = getMovieSize(movie.sizeId);
  const sizeMult = size ? size.incomeMult : 1;
  const cost = Math.floor(MARKETING_BASE_COST * sizeMult * Math.pow(1.5, marketingCount));

  const updatedMovies = movies.map((m) => {
    if (m.instanceId === movieInstanceId) {
      return {
        ...m,
        bonusHours: (m.bonusHours || 0) + bonusHours,
        marketingCount: marketingCount + 1,
      };
    }
    return m;
  });

  return { business: { ...business, movies: updatedMovies }, bonusHours, cost };
}
```

### openSet()

```js
function openSet(business, setId) {
  const set = getMovieSet(setId);
  const released = (business.movies || []).filter((m) => m.releasedAt).length;
  const rated = (business.movies || []).filter((m) => m.releasedAt && m.rating).map((m) => m.rating);
  const averageRating = rated.length ? rated.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r, 0) / rated.length : 0;
  const staffCount = typeof business.staff === 'number' ? business.staff : 0;
  if (!set) return { error: 'invalid_set' };
  if (staffCount < (set.staffUnlock || 0) || released < (set.requiredReleased || 0) || averageRating < (set.requiredAverageRating || 0)) return { error: 'progress_locked' };
  if ((business.sets || []).some((owned) => owned.setId === setId)) return { error: 'already_exists' };
  return { ...business, sets: [...(business.sets || []), { setId, name: set.name, openedAt: Date.now() }], cost: set.openCost };
}
```

### generateStaffMember()

```js
function generateStaffMember() {
  const person = generatePerson();
  const roll = Math.random();
  let skill;
  if (roll < 0.05) skill = Math.floor(Math.random() * 2) + 1;
  else if (roll < 0.60) skill = Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) + 3;
  else if (roll < 0.85) skill = Math.floor(Math.random() * 2) + 8;
  else skill = 10;
  return {
    id: `staff_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
    name: person.name,
    age: person.age,
    gender: person.gender,
    skill,
    salary: STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY,
    hiredAt: Date.now(),
  };
}
```

### getStaffAvgSkill()

```js
function getStaffAvgSkill(business) {
  const members = business.staffMembers || [];
  if (members.length === 0) return 0;
  return members.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.skill, 0) / members.length;
}
```

### hireStaff()

```js
function hireStaff(business, count) {
  const currentHiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  if (currentHiring.length + count > MAX_HIRING_AT_ONCE) return null;

  const now = Date.now();
  const newHires = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const duration = STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS + Math.random() * (STAFF_HIRE_MAX_MS - STAFF_HIRE_MIN_MS);
    const member = generateStaffMember();
    newHires.push({
      id: `hire_${now}_${i}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5)}`,
      hiredAt: now,
      completesAt: now + duration,
      staffMember: member,
    });
  }

  return {
    ...business,
    hiringStaff: [...currentHiring, ...newHires],
  };
}
```

### completeHiredStaff()

```js
function completeHiredStaff(business) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const completed = hiring.filter((h) => h.completesAt <= now);
  const stillHiring = hiring.filter((h) => h.completesAt > now);

  if (completed.length === 0) return business;

  const newMembers = completed.map((h) => h.staffMember).filter(Boolean);
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (business.staff || 0) + completed.length,
    staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), ...newMembers],
    hiringStaff: stillHiring,
  };
}
```

### instantHireStaff()

```js
function instantHireStaff(business, hireId) {
  const hiring = business.hiringStaff || [];
  const hire = hiring.find((h) => h.id === hireId);
  if (!hire) return business;

  const newMember = hire.staffMember || generateStaffMember();
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: (business.staff || 0) + 1,
    staffMembers: [...(business.staffMembers || []), newMember],
    hiringStaff: hiring.filter((h) => h.id !== hireId),
  };
}
```

### fireStaff()

```js
function fireStaff(business, staffId) {
  return {
    ...business,
    staff: Math.max(0, (business.staff || 0) - 1),
    staffMembers: (business.staffMembers || []).filter((s) => s.id !== staffId),
  };
}
```

### raiseStaff()

```js
function raiseStaff(business, staffId) {
  const members = (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => {
    if (s.id === staffId) return { ...s, salary: Math.round((s.salary || STAFF_SALARY_HOURLY) * 1.15) };
    return s;
  });
  return { ...business, staffMembers: members };
}
```

### trainStaff()

```js
function trainStaff(business, staffId) {
  const members = (business.staffMembers || []).map((s) => {
    if (s.id === staffId) return { ...s, skill: Math.min(10, (s.skill || 5) + 1) };
    return s;
  });
  return { ...business, staffMembers: members };
}
```

```js
}
```

### migrateMovieStudio()

```js
function migrateMovieStudio(business) {
  let migrated = { ...business };

  // Old format had staff as object {scriptwriter, actor, crew} — convert to number
  if (typeof migrated.staff !== 'number') {
    if (migrated.staff && typeof migrated.staff === 'object') {
      migrated.staff = Object.values(migrated.staff).reduce((sum, v) => sum + (typeof v === 'number' ? v : 0), 0);
    } else {
      migrated.staff = 0;
    }
  }

  // Remove old fields that no longer exist
  delete migrated.staffTypes;
  delete migrated.scripts;
  delete migrated.scriptTypes;

  // Ensure new fields exist
  if (!migrated.movies) migrated.movies = [];
  if (!migrated.staffMembers) migrated.staffMembers = [];
  if (!migrated.hiringStaff) migrated.hiringStaff = [];
  if (!migrated.sets) migrated.sets = [];

  return migrated;
}
```

### processMovieStudio()

```js
function processMovieStudio(business, minutesElapsed) {
  const hoursFraction = minutesElapsed / 60;
  const levelMult = getLevelMultiplier(business);
  const suspended = isSuspended(business);
  let income = 0;

  // Migrate old state and generate staffMembers for existing saves
  let workingBusiness = migrateMovieStudio(business);
  if (workingBusiness.staff > 0 && workingBusiness.staffMembers.length === 0) {
    const members = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < workingBusiness.staff; i++) {
      members.push(generateStaffMember());
    }
    workingBusiness = { ...workingBusiness, staffMembers: members };
  }

  // Income from released movies
  const movies = (workingBusiness.movies || []).map((m) => {
    if (m.releasedAt) {
      const movieIncome = suspended ? 0 : getMovieHourlyIncome(m) * levelMult * hoursFraction;
      income += movieIncome;
      return { ...m, income: (m.income || 0) + movieIncome };
    }
    return m;
  });

  // Staff salary
  const salary = getStaffSalaryHourly(workingBusiness) * hoursFraction;
  income -= salary;

  // Remove expired movies
  const now = Date.now();
  const activeMovies = movies.filter((m) => {
    if (!m.releasedAt) return true;
    const elapsedHr = (now - m.releasedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    const fatigueMult = m.fatigueMultiplier || 1.0;
    const totalLifespan = (MOVIE_LIFESPAN_HOURS + (m.bonusHours || 0)) * fatigueMult;
    return elapsedHr < totalLifespan;
  });

  return {
    updatedBusiness: {
      ...workingBusiness,
      movies: activeMovies,
      allTimeEarnings: (workingBusiness.allTimeEarnings || 0) + income,
    },
    income,
  };
}
```

### formatTimeRemaining()

```js
function formatTimeRemaining(ms) {
  const totalSec = Math.ceil(ms / 1000);
  const hours = Math.floor(totalSec / 3600);
  const mins = Math.floor((totalSec % 3600) / 60);
  const secs = totalSec % 60;
  if (hours > 0) return `${hours}h ${mins}m`;
  if (mins > 0) return `${mins}m ${secs}s`;
  return `${secs}s`;
}
```

### generatePerson()

```js
function generatePerson() {
  const isMale = Math.random() < 0.5;
  const firstNames = isMale ? MALE_FIRST_NAMES : FEMALE_FIRST_NAMES;
  const first = firstNames[Math.floor(Math.random() * firstNames.length)];
  const last = LAST_NAMES[Math.floor(Math.random() * LAST_NAMES.length)];
  return {
    name: `${first} ${last}`,
    gender: isMale ? 'male' : 'female',
    age: Math.floor(Math.random() * 28) + 22,
  };
}
```

### hashString()

```js
function hashString(str) {
  let h = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    h ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
  }
  return (h >>> 0) / 4294967295;
}
```

### seededNoise()

```js
function seededNoise(seed) {
  const x = Math.sin(seed * 12.9898) * 43758.5453;
  return x - Math.floor(x);
}
```

### itemKey()

```js
function itemKey(categoryId, itemId) {
  return `${categoryId}:${itemId}`;
}
```

### getCategory()

```js
function getCategory(id) {
  return COLLECTION_CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.id === id);
}
```

### getItemsByCategory()

```js
function getItemsByCategory(categoryId) {
  return COLLECTION_ITEMS[categoryId] || [];
}
```

### getItem()

```js
function getItem(categoryId, itemId) {
  const items = COLLECTION_ITEMS[categoryId] || [];
  return items.find((i) => i.id === itemId);
}
```

### getItemPrice()

```js
function getItemPrice(categoryId, item) {
  if (!item) return 0;
  return Math.round(item.price * (CATEGORY_SCALE[categoryId] || 1));
}
```

### getItemPriceById()

```js
function getItemPriceById(categoryId, itemId) {
  return getItemPrice(categoryId, getItem(categoryId, itemId));
}
```

### getItemVolatility()

```js
function getItemVolatility(categoryId, itemId) {
  return 0.5 + hashString(itemKey(categoryId, itemId)) * 1.3;
}
```

### getOwnedItems()

```js
function getOwnedItems(progression) {
  return (progression && progression.ownedItems) || [];
}
```

### hasOwnedEstate()

```js
function hasOwnedEstate(progression) {
  return getOwnedItems(progression).some((i) => i.categoryId === 'estates');
}
```

### getOwnedItemsByCategory()

```js
function getOwnedItemsByCategory(progression, categoryId) {
  return getOwnedItems(progression).filter((i) => i.categoryId === categoryId);
}
```

### isOwned()

```js
function isOwned(progression, categoryId, itemId) {
  return getOwnedItems(progression).some(
    (i) => i.categoryId === categoryId && i.itemId === itemId
  );
}
```

### getOwnedCount()

```js
function getOwnedCount(progression) {
  return getOwnedItems(progression).length;
}
```

### getOwnedEntry()

```js
function getOwnedEntry(progression, categoryId, itemId) {
  return getOwnedItems(progression).find(
    (i) => i.categoryId === categoryId && i.itemId === itemId
  );
}
```

### getEstateSlots()

```js
function getEstateSlots(estateId) {
  const idx = COLLECTION_ITEMS.estates.findIndex((e) => e.id === estateId);
  return idx < 0 ? 0 : 2 + idx;
}
```

### getTotalDisplayCapacity()

```js
function getTotalDisplayCapacity(progression) {
  return getOwnedItemsByCategory(progression, 'estates').reduce(
    (sum, e) => sum + getEstateSlots(e.itemId),
    0
  );
}
```

### ESTATE_UPGRADES (6 items)

| id | name | icon | valueBonus | costPct |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| grounds | Landscaped Grounds | leaf | 0.04 | 0.05 |
| wing | West Wing Extension | business | 0.08 | 0.12 |
| spa | Infinity Pool & Spa | water | 0.05 | 0.06 |
| cellar | Climate Wine Cellar | wine | 0.05 | 0.055 |
| helipad | Private Helipad | airplane | 0.07 | 0.09 |
| systems | Smart Estate Systems | hardware-chip | 0.06 | 0.07 |

- **ESTATE_UPGRADE_MAX_LEVEL**: 5

### getEstateUpgradeLevel()

```js
function getEstateUpgradeLevel(progression, estateId, upgradeId) {
  const all = (progression && progression.estateUpgrades) || {};
  return (all[estateId] || {})[upgradeId] || 0;
}
```

### getEstateUpgradeCost()

```js
function getEstateUpgradeCost(estateId, upgradeId, level) {
  const upgrade = ESTATE_UPGRADES.find((u) => u.id === upgradeId);
  if (!upgrade) return 0;
  const base = getItemPriceById('estates', estateId) * upgrade.costPct;
  return Math.round(base * Math.pow(1.55, level));
}
```

### getEstateUpgradeMultiplier()

```js
function getEstateUpgradeMultiplier(progression, estateId) {
  return ESTATE_UPGRADES.reduce((mult, u) => {
    return mult + u.valueBonus * getEstateUpgradeLevel(progression, estateId, u.id);
  }, 1);
}
```

### getEstateUpgradeCount()

```js
function getEstateUpgradeCount(progression, estateId) {
  return ESTATE_UPGRADES.reduce(
    (sum, u) => sum + getEstateUpgradeLevel(progression, estateId, u.id),
    0
  );
}
```

### upgradeEstate()

```js
function upgradeEstate(progression, estateId, upgradeId) {
  const level = getEstateUpgradeLevel(progression, estateId, upgradeId);
  if (level >= ESTATE_UPGRADE_MAX_LEVEL) return { progression, error: 'max_level' };
  const cost = getEstateUpgradeCost(estateId, upgradeId, level);
  const all = { ...((progression && progression.estateUpgrades) || {}) };
  all[estateId] = { ...(all[estateId] || {}), [upgradeId]: level + 1 };
  return { progression: { ...progression, estateUpgrades: all }, cost };
}
```

- **VAULT_CAPACITY_PER_GUARD**: 6

- **VAULT_BASE_MULTIPLIER**: 2

### getEstateGuards()

```js
function getEstateGuards(progression, estateId) {
  const all = (progression && progression.estateSecurity) || {};
  return all[estateId] || 0;
}
```

### getTotalGuards()

```js
function getTotalGuards(progression) {
  return getOwnedItemsByCategory(progression, 'estates').reduce(
    (sum, e) => sum + getEstateGuards(progression, e.itemId),
    0
  );
}
```

### getGuardHireCost()

```js
function getGuardHireCost(progression, estateId) {
  const guards = getEstateGuards(progression, estateId);
  const base = getItemPriceById('estates', estateId) * 0.02;
  return Math.round(base * Math.pow(1.4, guards));
}
```

### hireGuard()

```js
function hireGuard(progression, estateId) {
  const guards = getEstateGuards(progression, estateId);
  const cost = getGuardHireCost(progression, estateId);
  const all = { ...((progression && progression.estateSecurity) || {}) };
  all[estateId] = guards + 1;
  return { progression: { ...progression, estateSecurity: all }, cost };
}
```

### getVaultCapacity()

```js
function getVaultCapacity(progression) {
  return getOwnedItemsByCategory(progression, 'estates').reduce((sum, e) => {
    const base = getEstateSlots(e.itemId) * VAULT_BASE_MULTIPLIER;
    return sum + base + getEstateGuards(progression, e.itemId) * VAULT_CAPACITY_PER_GUARD;
  }, 0);
}
```

### getAreaCapacity()

```js
function getAreaCapacity(progression, areaId) {
  if (areaId === 'vault') return getVaultCapacity(progression);
  return getTotalDisplayCapacity(progression);
}
```

### getDisplayed()

```js
function getDisplayed(progression, areaId) {
  const d = (progression && progression.displayed) || {};
  return d[areaId] || [];
}
```

### isStorable()

```js
function isStorable(categoryId) {
  return categoryId !== 'estates';
}
```

### getStorableItems()

```js
function getStorableItems(progression) {
  return getOwnedItems(progression).filter((i) => isStorable(i.categoryId));
}
```

### getAreaUsage()

```js
function getAreaUsage(progression, areaId) {
  // The Vault is implicit: it holds every piece not currently exhibited.
  if (areaId === 'vault') {
    const exhibited =
      getDisplayed(progression, 'gallery').length + getDisplayed(progression, 'showroom').length;
    return Math.max(0, getStorableItems(progression).length - exhibited);
  }
  return getDisplayed(progression, areaId).length;
}
```

### getItemArea()

```js
function getItemArea(progression, categoryId, itemId) {
  const key = itemKey(categoryId, itemId);
  for (const area of STORAGE_AREAS) {
    if (area.unlimited) continue;
    if (getDisplayed(progression, area.id).includes(key)) return area.id;
  }
  return 'vault';
}
```

### setItemArea()

```js
function setItemArea(progression, categoryId, itemId, areaId) {
  if (!isStorable(categoryId)) return { progression, error: 'not_storable' };
  const key = itemKey(categoryId, itemId);
  const displayed = { ...((progression && progression.displayed) || {}) };
  for (const area of STORAGE_AREAS) {
    displayed[area.id] = (displayed[area.id] || []).filter((k) => k !== key);
  }
  if (areaId !== 'vault') {
    if ((displayed[areaId] || []).length >= getAreaCapacity(progression, areaId)) {
      return { progression, error: 'no_capacity' };
    }
    displayed[areaId] = [...(displayed[areaId] || []), key];
  }
  return { progression: { ...progression, displayed } };
}
```

### getItemValue()

```js
function getItemValue(progression, entry, now = Date.now()) {
  const def = getItem(entry.categoryId, entry.itemId);
  if (!def) return 0;
  const base = getItemPrice(entry.categoryId, def);
  const heldMs = Math.max(0, now - (entry.purchasedAt || now));
  const years = heldMs / MS_PER_YEAR;

  const volatility = getItemVolatility(entry.categoryId, entry.itemId);
  const inGallery = getItemArea(progression, entry.categoryId, entry.itemId) === 'gallery';
  const rate =
    BASE_APPRECIATION_PER_YEAR * volatility * (inGallery ? 1 + GALLERY_APPRECIATION_BONUS : 1);

  const growth = 1 + rate * years;
  const phase = hashString(itemKey(entry.categoryId, entry.itemId)) * Math.PI * 2;
  const wobble = 1 + Math.sin(heldMs / (6 * 60 * 60 * 1000) + phase) * 0.06;

  // Improvements only apply to property.
  const improvements =
    entry.categoryId === 'estates'
      ? getEstateUpgradeMultiplier(progression, entry.itemId)
      : 1;

  return Math.round(base * growth * wobble * improvements);
}
```

### getEntryCost()

```js
function getEntryCost(entry) {
  if (entry.paid != null) return entry.paid;
  return getItemPriceById(entry.categoryId, entry.itemId);
}
```

### getItemGain()

```js
function getItemGain(progression, entry, now = Date.now()) {
  const cost = getEntryCost(entry);
  const value = getItemValue(progression, entry, now);
  const gain = value - cost;
  return { value, cost, gain, percent: cost > 0 ? (gain / cost) * 100 : 0 };
}
```

```js
function getTotalCollectionValue(progression, now = Date.now()) {
  return getOwnedItems(progression).reduce(
    (sum, entry) => sum + getItemValue(progression, entry, now),
    0
  );
}
```

### getTotalInvested()

```js
function getTotalInvested(progression) {

}
```

### getAppreciationPerHour()

```js
function getAppreciationPerHour(progression, now = Date.now()) {
  const hour = 60 * 60 * 1000;
  return Math.max(
    0,
    getTotalCollectionValue(progression, now + hour) - getTotalCollectionValue(progression, now)
  );
}
```

```js
function getEstateHourlyIncome(progression, now = Date.now()) {

    (sum, entry) => sum + getItemValue(progression, entry, now) * ESTATE_YIELD_PER_HOUR,
    0
  );
}
```

### getCompletedSets()

```js
function getCompletedSets(progression) {
  return COLLECTION_CATEGORIES.filter((cat) => {
    const total = getItemsByCategory(cat.id).length;
    return total > 0 && getOwnedItemsByCategory(progression, cat.id).length === total;
  }).map((c) => c.id);
}
```

### getSetBonusMultiplier()

```js
function getSetBonusMultiplier(progression) {
  return 1 + getCompletedSets(progression).length * SET_BONUS_PER_CATEGORY;
}
```

### getPrestige()

```js
function getPrestige(progression, now = Date.now()) {
  const valueScore = Math.floor(getTotalCollectionValue(progression, now) / 1e9);
  const setScore = getCompletedSets(progression).length * 250;
  const showroomValue = getDisplayed(progression, 'showroom').reduce((sum, key) => {
    const [categoryId, itemId] = key.split(':');
    const entry = getOwnedEntry(progression, categoryId, itemId);
    return sum + (entry ? getItemValue(progression, entry, now) * 0.25 : 0);
  }, 0);
  return valueScore + setScore + Math.floor(showroomValue / 1e9);
}
```

### buyItem()

```js
function buyItem(progression, categoryId, itemId, paid) {
  const item = getItem(categoryId, itemId);
  if (!item) return { progression, error: 'not_found' };
  if (isOwned(progression, categoryId, itemId)) {
    return { progression, error: 'already_owned' };
  }
  const cost = paid != null ? paid : getItemPrice(categoryId, item);
  return {
    progression: {
      ...progression,
      ownedItems: [
        ...getOwnedItems(progression),
        { categoryId, itemId, purchasedAt: Date.now(), paid: cost },
      ],
    },
    cost,
  };
}
```

### sellItem()

```js
function sellItem(progression, categoryId, itemId, now = Date.now()) {
  const entry = getOwnedEntry(progression, categoryId, itemId);
  if (!entry) return { progression, error: 'not_owned' };
  const { gross, proceeds } = getExchangeQuote(progression, entry, now);
  const key = itemKey(categoryId, itemId);
  const displayed = { ...((progression && progression.displayed) || {}) };
  for (const area of STORAGE_AREAS) {
    displayed[area.id] = (displayed[area.id] || []).filter((k) => k !== key);
  }
  return {
    progression: {
      ...progression,
      displayed,
      ownedItems: getOwnedItems(progression).filter(
        (i) => !(i.categoryId === categoryId && i.itemId === itemId)
      ),
    },
    proceeds,
    gross,
    commission: gross - proceeds,
  };
}
```

- **AUCTION_WINDOW_MS**: 600000

- **DEALER_WINDOW_MS**: 3600000

- **EXCHANGE_WINDOW_MS**: 60000

### unownedPool()

```js
function unownedPool(progression) {
  const pool = [];
  for (const cat of COLLECTION_CATEGORIES) {
    for (const item of getItemsByCategory(cat.id)) {
      if (!isOwned(progression, cat.id, item.id)) {
        pool.push({ categoryId: cat.id, itemId: item.id, name: item.name });
      }
    }
  }
  return pool;
}
```

### pickDeterministic()

```js
function pickDeterministic(pool, count, seed) {
  const picks = [];
  const used = new Set();
  let cursor = seed;
  let guard = 0;
  while (picks.length < count && used.size < pool.length && guard < 500) {
    guard++;
    cursor = seededNoise(cursor + picks.length * 7.31 + guard * 0.013);
    const idx = Math.floor(cursor * pool.length) % pool.length;
    if (!used.has(idx)) {
      used.add(idx);
      picks.push(pool[idx]);
    }
  }
  return picks;
}
```

### getAuctionWindow()

```js
function getAuctionWindow(now = Date.now()) {
  return Math.floor(now / AUCTION_WINDOW_MS);
}
```

### getAuctionEndsAt()

```js
function getAuctionEndsAt(now = Date.now()) {
  return (getAuctionWindow(now) + 1) * AUCTION_WINDOW_MS;
}
```

### getAuctionLots()

```js
function getAuctionLots(progression, now = Date.now()) {
  const window = getAuctionWindow(now);
  const pool = unownedPool(progression);
  if (pool.length === 0) return [];
  return pickDeterministic(pool, Math.min(3, pool.length), window * 1.7).map((pick, i) => {
    const market = getItemPriceById(pick.categoryId, pick.itemId);
    const openingBid = Math.round(market * (0.45 + seededNoise(window + i * 3.3) * 0.2));
    // How far rivals will chase it, as a fraction of market value.
    const rivalCeiling = Math.round(market * (0.8 + seededNoise(window + i * 5.9) * 0.45));
    return {
      ...pick,
      lotId: `${window}-${pick.categoryId}-${pick.itemId}`,
      market,
      openingBid,
      rivalCeiling,
      rivalCount: 2 + Math.floor(seededNoise(window + i * 8.1) * 3),
    };
  });
}
```

### getAuctionBids()

```js
function getAuctionBids(progression) {
  return (progression && progression.auctionBids) || {};
}
```

### getAuctionBid()

```js
function getAuctionBid(progression, lotId) {
  return getAuctionBids(progression)[lotId] || null;
}
```

### placeAuctionBid()

```js
function placeAuctionBid(progression, lot, amount, leader, leaderName) {
  const bids = { ...getAuctionBids(progression) };
  const existing = bids[lot.lotId];
  bids[lot.lotId] = {
    lotId: lot.lotId,
    window: getAuctionWindow(),
    categoryId: lot.categoryId,
    itemId: lot.itemId,
    name: lot.name,
    market: lot.market,
    rivalCeiling: lot.rivalCeiling,
    currentBid: amount,
    leader,
    leaderName,
    bidCount: (existing ? existing.bidCount : 0) + 1,
  };
  return { ...progression, auctionBids: bids };
}
```

### resolveAuctions()

```js
function resolveAuctions(progression, now = Date.now()) {
  const currentWindow = getAuctionWindow(now);
  const bids = getAuctionBids(progression);
  const remaining = {};
  const won = [];
  const lost = [];
  let next = progression;
  let spent = 0;

  for (const lotId of Object.keys(bids)) {
    const record = bids[lotId];
    if (record.window >= currentWindow) {
      remaining[lotId] = record;
      continue;
    }
    const playerWon =
      record.leader === 'player' && record.currentBid >= record.rivalCeiling;
    if (playerWon && !isOwned(next, record.categoryId, record.itemId)) {
      const result = buyItem(next, record.categoryId, record.itemId, record.currentBid);
      if (!result.error) {
        next = result.progression;
        spent += record.currentBid;
        won.push({ name: record.name, price: record.currentBid });
      }
    } else if (record.leader === 'player') {
      lost.push({ name: record.name, price: record.currentBid });
    }
  }

  return { progression: { ...next, auctionBids: remaining }, won, lost, spent };
}
```

### getDealerWindow()

```js
}
```

```js

}
```

### getDealerStock()

```js
function getDealerStock(progression, now = Date.now()) {
  const window = getDealerWindow(now);
  const pool = unownedPool(progression);
  if (pool.length === 0) return [];
  return pickDeterministic(pool, Math.min(4, pool.length), window * 2.9).map((pick, i) => {
    const market = getItemPriceById(pick.categoryId, pick.itemId);
    const askMult = 1.05 + seededNoise(window + i * 4.2) * 0.3;
    const floorMult = 0.78 + seededNoise(window + i * 6.6) * 0.17;
    return {
      ...pick,
      dealId: `${window}-${pick.categoryId}-${pick.itemId}`,
      market,
      askPrice: Math.round(market * askMult),
      floorPrice: Math.round(market * floorMult),
      patience: 3, // lowball offers tolerated before they walk away
    };
  });
}
```

### getExchangeMultiplier()

```js
function getExchangeMultiplier(categoryId, itemId, now = Date.now()) {
  const window = getExchangeWindow(now);
  const base = hashString(itemKey(categoryId, itemId));
  return 0.9 + seededNoise(window * 1.13 + base * 100) * 0.25;
}
```

### getExchangeQuote()

```js
function getExchangeQuote(progression, entry, now = Date.now()) {
  const value = getItemValue(progression, entry, now);
  const multiplier = getExchangeMultiplier(entry.categoryId, entry.itemId, now);
  const gross = Math.round(value * multiplier);
  return { gross, proceeds: Math.round(gross * (1 - EXCHANGE_COMMISSION)), multiplier };
}
```

```js

}
```

### getPendingAcquisition()

```js

    (p) => p.categoryId === categoryId && p.itemId === itemId
  );
}
```

### startAcquisition()

```js
function startAcquisition(progression, categoryId, itemId) {
  const item = getItem(categoryId, itemId);
  if (!item) return { progression, error: 'not_found' };
  if (isOwned(progression, categoryId, itemId)) return { progression, error: 'already_owned' };
  if (getPendingAcquisition(progression, categoryId, itemId)) {
    return { progression, error: 'already_pending' };
  }
  const cost = getRetailPrice(categoryId, itemId);
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  const pending = {
    id: `${categoryId}-${itemId}-${startedAt}`,
    categoryId,
    itemId,
    name: item.name,
    paid: cost,
    startedAt,
    completesAt: startedAt + randomDeliveryMs(),
  };
  return {
    progression: {
      ...progression,
      pendingAcquisitions: [...getPendingAcquisitions(progression), pending],
    },
    cost,
  };
}
```

### rushAcquisition()

```js
function rushAcquisition(progression, pendingId) {
  return {
    progression: {
      ...progression,
      pendingAcquisitions: getPendingAcquisitions(progression).map((p) =>
        p.id === pendingId ? { ...p, completesAt: Date.now() } : p
      ),
    },
  };
}
```

### processAcquisitions()

```js
function processAcquisitions(progression, now = Date.now()) {
  const pending = getPendingAcquisitions(progression);
  if (pending.length === 0) return { progression, completed: [] };

  const completed = [];
  let next = progression;
  const remaining = [];

  for (const p of pending) {
    if (p.completesAt > now) {
      remaining.push(p);
      continue;
    }
    const result = buyItem(next, p.categoryId, p.itemId, p.paid);
    if (!result.error) {
      next = result.progression;
      completed.push(p);
    }
  }

  if (completed.length === 0) return { progression, completed: [] };
  return { progression: { ...next, pendingAcquisitions: remaining }, completed };
}
```

### startEstateUpgrade()

```js
function startEstateUpgrade(progression, estateId, upgradeId) {
  const level = getEstateUpgradeLevel(progression, estateId, upgradeId);
  if (level >= ESTATE_UPGRADE_MAX_LEVEL) return { progression, error: 'max_level' };
  if (getEstateWork(progression, estateId, 'upgrade', upgradeId)) {
    return { progression, error: 'already_running' };
  }
  const cost = getEstateUpgradeCost(estateId, upgradeId, level);
  const upgrade = ESTATE_UPGRADES.find((u) => u.id === upgradeId);
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  const work = {
    id: `${estateId}-${upgradeId}-${startedAt}`,
    estateId,
    kind: 'upgrade',
    upgradeId,
    label: upgrade ? upgrade.name : 'Improvement',
    startedAt,
    completesAt: startedAt + UPGRADE_BUILD_MS,
  };
  return {
    progression: { ...progression, estateWorks: [...getEstateWorks(progression), work] },
    cost,
  };
}
```

```js
function startGuardHire(progression, estateId) {
  if (getEstateWork(progression, estateId, 'guard')) {
    return { progression, error: 'already_running' };
  }
  const cost = getGuardHireCost(progression, estateId);
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  const work = {
    id: `${estateId}-guard-${startedAt}`,
    estateId,
    kind: 'guard',

    startedAt,
    completesAt: startedAt + GUARD_ONBOARD_MS,
  };

}
```

```js
        w.id === workId ? { ...w, completesAt: Date.now() } : w
```

### processEstateWorks()

```js
function processEstateWorks(progression, now = Date.now()) {
  const works = getEstateWorks(progression);
  if (works.length === 0) return { progression, completed: [] };

  const completed = [];
  const remaining = [];
  const upgrades = { ...((progression && progression.estateUpgrades) || {}) };
  const security = { ...((progression && progression.estateSecurity) || {}) };

  for (const w of works) {
    if (w.completesAt > now) {
      remaining.push(w);
      continue;
    }
    if (w.kind === 'upgrade') {
      const current = (upgrades[w.estateId] || {})[w.upgradeId] || 0;
      upgrades[w.estateId] = {
        ...(upgrades[w.estateId] || {}),
        [w.upgradeId]: Math.min(ESTATE_UPGRADE_MAX_LEVEL, current + 1),
      };
    } else if (w.kind === 'guard') {
      security[w.estateId] = (security[w.estateId] || 0) + 1;
    }
    completed.push(w);
  }

  if (completed.length === 0) return { progression, completed: [] };
  return {
    progression: {
      ...progression,
      estateUpgrades: upgrades,
      estateSecurity: security,
      estateWorks: remaining,
    },
    completed,
  };
}
```

### RIVAL_NAMES

- Blackwood Trust, Anonymous Bidder, Halloran Group, Vance Holdings, The Consortium, Marchetti Family, Paddle 12, Northgate Capital

### tickAuctions()

```js
function tickAuctions(progression, now = Date.now()) {
  const bids = getAuctionBids(progression);
  const lotIds = Object.keys(bids);
  if (lotIds.length === 0) return { progression, events: [] };

  const currentWindow = getAuctionWindow(now);
  const events = [];
  const next = { ...bids };
  let changed = false;

  for (const lotId of lotIds) {
    const record = next[lotId];
    if (record.window !== currentWindow) continue;
    if (record.leader !== 'player') continue;
    if (record.currentBid >= record.rivalCeiling) continue;
    if (Math.random() > 0.5) continue;

    const raise = Math.round(record.currentBid * (1.03 + Math.random() * 0.06));
    const bid = Math.min(raise, record.rivalCeiling);
    if (bid <= record.currentBid) continue;

    const by = RIVAL_NAMES[Math.floor(Math.random() * RIVAL_NAMES.length)];
    next[lotId] = { ...record, currentBid: bid, leader: 'room', leaderName: by, bidCount: record.bidCount + 1 };
    changed = true;
    events.push({ type: 'outbid', name: record.name, bid, by, lotId });
  }

  if (!changed) return { progression, events: [] };
  return { progression: { ...progression, auctionBids: next }, events };
}
```

```js
    estateSecurity: {},
    estateWorks: [],
    auctionBids: {},
    pendingAcquisitions: [],
  };
}
```
